// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Smart Finance — Apps Script Backend // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Deploy: Publish > Deploy as Web App // Execute as: Me | Access: Anyone // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── Config ────────────────────────────────────────────────── const CONFIG = { SPREADSHEET_ID: '1wVBPXR7YCwDCsSXsJKi9vh-jbFz8zQvPH2isyPlxbKU', // ← ใส่ ID ของ Google Sheets ที่สร้างไว้ TIMEZONE: 'Asia/Bangkok', DATE_FORMAT: 'dd/MM/yyyy', DATETIME_FORMAT: 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm', MAX_ROWS_PER_QUERY: 500, CACHE_TTL: 300, // 5 นาที }; // ชื่อ Sheet คงที่ const SHEETS = { USERS: 'users', CONFIG: 'config', CUSTOMERS: 'customers', SUPPLIERS: 'suppliers', CREDIT_RULES: 'credit_rules', AR_LEDGER: 'ar_ledger', AP_LEDGER: 'ap_ledger', ALERTS_LOG: 'alerts_log', MATCH_LOG: 'match_log', REPORT_ARCHIVE: 'report_archive', }; // ชื่อ Sheet แบบ dynamic // ── Year-based (refactored): 1 sheet ต่อปี, ไม่ระเบิดเป็นพันๆ sheet ── function getYearFromKey(date) { if (!date) return String(new Date().getFullYear()); var str = String(date).replace(/-/g, '').replace(/\//g, ''); // ถ้าเป็น YYYYMMDD (8 หลัก) หรือ YYYYMM (6 หลัก) → เอา 4 หลักแรก if (/^\d{4}/.test(str)) { var y = Number(str.substring(0, 4)); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; // พ.ศ. → ค.ศ. return String(y); } try { var d = (date instanceof Date) ? date : parseDateKey(date); return Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyy'); } catch (e) { return String(new Date().getFullYear()); } } function getDailySheet(date) { return 'bills_' + getYearFromKey(date); } function getStmtSheet(date) { return 'stmts_' + getYearFromKey(date); } function getMatchSheet(date) { return 'match_' + getYearFromKey(date); } function getMonthlySheet(date) { return 'monthly_' + Utilities.formatDate(parseDateKey(date), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyyMM'); } function getYearlySheet(date) { return 'yearly_' + Utilities.formatDate(parseDateKey(date), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyy'); } // ── Utilities ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function getSpreadsheet() { return SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); } function getSheet(name) { const ss = getSpreadsheet(); let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name); return sheet; } function getOrCreateSheet(name, headers) { const ss = getSpreadsheet(); let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(name); if (headers && headers.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length) .setFontWeight('bold') .setBackground('#1a1d27') .setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } } else if (headers && headers.length > 0) { // ── Auto schema migration: add missing columns at the end ── var existingHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1)).getValues()[0]; var existingSet = {}; existingHeaders.forEach(function(h) { if (h) existingSet[String(h).trim()] = true; }); var missing = headers.filter(function(h) { return !existingSet[h]; }); if (missing.length > 0) { var startCol = existingHeaders.length + 1; // Ensure existing row has all old headers first (pad if needed) if (existingHeaders.filter(function(h){return h;}).length === 0) { // Empty sheet — set headers from scratch sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]); } else { // Append missing headers sheet.getRange(1, startCol, 1, missing.length).setValues([missing]); } sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()) .setFontWeight('bold') .setBackground('#1a1d27') .setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); if (sheet.getFrozenRows() === 0) sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } } return sheet; } function formatDateKey(date) { if (!date) return todayKey(); const str = String(date).replace(/-/g, '').replace(/\//g, ''); // ถ้าเป็น YYYYMMDD อยู่แล้ว ให้ return ตรงๆ if (/^\d{8}$/.test(str)) return str; // ถ้าเป็น Date object หรือ string อื่น try { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(date), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyyMMdd'); } catch (e) { return str; } } // แปลง YYYYMMDD หรือ dd/mm/yyyy → Date object function parseDateKey(dateKey) { if (!dateKey) return new Date(); const str = String(dateKey); // YYYYMMDD if (/^\d{8}$/.test(str)) { return new Date(Number(str.substring(0, 4)), Number(str.substring(4, 6)) - 1, Number(str.substring(6, 8))); } // dd/mm/yyyy const parts = str.split('/'); if (parts.length === 3 && parts[2].length === 4) { return new Date(Number(parts[2]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[0])); } return new Date(str); } function formatDateDisplay(date) { if (!date) return ''; const d = (typeof date === 'string') ? parseDateKey(date) : new Date(date); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(date); return Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, CONFIG.DATE_FORMAT); } function formatDateTimeDisplay(date) { if (!date) return ''; const d = (typeof date === 'string') ? parseDateKey(date) : new Date(date); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(date); return Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, CONFIG.DATETIME_FORMAT); } function nowDisplay() { return formatDateTimeDisplay(new Date()); } function todayKey() { return formatDateKey(new Date()); } // แปลง Sheet data เป็น Array of Objects function sheetToObjects(sheet) { if (!sheet) return []; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return []; const headers = data[0]; return data.slice(1).map(row => { const obj = {}; headers.forEach((h, i) => { let val = row[i]; // ป้องกันเลข 0 หน้าหาย — ถ้า column เป็น username ให้เก็บเป็น string if (typeof val === 'number' && (h === 'username' || h === 'user_id' || h.endsWith('_id'))) { val = String(val).padStart(3, '0'); // ปรับตามความยาวที่ต้องการ } // แปลง Date object เป็น string dd/mm/yyyy (+ แปลง พ.ศ.) if (val instanceof Date) { val = convertDate(val); } else if (typeof val === 'string' && /\d{4}/.test(val)) { // แปลง พ.ศ. เฉพาะ field ที่เป็นวันที่เท่านั้น (ไม่แตะ hash, ref, description) var dateFields = ['date', 'bill_date', 'due_date', 'pay_date', 'next_pay_date', 'created_at', 'last_login', 'last_checked', 'action_at', 'matched_at', 'generated_at', 'sent_at', 'Bill Date', 'Pay Date', 'Bank - Due Date']; var hLower = String(h).toLowerCase(); var isDateField = dateFields.some(function(df) { return hLower === df.toLowerCase() || hLower.includes('date') || hLower.includes('_at'); }); if (isDateField) { val = val.replace(/(\d{4})/g, function(m) { var y = Number(m); return y > 2400 ? String(y - 543) : m; }); } } obj[h] = val; }); return obj; }).filter(obj => { // กรองแถวว่าง return Object.values(obj).some(v => v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined); }); } // เขียน Object กลับลง Sheet function appendRow(sheetName, obj, headers) { const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, headers); const sheetHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; const row = sheetHeaders.map(h => obj[h] !== undefined ? obj[h] : ''); sheet.appendRow(row); return obj; } // สร้าง ID อัตโนมัติ function generateId(prefix) { return prefix + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyyMMddHHmmss') + Math.floor(Math.random() * 100); } // Hash password (SHA-256) function hashPassword(password) { const raw = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, password); return raw.map(b => ('0' + ((b + 256) % 256).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join(''); } // JSON Response helpers (รองรับ JSONP callback) function jsonOk(data) { var json = JSON.stringify({ success: true, data: data }); return ContentService .createTextOutput(json) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function jsonError(message, code) { var json = JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: message, code: code || 400 }); return ContentService .createTextOutput(json) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } // ── CORS + Routing ────────────────────────────────────────── function doGet(e) { return handleRequest(e); } function doPost(e) { return handleRequest(e); } function handleRequest(e) { try { // Safety check if (!e) e = {}; const params = e.parameter || {}; let body = {}; // Parse POST body — รองรับ 3 รูปแบบ: // 1) application/json (text/plain) ใน e.postData.contents // 2) application/x-www-form-urlencoded → GAS auto parse เข้า e.parameter // - ถ้า frontend ส่ง payload=JSON string → parse เพิ่ม // 3) GET query string → e.parameter if (e.postData && e.postData.contents) { try { body = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents); } catch (err) { body = {}; } } // รองรับ URLSearchParams POST (เลี่ยง CORS ใน Edge/Brave/Safari) if (params.payload && typeof params.payload === 'string') { try { const parsed = JSON.parse(params.payload); body = Object.assign({}, body, parsed); } catch (err) { Logger.log('Failed to parse payload: ' + err.message); } } // action มาได้ทั้งจาก URL parameter หรือ POST body const action = params.action || body.action || ''; // Merge params (body override params) const req = Object.assign({}, params, body); delete req.payload; // internal field // Auto-parse stringified JSON values — fix GET requests ที่ array/object ถูก stringify ใน URL // เช่น saveSpTemps({records: [...]}) ผ่าน GET จะได้ req.records = '[{"no":...}]' (string) // → Array.isArray() fail → error "records must be array" // Defensive parse ที่นี่ที่เดียวครอบคลุมทุก endpoint Object.keys(req).forEach(function(k) { var v = req[k]; if (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0) { var c = v.charAt(0); if (c === '[' || c === '{') { try { req[k] = JSON.parse(v); } catch (_) { /* keep original string */ } } } }); Logger.log('handleRequest: action=' + action + ' method=' + (e.postData ? 'POST' : 'GET')); // Route switch (action) { // AUDIT — for sheet/schema inspection case 'listSheets': return listSheetsHandler(req); case 'getSheetInfo': return getSheetInfoHandler(req); case 'deleteSheet': return deleteSheetHandler(req); // AUTH case 'login': return login(req); // USERS case 'getUsers': return getUsers(req); case 'createUser': return createUser(req); case 'updateUser': return updateUser(req); case 'deleteUser': return deleteUser(req); // CUSTOMERS case 'getCustomers': return getCustomers(req); case 'createCustomer': return createCustomer(req); case 'updateCustomer': return updateCustomer(req); // CREDIT case 'getCreditRules': return getCreditRules(req); case 'setCreditRule': return setCreditRule(req); case 'breakCredit': return breakCredit(req); case 'reschedulePayment': return reschedulePayment(req); // DAILY SALES case 'getDailySales': return getDailySales(req); case 'saveDailySales': return saveDailySales(req); case 'importDailySales': return importDailySales(req); // DAILY META case 'getDailyMeta': return getDailyMeta(req); case 'saveDailyMeta': return jsonOk(saveDailyMeta(req)); // STATEMENTS case 'getStatements': return getStatements(req); case 'saveStatements': return saveStatements(req); // MATCHING case 'autoMatch': return autoMatch(req); case 'manualMatch': return manualMatch(req); case 'unmatch': return unmatchEntry(req); case 'rematch': return rematch(req); case 'getMatchLog': return getMatchLog(req); // AR LEDGER case 'getArLedger': return getArLedger(req); case 'updateArLedger': return jsonOk(updateArLedger(req)); // REPORTS case 'generateMonthly': return generateMonthly(req); case 'generateYearly': return generateYearly(req); case 'getMonthlySummary': return getMonthlySummary(req); case 'getYearlySummary': return getYearlySummary(req); // ALERTS case 'getAlerts': return getAlerts(req); case 'checkAlerts': return checkAlerts(req); // PAYMENTS (customer receives) case 'recordPayment': return recordPayment(req); case 'getPaymentHistory': return getPaymentHistory(req); // ═══ BILLING NOTES (ใบวางบิล + ติดตามหนี้) — 4/5/26 ═══ case 'getBillingNotes': return getBillingNotes(req); case 'getNextBillingNo': return getNextBillingNo(req); case 'createBillingNote': return createBillingNote(req); case 'updateBillingNote': return updateBillingNote(req); case 'deleteBillingNote': return deleteBillingNote(req); case 'cancelBillingNote': return cancelBillingNote(req); case 'recordBillingPayment': return recordBillingPayment(req); case 'getBillingPayments': return getBillingPayments(req); case 'getBillingDebtAging': return getBillingDebtAging(req); case 'getBillingCustomerStmt': return getBillingCustomerStmt(req); // SP (Smart Payments) case 'savePayments': return savePayments(req); case 'getPayments': return getPayments(req); // CASHFLOW case 'saveCashflow': return saveCashflow(req); case 'getCashflow': return getCashflow(req); // SUPPLIERS case 'saveSuppliers': return saveSuppliers(req); case 'getSuppliers': return getSuppliers(req); case 'saveSupplierPayments': return saveSupplierPayments(req); case 'getSupplierPayments': return getSupplierPayments(req); // PP (Pay Persons reports) case 'savePPReport': return savePPReport(req); case 'getPPReport': return getPPReport(req); case 'exportPPReportForTax': return exportPPReportForTax(req); case 'exportSPReportForTax': return exportSPReportForTax(req); // TRANSFER LOG case 'saveTransferLog': return saveTransferLog(req); case 'getTransferLog': return getTransferLog(req); // BILL STATUS case 'updateBillStatus': return updateBillStatus(req); // ARCHIVE + REPORTS case 'getMatchedArchive': return getMatchedArchive(req); case 'generateReport': return generateReport(req); case 'getReportList': return getReportList(req); // DASHBOARD / CONFIG case 'getDashboard': return getDashboard(req); case 'getConfig': return getConfigData(req); // SHEETS HEALTH case 'listSheets': return listSheets(req); // ═══ UUID-based sync (v1.0) ═══ case 'prepareSheet': return prepareSheet(req); case 'upsertBills': return upsertBills(req); case 'upsertStatements': return upsertStatements(req); case 'upsertMeta': return upsertMeta(req); case 'clearSheetData': return clearSheetData(req); case 'getBillsWithUuid': return getBillsWithUuid(req); case 'getStatementsWithUuid': return getStatementsWithUuid(req); case 'getMetaWithUuid': return getMetaWithUuid(req); case 'verifySheetCount': return verifySheetCount(req); case 'ensureBillsColumns': return ensureBillsColumns(req); case 'alignBillsSchema': return alignBillsSchema(req); case 'reorderBillsSchema': return reorderBillsSchema(req); case 'finalizeBillsSchema': return finalizeBillsSchema(req); case 'finalizeStmtsSchema': return finalizeStmtsSchema(req); case 'checkStmtsSchemaDrift': return checkStmtsSchemaDrift(req); case 'dedupStmtsByUuid': return dedupStmtsByUuid(req); case 'checkBillsSchemaDrift': return checkBillsSchemaDrift(req); case 'saveSpTemps': return saveSpTemps(req); case 'getSpTemps': return getSpTemps(req); case 'deleteSpTemp': return deleteSpTemp(req); case 'migrateSpTempSchema': return migrateSpTempSchema(req); // 🆕 add sp_temp_uuid + bills_json case 'fixMetaDates': return fixMetaDates(req); // 🆕 normalize Buddhist/Date → ISO case 'fixMatchStatus': return fixMatchStatus(req); // 🆕 rename 'matched'→'paid', 'unmatched'→'unpaid' case 'dropBillsColumns': return dropBillsColumns(req); // 🆕 ลบ dead/unused columns ใน bills_YYYY case 'deleteMetaByDateRange': return deleteMetaByDateRange(req); // 🆕 ลบ rows ตามช่วงวันที่ // ═══ MONTHLY MODULE (Phase 1A + 1B) ═══ case 'prepareMonthlySheets': return prepareMonthlySheets(req); case 'getMonthlyFront': return getMonthlyFront(req); case 'upsertMonthlyFront': return upsertMonthlyFront(req); case 'deleteMonthlyFrontByPeriod': return deleteMonthlyFrontByPeriod(req); case 'getMonthlyInvoice': return getMonthlyInvoice(req); case 'upsertMonthlyInvoice': return upsertMonthlyInvoice(req); case 'deleteMonthlyInvoiceByPeriod': return deleteMonthlyInvoiceByPeriod(req); case 'checkMonthlyPeriod': return checkMonthlyPeriod(req); // ═══ FRONT PC (ยอดขาย PC — line-item tracking with submission workflow) ═══ case 'preparePcSheet': return preparePcSheet(req); case 'upsertPcBatch': return upsertPcBatch(req); case 'getPcLineitems': return getPcLineitems(req); case 'updatePcStatus': return updatePcStatus(req); case 'deletePcByBatch': return deletePcByBatch(req); // PC Submissions (ใบส่ง PC) case 'createOrAddPcSubmission': return createOrAddPcSubmission(req); case 'removeFromPcSubmission': return removeFromPcSubmission(req); case 'listPcSubmissions': return listPcSubmissions(req); case 'getPcSubmissionDetail': return getPcSubmissionDetail(req); case 'deletePcSubmission': return deletePcSubmission(req); // PC Split (manual user-driven splitting) case 'splitPcLineitem': return splitPcLineitem(req); case 'unsplitPcLineitems': return unsplitPcLineitems(req); case 'countPcSplitsByPeriod': return countPcSplitsByPeriod(req); case 'checkPcPeriod': return checkPcPeriod(req); // MAINTENANCE case 'migrateSchemaV2': return jsonOk({ result: migrateSchemaV2() }); // DEBUG case 'ping': return jsonOk({ pong: true, time: nowDisplay(), action_received: action, params_keys: Object.keys(req) }); default: // ⭐ V2 routes (bank split + date-range queries + audit) — Phase D, 26/4/26 var _v2 = (typeof routeV2 === 'function') ? routeV2(action, req) : null; if (_v2) return _v2; return jsonError('Unknown action: ' + action + ' | params: ' + JSON.stringify(Object.keys(req)), 404); } } catch (err) { return jsonError(err.message, 500); } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Auth & User Management // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // AUDIT HANDLERS (read-only sheet inspection) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function listSheetsHandler(req) { // List all sheets in spreadsheet with row count + last update const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); const sheets = ss.getSheets(); const result = sheets.map(sh => ({ name: sh.getName(), rows: sh.getLastRow(), cols: sh.getLastColumn(), is_hidden: sh.isSheetHidden() })); return jsonOk(result); } function getSheetInfoHandler(req) { // Return sheet info: headers + row count const sheetName = req.sheet || ''; if (!sheetName) return jsonError('Missing sheet name'); const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sh) return jsonOk({ exists: false }); const lastRow = sh.getLastRow(); const lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); let headers = []; if (lastRow >= 1 && lastCol >= 1) { headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(String); } return jsonOk({ exists: true, name: sheetName, rows: lastRow, cols: lastCol, data_rows: Math.max(0, lastRow - 1), headers: headers, is_hidden: sh.isSheetHidden() }); } function deleteSheetHandler(req) { // Safety-guarded sheet deletion // Only allow: // - Names matching *_backup_* pattern (auto-generated backups) // - Names ending with _backup_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS // Reject: // - Active business sheets (bills_, stmts_, customers, suppliers, etc.) // - Production tables const sheetName = req.sheet || ''; if (!sheetName) return jsonError('Missing sheet name'); // Whitelist patterns (only auto-backup sheets) var allowedPatterns = [ /_backup_\d{8}_\d{6}$/, // _backup_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS /_backup_\d{14}$/, // _backup_YYYYMMDDHHMMSS ]; var isAllowed = allowedPatterns.some(function(p) { return p.test(sheetName); }); if (!isAllowed) { return jsonError('Refused: only auto-backup sheets matching *_backup_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS can be deleted. Got: ' + sheetName); } const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sh) return jsonOk({ deleted: false, reason: 'Sheet not found' }); const rowsBefore = sh.getLastRow(); ss.deleteSheet(sh); return jsonOk({ deleted: true, name: sheetName, rows_deleted: rowsBefore }); } const USER_HEADERS = ['user_id', 'username', 'password_hash', 'display_name', 'role', 'permissions', 'status', 'last_login', 'created_at']; function login(req) { const { username, password } = req; if (!username || !password) return jsonError('กรุณากรอก username และ password'); const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Sheet users'); const users = sheetToObjects(sheet); // เปรียบเทียบ username แบบ string (ป้องกัน 0 หน้าหาย) const user = users.find(u => String(u.username).replace(/^'/, '') === String(username)); if (!user) return jsonError('ไม่พบผู้ใช้นี้', 401); const inputHash = hashPassword(password); if (user.password_hash !== inputHash) return jsonError('รหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง', 401); if (user.status !== 'active') return jsonError('บัญชีถูกระงับ', 403); // อัปเดต last_login updateUserField(user.user_id, 'last_login', nowDisplay()); return jsonOk({ user_id: user.user_id, username: String(user.username).replace(/^'/, ''), display_name: user.display_name, role: user.role, permissions: String(user.permissions).split(',').map(p => p.trim()), }); } function getUsers(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); const users = sheetToObjects(sheet).map(u => ({ user_id: u.user_id, username: String(u.username).replace(/^'/, ''), display_name: u.display_name, role: u.role, permissions: String(u.permissions).split(',').map(p => p.trim()), status: u.status, last_login: u.last_login, created_at: u.created_at, })); return jsonOk(users); } function createUser(req) { const { username, password, display_name, role, permissions } = req; if (!username || !password || !display_name) return jsonError('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ'); const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(SHEETS.USERS, USER_HEADERS); const users = sheetToObjects(sheet); // ตรวจ username ซ้ำ if (users.find(u => String(u.username).replace(/^'/, '') === String(username))) { return jsonError('Username นี้มีอยู่แล้ว'); } const newUser = { user_id: generateId('U'), username: "'" + String(username), // บังคับ plain text ด้วย ' password_hash: hashPassword(password), display_name: display_name, role: role || 'staff', permissions: Array.isArray(permissions) ? permissions.join(',') : (permissions || 'read'), status: 'active', last_login: '', created_at: nowDisplay(), }; appendRow(SHEETS.USERS, newUser, USER_HEADERS); return jsonOk({ user_id: newUser.user_id, username: username }); } function updateUser(req) { const { user_id, display_name, role, permissions, status } = req; if (!user_id) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ user_id'); const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Sheet users'); const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('user_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === user_id) { if (display_name !== undefined) sheet.getRange(r + 1, headers.indexOf('display_name') + 1).setValue(display_name); if (role !== undefined) sheet.getRange(r + 1, headers.indexOf('role') + 1).setValue(role); if (permissions !== undefined) { const perm = Array.isArray(permissions) ? permissions.join(',') : permissions; sheet.getRange(r + 1, headers.indexOf('permissions') + 1).setValue(perm); } if (status !== undefined) sheet.getRange(r + 1, headers.indexOf('status') + 1).setValue(status); return jsonOk({ user_id: user_id, updated: true }); } } return jsonError('ไม่พบ user_id: ' + user_id); } function deleteUser(req) { const { user_id } = req; if (!user_id) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ user_id'); const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Sheet users'); const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const idCol = data[0].indexOf('user_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === user_id) { sheet.deleteRow(r + 1); return jsonOk({ user_id: user_id, deleted: true }); } } return jsonError('ไม่พบ user_id: ' + user_id); } function updateUserField(userId, field, value) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sheet) return; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('user_id'); const fieldCol = headers.indexOf(field); if (fieldCol === -1) return; for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === userId) { sheet.getRange(r + 1, fieldCol + 1).setValue(value); return; } } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Customers (ลูกหนี้ / ลูกค้า) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const CUSTOMER_HEADERS = ['cust_id', 'cust_name', 'contact_person', 'phone', 'email', 'address', 'credit_limit', 'credit_status', 'payment_terms', 'notes']; function getCustomers(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); const data = sheetToObjects(sheet); // กรองตาม status ถ้ามี if (req.status) { return jsonOk(data.filter(c => c.credit_status === req.status)); } return jsonOk(data); } function createCustomer(req) { const { cust_name, contact_person, phone, email, address, credit_limit, payment_terms, notes } = req; if (!cust_name) return jsonError('กรุณาระบุชื่อลูกค้า'); const customer = { cust_id: generateId('C'), cust_name, contact_person: contact_person || '', phone: phone || '', email: email || '', address: address || '', credit_limit: Number(credit_limit) || 0, credit_status: (Number(req.credit_limit) || 0) > 0 ? 'active' : 'cash_only', payment_terms: payment_terms || '30 วัน', notes: notes || '', }; appendRow(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS, customer, CUSTOMER_HEADERS); return jsonOk(customer); } function updateCustomer(req) { const { cust_id } = req; if (!cust_id) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ cust_id'); const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === cust_id) { const fields = ['cust_name', 'contact_person', 'phone', 'email', 'address', 'credit_limit', 'credit_status', 'payment_terms', 'notes']; fields.forEach(f => { if (req[f] !== undefined) { const col = headers.indexOf(f); if (col !== -1) sheet.getRange(r + 1, col + 1).setValue(req[f]); } }); return jsonOk({ cust_id, updated: true }); } } return jsonError('ไม่พบ cust_id: ' + cust_id); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Credit Rules & Alerts // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const CREDIT_HEADERS = ['rule_id', 'cust_id', 'cust_name', 'credit_limit', 'due_date', 'alert_days_before', 'max_overdue_days', 'auto_break', 'next_pay_date', 'status', 'last_checked', 'notes']; const ALERT_HEADERS = ['alert_id', 'alert_type', 'cust_id', 'cust_name', 'phone', 'amount_due', 'due_date', 'days_overdue', 'action_taken', 'next_pay_date', 'action_by', 'action_at', 'notes']; function getCreditRules(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CREDIT_RULES); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); let data = sheetToObjects(sheet); if (req.cust_id) data = data.filter(r => r.cust_id === req.cust_id); if (req.status) data = data.filter(r => r.status === req.status); return jsonOk(data); } function setCreditRule(req) { const { cust_id, credit_limit, due_date, alert_days_before, max_overdue_days, auto_break, next_pay_date, notes } = req; if (!cust_id) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ cust_id'); // ดึงชื่อลูกค้า const custSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); const customers = custSheet ? sheetToObjects(custSheet) : []; const customer = customers.find(c => c.cust_id === cust_id); const custName = customer ? customer.cust_name : ''; const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(SHEETS.CREDIT_RULES, CREDIT_HEADERS); const existing = sheetToObjects(sheet); const existingRule = existing.find(r => r.cust_id === cust_id); if (existingRule) { // อัปเดต rule เดิม const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === cust_id) { const updates = { credit_limit, due_date, alert_days_before, max_overdue_days, auto_break, next_pay_date, notes, last_checked: nowDisplay() }; Object.keys(updates).forEach(f => { if (updates[f] !== undefined) { const col = headers.indexOf(f); if (col !== -1) sheet.getRange(r + 1, col + 1).setValue(updates[f]); } }); return jsonOk({ rule_id: existingRule.rule_id, updated: true }); } } // ── Fallback: rule อยู่ใน in-memory list แต่หา row ใน sheet ไม่เจอ (race condition/ถูกลบ) ── return jsonError('พบ rule ใน cache แต่หา row ใน sheet ไม่เจอ cust_id: ' + cust_id); } else { // สร้าง rule ใหม่ const rule = { rule_id: generateId('R'), cust_id, cust_name: custName, credit_limit: Number(credit_limit) || 0, due_date: due_date || '', alert_days_before: Number(alert_days_before) || 3, max_overdue_days: Number(max_overdue_days) || 7, auto_break: auto_break || 'NO', next_pay_date: next_pay_date || '', status: 'active', last_checked: nowDisplay(), notes: notes || '', }; appendRow(SHEETS.CREDIT_RULES, rule, CREDIT_HEADERS); return jsonOk(rule); } } function breakCredit(req) { const { cust_id, action_by, notes } = req; if (!cust_id) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ cust_id'); // อัปเดต credit_rules status const ruleSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CREDIT_RULES); if (ruleSheet) { const data = ruleSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); const statusCol = headers.indexOf('status'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === cust_id) { ruleSheet.getRange(r + 1, statusCol + 1).setValue('credit_break'); break; } } } // อัปเดต customers status const custSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); if (custSheet) { const data = custSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); const statusCol = headers.indexOf('credit_status'); let custName = '', phone = '', amountDue = 0; for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === cust_id) { custSheet.getRange(r + 1, statusCol + 1).setValue('credit_break'); custName = data[r][headers.indexOf('cust_name')]; phone = data[r][headers.indexOf('phone')]; break; } } // ดึงยอดค้างจาก ar_ledger const arSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (arSheet) { const arData = sheetToObjects(arSheet); const ar = arData.find(a => a.cust_id === cust_id); if (ar) amountDue = Number(ar.total_outstanding) || 0; } // บันทึก alert const alert = { alert_id: generateId('A'), alert_type: 'credit_break', cust_id, cust_name: custName, phone, amount_due: amountDue, due_date: formatDateDisplay(new Date()), days_overdue: 0, action_taken: 'credit_break', next_pay_date: '', action_by: action_by || 'Admin', action_at: nowDisplay(), notes: notes || 'เบรกเครดิตโดย ' + (action_by || 'Admin'), }; appendRow(SHEETS.ALERTS_LOG, alert, ALERT_HEADERS); return jsonOk({ cust_id, status: 'credit_break', alert_id: alert.alert_id }); } return jsonError('ไม่พบข้อมูลลูกค้า'); } function reschedulePayment(req) { const { cust_id, next_pay_date, action_by, notes } = req; if (!cust_id || !next_pay_date) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ cust_id หรือ next_pay_date'); // อัปเดต credit_rules const ruleSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CREDIT_RULES); if (ruleSheet) { const data = ruleSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][idCol] === cust_id) { const npCol = headers.indexOf('next_pay_date'); const stCol = headers.indexOf('status'); ruleSheet.getRange(r + 1, npCol + 1).setValue(next_pay_date); ruleSheet.getRange(r + 1, stCol + 1).setValue('rescheduled'); break; } } } // บันทึก alert const custSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); const customers = custSheet ? sheetToObjects(custSheet) : []; const cust = customers.find(c => c.cust_id === cust_id); const alert = { alert_id: generateId('A'), alert_type: 'reschedule', cust_id, cust_name: cust ? cust.cust_name : '', phone: cust ? cust.phone : '', amount_due: 0, due_date: '', days_overdue: 0, action_taken: 'reschedule', next_pay_date, action_by: action_by || 'Admin', action_at: nowDisplay(), notes: notes || 'นัดชำระใหม่ ' + next_pay_date, }; appendRow(SHEETS.ALERTS_LOG, alert, ALERT_HEADERS); return jsonOk({ cust_id, next_pay_date, alert_id: alert.alert_id }); } // ── Alerts Check (เรียกจาก Trigger รายวัน) ────────────── // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Credit Rules — JKN (จงกลณีการไฟฟ้า) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Rule: ทุกบิลเครดิตครบกำหนด "วันที่ 20 ของเดือนถัดไป" // - วันที่ 20: active → overdue // - วันที่ 25: overdue → credit_break (ห้ามเปิดบิลเครดิต แต่ขายเงินสดได้) // - จ่ายหมด: กลับเป็น active // - มีบิลค้างอยู่: คง overdue/credit_break ไว้ // คำนวณ due_date จากวันที่ซื้อ = วันที่ 20 ของเดือนถัดไป function calcDueDate(billDate) { const d = (billDate instanceof Date) ? billDate : parseDate(billDate); if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return null; // ไปเดือนถัดไป วันที่ 20 const nextMonth = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 20); return nextMonth; } // คำนวณสถานะเครดิตจากยอดค้าง + วันที่ปัจจุบัน function computeCreditStatus(bills, today) { today = today || new Date(); if (!bills || bills.length === 0) return 'active'; let hasOverdue = false; let hasCreditBreak = false; for (var i = 0; i < bills.length; i++) { var b = bills[i]; if (b.paid || Number(b.outstanding) <= 0) continue; var due = b.due_date instanceof Date ? b.due_date : parseDate(b.due_date); if (!due) continue; // วันที่ 20 = เลยกำหนด if (today >= due) hasOverdue = true; // หาวันที่ 25 ของเดือนเดียวกับ due_date var breakDate = new Date(due.getFullYear(), due.getMonth(), 25); if (today >= breakDate) hasCreditBreak = true; } if (hasCreditBreak) return 'credit_break'; if (hasOverdue) return 'overdue'; return 'active'; } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Check Alerts — รันทุกวัน 08:00 // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function checkAlerts(req) { const custSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); const arSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (!custSheet) return jsonOk({ checked: 0, alerts: [] }); const customers = sheetToObjects(custSheet); const arData = arSheet ? sheetToObjects(arSheet) : []; const today = new Date(); const alerts = []; var statusChanged = 0; customers.forEach(function(cust) { // ข้าม cash_only if (cust.credit_status === 'cash_only') return; // หาบิลค้างของลูกค้า const ar = arData.find(function(a) { return a.cust_id === cust.cust_id; }); if (!ar) return; if (!Number(ar.total_outstanding)) return; // คำนวณสถานะใหม่ const bills = ar.bills_json ? JSON.parse(ar.bills_json) : [{ due_date: ar.due_date, outstanding: ar.total_outstanding, paid: false, }]; const newStatus = computeCreditStatus(bills, today); const oldStatus = cust.credit_status || 'active'; // อัปเดตสถานะถ้าเปลี่ยน if (newStatus !== oldStatus) { updateCustomerStatus(cust.cust_id, newStatus); statusChanged++; } // สร้าง alert ถ้า overdue หรือ credit_break if (newStatus === 'overdue' || newStatus === 'credit_break') { alerts.push({ alert_type: newStatus, cust_id: cust.cust_id, cust_name: cust.cust_name, phone: cust.phone || '', contact_person: cust.contact_person || '', amount_due: Number(ar.total_outstanding) || 0, due_date: ar.due_date || '', bills_by_month: ar.bills_by_month || '', }); } }); // บันทึก alerts log alerts.forEach(function(a) { const alert = { alert_id: generateId('A'), alert_type: a.alert_type, cust_id: a.cust_id, cust_name: a.cust_name, phone: a.phone, amount_due: a.amount_due, due_date: a.due_date, days_overdue: 0, action_taken: 'notified', next_pay_date: '', action_by: 'System', action_at: nowDisplay(), notes: 'แจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ: ' + (a.alert_type === 'overdue' ? 'เลยกำหนดชำระ' : 'เบรกเครดิต'), }; appendRow(SHEETS.ALERTS_LOG, alert, ALERT_HEADERS); }); return jsonOk({ checked: customers.length, status_changed: statusChanged, alerts: alerts }); } // อัปเดตสถานะลูกค้า function updateCustomerStatus(custId, newStatus) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOMERS); if (!sheet) return; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); const statusCol = headers.indexOf('credit_status'); for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (String(data[r][idCol]).replace(/^'/, '') === String(custId)) { sheet.getRange(r + 1, statusCol + 1).setValue(newStatus); return true; } } return false; } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // บันทึกการชำระเงิน (ผู้ใช้เลือกบิลที่จะตัด) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function recordPayment(req) { const { cust_id, amount, bills_to_clear, payment_date, payment_method, reference, action_by } = req; if (!cust_id || !amount) return jsonError('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ (cust_id, amount)'); if (!bills_to_clear || !Array.isArray(bills_to_clear)) return jsonError('ต้องระบุบิลที่ต้องการตัดชำระ'); const arSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (!arSheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ AR Ledger'); const data = arSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('cust_id'); const outCol = headers.indexOf('total_outstanding'); const billsCol = headers.indexOf('bills_json'); var rowIdx = -1; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (String(data[r][idCol]).replace(/^'/, '') === String(cust_id)) { rowIdx = r; break; } } if (rowIdx === -1) return jsonError('ไม่พบลูกค้าใน AR Ledger'); // อ่าน bills เดิม var bills = []; if (billsCol >= 0 && data[rowIdx][billsCol]) { try { bills = JSON.parse(data[rowIdx][billsCol]); } catch (e) { bills = []; } } // ตัดบิลตามที่ user เลือก var totalPaid = 0; var remaining = Number(amount); bills_to_clear.forEach(function(billNo) { const bill = bills.find(function(b) { return b.bill_no === billNo; }); if (!bill || bill.paid) return; const out = Number(bill.outstanding) || 0; if (remaining >= out) { bill.paid = true; bill.paid_date = payment_date || formatDateDisplay(new Date()); bill.paid_amount = out; totalPaid += out; remaining -= out; } else if (remaining > 0) { bill.outstanding = out - remaining; bill.partial_paid = (Number(bill.partial_paid) || 0) + remaining; totalPaid += remaining; remaining = 0; } }); // อัปเดต AR Ledger const newOutstanding = bills.reduce(function(s, b) { return s + (b.paid ? 0 : Number(b.outstanding) || 0); }, 0); arSheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, outCol + 1).setValue(newOutstanding); if (billsCol >= 0) arSheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, billsCol + 1).setValue(JSON.stringify(bills)); // คำนวณสถานะใหม่ const newStatus = computeCreditStatus(bills, new Date()); updateCustomerStatus(cust_id, newStatus); // บันทึก payment log const paySheet = getOrCreateSheet('payments_log', ['payment_id', 'cust_id', 'payment_date', 'amount', 'payment_method', 'reference', 'bills_cleared', 'remaining', 'action_by', 'action_at']); paySheet.appendRow([ generateId('PAY'), "'" + cust_id, payment_date || formatDateDisplay(new Date()), totalPaid, payment_method || 'Cash', reference || '', bills_to_clear.join(', '), newOutstanding, action_by || 'Admin', nowDisplay(), ]); return jsonOk({ paid: totalPaid, remaining_change: remaining, new_outstanding: newOutstanding, new_status: newStatus, bills_cleared: bills_to_clear.length, }); } function getAlerts(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.ALERTS_LOG); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); let data = sheetToObjects(sheet); // กรองตาม cust_id if (req.cust_id) data = data.filter(a => a.cust_id === req.cust_id); // กรองตาม type if (req.alert_type) data = data.filter(a => a.alert_type === req.alert_type); // เรียงล่าสุดก่อน data.reverse(); // จำกัดจำนวน if (req.limit) data = data.slice(0, Number(req.limit)); return jsonOk(data); } // ── AR Ledger ─────────────────────────────────────────────── const AR_HEADERS = ['cust_id', 'cust_name', 'contact_person', 'phone', 'credit_limit', 'total_outstanding', 'credit_status', 'due_date', 'bills_json', 'bills_by_month', 'last_updated']; function getArLedger(req) { // ── Auto-refresh ถ้าขอ (req.refresh=true) หรือ data เก่า/ไม่มี ── // ลูกหนี้ = บิล SS/SR/OS/OR ที่ยัง match_status != 'matched' (ยังไม่ได้รับชำระ) if (req.refresh) { updateArLedger(req); } const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (!sheet) { // ไม่มี sheet → auto-build ครั้งแรก updateArLedger(req); } const finalSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (!finalSheet) return jsonOk([]); let data = sheetToObjects(finalSheet); // parse bills_json กลับ data.forEach(function(row) { try { row.bills = row.bills_json ? JSON.parse(row.bills_json) : []; } catch (e) { row.bills = []; } try { row.months = row.bills_by_month ? JSON.parse(row.bills_by_month) : {}; } catch (e) { row.months = {}; } }); if (req.status) data = data.filter(a => a.credit_status === req.status); if (req.cust_id) data = data.filter(a => String(a.cust_id) === String(req.cust_id)); return jsonOk(data); } // ── Auto-populate ar_ledger จาก bills_YYYY ทั้งหมด ── // ใช้บิลที่ยังไม่ matched เป็นลูกหนี้คงค้าง // รวบรวมตาม customer (remarks1/customer) → คำนวณ total_outstanding + bills_by_month + status function updateArLedger(req) { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var allBills = []; // ดึงบิลทุกปี (bills_YYYY) ss.getSheets().forEach(function(sh) { var name = sh.getName(); if (/^bills_\d{4}$/.test(name)) { allBills = allBills.concat(sheetToObjects(sh)); } }); if (allBills.length === 0) { // ไม่มีบิล → clear ar_ledger var arSheet = getOrCreateSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER, AR_HEADERS); if (arSheet.getLastRow() > 1) { arSheet.getRange(2, 1, arSheet.getLastRow()-1, AR_HEADERS.length).clearContent(); } return { updated: 0 }; } // ── Filter: บิลที่ยังเป็น "ลูกหนี้" ── // = type SS/SR/OS/OR + ยังไม่ matched + มีมูลค่าเป็นบวก (ไม่ใช่ refund) var arBills = allBills.filter(function(b) { var bt = String(b.bill_type || '').toUpperCase(); var ms = String(b.match_status || '').toLowerCase(); var amt = Number(b.amount) || 0; // รับ SS/SR/OS/OR ที่ไม่ matched return ['SS', 'SR', 'OS', 'OR'].includes(bt) && ms !== 'paid' && ms !== 'paid' && amt > 0; }); // ── Group by customer ── // ใช้ cust_id ถ้ามี ไม่งั้นใช้ customer name var byCust = {}; arBills.forEach(function(b) { var custId = b.cust_id || b.customer || 'Unknown'; var custName = b.customer || custId; if (!byCust[custId]) { byCust[custId] = { cust_id: custId, cust_name: custName, bills: [], total: 0, byMonth: {}, oldestDate: null, }; } byCust[custId].bills.push({ bill_no: b.bill_no || '', date: b.date || '', amount: Number(b.amount) || 0, type: b.bill_type || '', station: b.station || '', }); byCust[custId].total += Number(b.amount) || 0; // By month: '2026-01' → amount var dt = parseDate(b.date); if (dt) { var monthKey = dt.getFullYear() + '-' + String(dt.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'); byCust[custId].byMonth[monthKey] = (byCust[custId].byMonth[monthKey] || 0) + (Number(b.amount) || 0); // Track oldest if (!byCust[custId].oldestDate || dt < byCust[custId].oldestDate) { byCust[custId].oldestDate = dt; } } }); // ── Credit status: overdue ถ้ามีบิลเก่ากว่า 60 วัน, credit_break ถ้ากว่า 90 ── var today = new Date(); var DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; var now = nowDisplay(); // Load existing credit_limit from ar_ledger เพื่อไม่ให้หาย var existingLimits = {}; var existingSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); if (existingSheet) { var existing = sheetToObjects(existingSheet); existing.forEach(function(r) { existingLimits[r.cust_id] = { credit_limit: Number(r.credit_limit) || 0, contact_person: r.contact_person || '', phone: r.phone || '', }; }); } // ── Build rows ── var rows = []; Object.keys(byCust).forEach(function(custId) { var c = byCust[custId]; var oldestAge = c.oldestDate ? Math.floor((today - c.oldestDate) / DAY_MS) : 0; var status = 'active'; if (oldestAge > 90) status = 'credit_break'; else if (oldestAge > 60) status = 'overdue'; var existing = existingLimits[custId] || {}; var dueDate = c.oldestDate ? formatDateDisplay(new Date(c.oldestDate.getTime() + 30 * DAY_MS)) : ''; rows.push([ custId, c.cust_name, existing.contact_person || '', existing.phone || '', existing.credit_limit || 0, c.total, status, dueDate, JSON.stringify(c.bills), JSON.stringify(c.byMonth), now, ]); }); // ── Batch write ── var arSheet = getOrCreateSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER, AR_HEADERS); if (arSheet.getLastRow() > 1) { arSheet.getRange(2, 1, arSheet.getLastRow()-1, AR_HEADERS.length).clearContent(); } if (rows.length > 0) { arSheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, AR_HEADERS.length).setValues(rows); } return { updated: rows.length, total_bills_scanned: arBills.length }; } // Helper: parse dd/mm/yyyy function parseDate(str) { if (!str) return null; if (str instanceof Date) return str; const parts = String(str).split('/'); if (parts.length === 3) { return new Date(Number(parts[2]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[0])); } return new Date(str); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Daily Sales & Statements // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── Headers ตาม format ไฟล์จริง ── const DAILY_HEADERS = ['Bill Number', 'Bill Date', 'Station', 'Type', 'Baht', 'Confirmation', 'Pay Date', 'Remarks1', 'Remarks2', 'Bank - Due Date']; // Summary rows ที่อยู่ด้านบนของ Main sheet (Row 2-12) const STATIONS = ['Front1', 'Front2', 'Front3', 'Front4', 'Invoice', 'Order', 'Payment', 'Invoice Credit', 'Order Credit']; const SUMMARY_ROWS = ['นำเงินเข้า', 'ยอดขาย', 'นำเงินออก', 'ยอดคืนสินค้า', 'Cash Drawer', 'Transfer', 'Card', 'Cheque', 'Refund', 'Sum']; // KTB Statement const STMT_KTB_HEADERS = ['Date', 'Teller Id', 'Transaction Code', 'Description', 'Cheque No.', 'Amount', 'Tax', 'Balance', 'Init Br.', 'Front1', 'Front2', 'Front3', 'Front4', 'Invoice', 'Check', 'Remarks']; // KBANK Statement const STMT_KBANK_HEADERS = ['วันที่', 'เวลา/ วันที่มีผล', 'รายการ', 'ถอนเงิน', 'xxx', 'ฝากเงิน', 'xxx2', 'ยอดคงเหลือ', 'xxx3', 'ช่องทาง', 'xxx4', 'รายละเอียด', 'Front1', 'Front2', 'Front3', 'Front4', 'Invoice', 'Check']; // BBL Statement const STMT_BBL_HEADERS = ['No', 'วันที่', 'รายละเอียด', 'หักบัญชี', 'เข้าบัญชี', 'ยอดคงเหลือ', 'ช่องทางทำรายการ', 'Bill Number', 'Remarks', 'Remarks2']; // Smart Docs (SS/SR/OS/OR) const SMART_HEADERS = ['Bill No', 'Tax Rate', 'Customer', 'Date', 'Cashier', 'Amount Excl VAT', 'VAT', 'Discount', 'Amount Incl VAT']; // Bill List (รวมทุกบิล) const BILLLIST_HEADERS = ['Bill Number', 'Bill Date', 'Station', 'Type', 'Baht', 'Confirmation', 'Pay Date', 'Remarks1', 'Remarks2', 'Bank - Due Date']; // ── Year-based Headers (1 sheet ต่อปี) ── // รวมฟิลด์ date ในทุกแถว เพราะเก็บข้ามวันในแผ่นเดียวกัน // Order: bill_uuid → UI columns → match group → metadata → legacy aliases (last) const BILLS_YEAR_HEADERS = [ // System PK 'bill_uuid', // UI columns (ตามลำดับใน Bills page table) 'bill_no', 'date', 'station', 'type', 'amount', 'wht_enabled', 'wht_amount', 'transfer_fee', 'card_fee', 'confirmation', 'pay_date', 'remarks1', 'remarks2', 'bank', 'due_date', // Match group (paid_by/paid_bills/match_method/match_reason เป็น stmt fields ไม่ใช่ bill) 'match_status', 'match_ref', 'paid_at', 'paid_by_sp', // Metadata 'source', 'created_at', // Legacy aliases (mirror columns) — ทำหน้าที่ duplicate ของ field หลัก // ไม่ได้ใช้ตรง ๆ แต่ยังเก็บไว้เพื่อ backward compat 'bill_type', 'pay_method', 'match_bank', 'match_date' ]; // ── STMT_HEADERS: Generic schema for stmts_YYYY sheets ── const STMT_HEADERS = ['stmt_id', 'bank', 'date', 'time', 'ref_no', 'description', 'amount', 'balance', 'match_status', 'match_station', 'match_bill', 'match_sp', 'matched_by', 'matched_at', 'notes']; // ── Daily Meta Headers (1 sheet ต่อปี: meta_YYYY) ── // เก็บ summary + cash_count + change แบบ key-value ต่อวัน // meta_type: 'summary' | 'cash_count' | 'change' // - summary: key=cashIn/sales/cashOut/returns/cashDrawer/transfer/card/cheque/refund, station=Front1..., value=amount // - cash_count: key=denomination (1/5/10/20/50/100/500/1000), station=Front1..., value=count // - change: key=denomination, station=Invoice (ทอนอยู่ที่ Invoice), value=count const META_HEADERS = ['date', 'meta_type', 'station', 'key', 'value', 'updated_at', 'updated_by']; // ── Helper: sheet ชื่อ meta_YYYY ── function getMetaSheet(dateKey) { var year = getYearFromKey(dateKey); return 'meta_' + year; } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // filterByDateFlex — ใช้เก็บต่อปี แต่ filter ได้หลายระดับ // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ตรวจ req ว่าให้ filter แบบไหน แล้ว return function(row) → bool // req.date = 'YYYYMMDD' → วันเดียว // req.month = 'YYYYMM' → ทั้งเดือน // req.year = 'YYYY' → ทั้งปี (default ถ้าไม่ส่ง filter) // req.date_from = 'YYYYMMDD' + req.date_to = 'YYYYMMDD' → ช่วง // field default = 'date' → ชื่อ column ที่เก็บวันที่ (dd/mm/yyyy) function filterByDateFlex(req, field) { field = field || 'date'; // ── Priority 1: date_from + date_to (ช่วง) ── if (req.date_from && req.date_to) { var from = parseDateKey(req.date_from); var to = parseDateKey(req.date_to); return function(row) { var d = parseDate(row[field]); if (!d) return false; return d >= from && d <= to; }; } // ── Priority 2: date (วันเดียว) ── if (req.date && /^\d{8}$/.test(String(req.date))) { var target = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(req.date)); return function(row) { return String(row[field] || '').trim() === target; }; } // ── Priority 3: month (YYYYMM หรือ YYYY-MM) ── if (req.month) { var monthKey = String(req.month).replace('-', ''); // monthKey = YYYYMM; target mm/yyyy var year = monthKey.substring(0, 4); var mon = monthKey.substring(4, 6); return function(row) { var s = String(row[field] || '').trim(); // dd/mm/yyyy → ต้องมี /mm/yyyy ตรง var m = s.match(/^\d{1,2}\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (!m) return false; var y2 = m[2]; if (Number(y2) > 2400) y2 = String(Number(y2) - 543); return String(m[1]).padStart(2,'0') === mon && y2 === year; }; } // ── Priority 4: year (ทั้งปี) — default ถ้าไม่ส่ง filter อื่น ── if (req.year) { var y = String(req.year); return function(row) { var s = String(row[field] || '').trim(); var m = s.match(/\/(\d{4})/); if (!m) return false; var y2 = m[1]; if (Number(y2) > 2400) y2 = String(Number(y2) - 543); return y2 === y; }; } // ── Default: รับทุกแถว (ใช้ทั้ง year sheet) ── return function() { return true; }; } function getDailySales(req) { // รองรับ req.date (YYYYMMDD), req.month (YYYYMM), req.year (YYYY), req.date_from+date_to // ถ้าไม่ส่งอะไรเลย → ใช้ปีปัจจุบัน const pivotDate = req.date || req.date_from || (req.month ? req.month + '01' : '') || (req.year ? req.year + '0101' : todayKey()); const ss = getSpreadsheet(); // ── หา sheet bills_YYYY จาก pivot date ── var yearSheetName = getDailySheet(pivotDate); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(yearSheetName); var sheetName = yearSheetName; // ถ้าเป็น date range ข้ามปี ให้รวมหลาย sheet var sheetsToScan = []; if (req.date_from && req.date_to) { var yFrom = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_from)); var yTo = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_to)); for (var y = yFrom; y <= yTo; y++) { var s = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + y); if (s) sheetsToScan.push(s); } } else { if (sheet) sheetsToScan.push(sheet); } if (sheetsToScan.length === 0) { return jsonOk({ scope: req, data: [], totals: {}, sheet_name: yearSheetName }); } // ── รวมข้อมูลทุก sheet ที่อยู่ในช่วง ── var all = []; sheetsToScan.forEach(function(s) { all = all.concat(sheetToObjects(s)); }); // ── Apply date filter ── var matchDate = filterByDateFlex(req, 'date'); var data = all.filter(matchDate); // กรอง station (ถ้าระบุ) if (req.station && req.station !== 'ALL') { data = data.filter(function(d) { return d.station === req.station; }); } var totals = { count: data.length, total_sales: data.filter(function(d) { return Number(d.amount) > 0; }).reduce(function(s, d) { return s + Number(d.amount); }, 0), total_returns: data.filter(function(d) { return Number(d.amount) < 0; }).reduce(function(s, d) { return s + Number(d.amount); }, 0), net_sales: data.reduce(function(s, d) { return s + Number(d.amount); }, 0), by_station: {}, }; data.forEach(function(d) { var st = d.station || ''; if (!totals.by_station[st]) totals.by_station[st] = 0; totals.by_station[st] += Number(d.amount) || 0; }); // ── Field aliases: Sheet column names → Frontend field names ── // frontend ใช้ b.type, b.confirmation, b.pay_date แต่ sheet เก็บเป็น // pay_method, match_bank, match_date → ต้อง alias data = data.map(function(d) { return Object.assign({}, d, { // Core aliases type: d.type || d.pay_method || '', confirmation: d.confirmation || d.match_bank || '', pay_date: d.pay_date || d.match_date || '', // Extra aliases status: d.status || d.match_status || '', matched_stmt_ref: d.matched_stmt_ref || d.match_ref || '', bank: d.bank || d.match_bank || '', remarks1: d.remarks1 || d.customer || '', remarks2: d.remarks2 || d.notes || '', vat_amount: d.vat_amount != null ? d.vat_amount : (d.vat || 0), // Numbers: ensure proper type (sheet may return string) wht_amount: Number(d.wht_amount) || 0, transfer_fee: Number(d.transfer_fee) || 0, card_fee: Number(d.card_fee) || 0, // Booleans is_vat: d.is_vat === 'Y' ? true : (d.is_vat === 'N' ? false : undefined), }); }); return jsonOk({ scope: { date: req.date, month: req.month, year: req.year, date_from: req.date_from, date_to: req.date_to }, sheet_name: sheetName, data: data, totals: totals, summary: {} }); } function saveDailySales(req) { const date = req.date || todayKey(); const rows = req.rows; if (!rows || !Array.isArray(rows)) return jsonError('ไม่มีข้อมูล rows'); // Year-based sheet const sheetName = getDailySheet(date); const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, BILLS_YEAR_HEADERS); const sheetHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; const defaultDateDisplay = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(date)); // ── FULL REPLACE MODE ── // ถ้า req.replace_all === true → clear sheet ทั้งปี แล้ว write ใหม่ // ใช้เวลาต้อง sync full (ครั้งแรก หรือ manual full-sync) // ข้อดี: clean, atomic, ไม่มีบิลซ้ำแม้ bill_no ว่าง const replaceAll = req.replace_all === true || req.replace_all === 'true'; // ── APPEND ONLY MODE ── // ถ้า req.append_only === true → append ตรงๆ ไม่ dedup (ใช้กับ chunks ถัดไปหลัง replace_all) // ต้องใช้ร่วมกับ replace_all ในชุด batch เดียวกัน เพื่อให้ได้ผลรวมที่ clean const appendOnly = req.append_only === true || req.append_only === 'true'; let preserved = []; let skipDedup = false; if (replaceAll) { // Clear + write fresh — preserved = [] skipDedup = true; } else if (appendOnly) { // Read existing → keep all → append new const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow > 1) { preserved = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sheetHeaders.length).getValues(); } skipDedup = true; } else { // ── Normal: REPLACE BY composite key ── // Key: bill_no + date + amount + pay_method // เหตุผล: บาง bill_no (เช่น SP) มีจ่ายหลายครั้ง (split/partial payment) // → dedup by bill_no อย่างเดียว จะ merge ข้อมูลหาย // → ใช้ composite key เพื่อแยก payment แต่ละงวด function compositeKey(r) { return [ String(r.bill_no || '').trim(), String(r.date || '').trim(), String(Number(r.amount) || 0), String(r.pay_method || r.type || '').trim(), ].join('|'); } const newKeySet = {}; rows.forEach(function(r) { const bn = String(r.bill_no || '').trim(); if (bn) newKeySet[compositeKey(r)] = true; }); const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); const existing = lastRow > 1 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sheetHeaders.length).getValues() : []; const billNoIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('bill_no'); const dateIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('date'); const amtIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('amount'); const payIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('pay_method'); preserved = existing.filter(function(r) { const existingBillNo = String(r[billNoIdx] || '').trim(); if (!existingBillNo) return true; // preserve rows without bill_no const key = [ existingBillNo, String(r[dateIdx] || '').trim(), String(Number(r[amtIdx]) || 0), String(r[payIdx] || '').trim(), ].join('|'); return !newKeySet[key]; }); } // ── Build new rows ── let rowId = preserved.length; const newRows = rows.map(function(row) { rowId++; const billType = extractBillType(row.bill_no); const station = row.station || ''; var payMethod = row.pay_method || row.type || ''; if (billType === 'SC') payMethod = 'Refund'; else if (billType === 'SR') payMethod = 'Unknown'; else if (billType === 'OR') payMethod = 'Unknown'; else if (billType === 'OS' || billType === 'SS' || billType === 'SP') { payMethod = row.pay_method || row.type || ''; } const vatRate = 0.07; const amount = Number(row.amount) || 0; const vat = Math.round(amount * vatRate / (1 + vatRate) * 100) / 100; const net = amount - vat; var rowDate = row.date || row.date_th || row['Bill Date'] || defaultDateDisplay; rowDate = convertDate(rowDate); const entry = { row_id: rowId, bill_no: row.bill_no || '', bill_type: billType, date: rowDate, station: station, pay_method: payMethod, customer: row.customer || row.remarks1 || '', cust_id: row.cust_id || '', amount: amount, vat: vat, net_amount: net, sp_no: row.sp_no || '', pp_no: row.pp_no || '', match_status: row.match_status || row.status || 'pending', match_bank: row.match_bank || row.confirmation || row.bank || '', match_date: row.match_date || row.pay_date || '', match_ref: row.match_ref || row.matched_stmt_ref || '', notes: row.notes || row.remarks2 || '', source_file: row.source_file || '', created_by: req.created_by || '', created_at: nowDisplay(), // ── Extra fields จาก backup JSON ── wht_amount: Number(row.wht_amount) || 0, wht_enabled: row.wht_enabled === true || row.wht_enabled === 'true' ? 'Y' : '', transfer_fee: Number(row.transfer_fee) || 0, card_fee: Number(row.card_fee) || 0, due_date: row.due_date || '', matched_at: row.matched_at || '', imported_from: row._imported_from || row.imported_from || '', imported_at: row._imported_at || row.imported_at || '', }; return sheetHeaders.map(function(h) { return entry[h] !== undefined ? entry[h] : ''; }); }); // ── Clear + Write ── const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow > 1) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sheetHeaders.length).clearContent(); } const finalRows = preserved.concat(newRows); if (finalRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, finalRows.length, sheetHeaders.length).setValues(finalRows); } return jsonOk({ date: date, sheet_name: sheetName, rows_added: newRows.length, preserved: preserved.length, total_rows: finalRows.length, mode: replaceAll ? 'full_replace' : (appendOnly ? 'append_only' : 'upsert'), }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Daily Meta — เก็บ summary + cash_count + change_data // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // รองรับ req ที่มีฟิลด์: // - summary: { cashIn_Front1: 5155, sales_Front2: 16514, ..., __byDate: {...} } // - cash_count: { __byDate: { '2026-01-02': { '1000_Invoice': 416, ... } } } // - change_data: { '2026-01-02': { 'Invoice': { '1': 50, ... } } } (ไม่บังคับ - จะดึงจาก cash_count ก็ได้) function saveDailyMeta(req) { var date = req.date || todayKey(); var updatedBy = req.created_by || req.updated_by || ''; var nowStr = nowDisplay(); var sheetName = getMetaSheet(date); var sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, META_HEADERS); var allRows = []; // แถวใหม่ที่จะเขียน // ─── 1) summary: key-value ต่อ station ต่อวัน ─── // Frontend ส่งในหลายรูปแบบ: // a) Flat keys: { cashIn_Front1: 5155, sales_Front2: 16514 } → ของวันปัจจุบัน // b) __byDate: { __byDate: { '2026-01-02': { นำเงินเข้า: { Front1: 5155, ... } } } } if (req.summary && typeof req.summary === 'object') { var s = req.summary; // แบบ a) flat keys Object.keys(s).forEach(function(k) { if (k === '__byDate') return; if (!k.includes('_')) return; var val = Number(s[k]); if (isNaN(val) || val === 0) return; // split "cashIn_Front1" → key=cashIn, station=Front1 var idx = k.indexOf('_'); var key = k.substring(0, idx); var station = k.substring(idx + 1); var targetDate = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(date)); allRows.push([targetDate, 'summary', station, key, val, nowStr, updatedBy]); }); // แบบ b) __byDate (Thai labels) if (s.__byDate && typeof s.__byDate === 'object') { var LABEL_MAP = { 'นำเงินเข้า': 'cashIn', 'ยอดขาย': 'sales', 'นำเงินออก': 'cashOut', 'ยอดคืนสินค้า': 'returns', 'Cash Drawer': 'cashDrawer', 'Transfer': 'transfer', 'Card': 'card', 'Cheque': 'cheque', 'Refund': 'refund', }; Object.keys(s.__byDate).forEach(function(dateISO) { var dayData = s.__byDate[dateISO]; var targetDate = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(dateISO.replace(/-/g, ''))); Object.keys(dayData || {}).forEach(function(label) { var prefix = LABEL_MAP[label] || label; var stations = dayData[label] || {}; Object.keys(stations).forEach(function(station) { var val = Number(stations[station]); if (isNaN(val) || val === 0) return; allRows.push([targetDate, 'summary', station, prefix, val, nowStr, updatedBy]); }); }); }); } } // ─── 2) cash_count: denomination count ต่อ station ต่อวัน ─── // Frontend: { __byDate: { '2026-01-02': { '1000_Invoice': 416, '500_Invoice': 5, ... } } } if (req.cash_count && typeof req.cash_count === 'object') { var cc = req.cash_count; if (cc.__byDate && typeof cc.__byDate === 'object') { Object.keys(cc.__byDate).forEach(function(dateISO) { var dayCash = cc.__byDate[dateISO] || {}; var targetDate = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(dateISO.replace(/-/g, ''))); Object.keys(dayCash).forEach(function(k) { // key pattern: "1000_Invoice" → denom=1000, station=Invoice var idx = k.indexOf('_'); if (idx === -1) return; var denom = k.substring(0, idx); var station = k.substring(idx + 1); var val = Number(dayCash[k]); if (isNaN(val) || val === 0) return; allRows.push([targetDate, 'cash_count', station, denom, val, nowStr, updatedBy]); }); }); } } // ─── 3) change_data (ถ้าส่งมาแยก): denomination count ต่อ station ต่อวัน ─── // Frontend: { '2026-01-02': { 'Invoice': { '1': 50, '5': 15, ... } } } if (req.change_data && typeof req.change_data === 'object') { Object.keys(req.change_data).forEach(function(dateISO) { var stationData = req.change_data[dateISO] || {}; var targetDate = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(dateISO.replace(/-/g, ''))); Object.keys(stationData).forEach(function(station) { var denoms = stationData[station] || {}; Object.keys(denoms).forEach(function(denom) { var val = Number(denoms[denom]); if (isNaN(val) || val === 0) return; allRows.push([targetDate, 'change', station, denom, val, nowStr, updatedBy]); }); }); }); } if (allRows.length === 0) { return { rows_added: 0 }; } // ── Batch write ── // DELETE old meta rows for the dates+types we're updating (ป้องกัน duplicate) var datesToClear = {}; allRows.forEach(function(row) { datesToClear[row[0] + '|' + row[1]] = true; // date|meta_type }); // อ่านข้อมูลเดิม → filter ออก → เขียนใหม่ทั้งหมด (ช้ากว่าเล็กน้อยแต่ simpler) var existingData = sheet.getLastRow() > 1 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, META_HEADERS.length).getValues() : []; var preserved = existingData.filter(function(r) { var dateTypeKey = String(r[0]) + '|' + String(r[1]); return !datesToClear[dateTypeKey]; }); var finalRows = preserved.concat(allRows); // clear เก่า + เขียนใหม่ if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, META_HEADERS.length).clearContent(); } if (finalRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, finalRows.length, META_HEADERS.length).setValues(finalRows); } return { rows_added: allRows.length, total_rows: finalRows.length }; } // ── Get meta (summary + cash_count + change) for a date/month/year/range ── function getDailyMeta(req) { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var pivotDate = req.date || req.date_from || (req.month ? req.month + '01' : '') || (req.year ? req.year + '0101' : todayKey()); // รวม sheets ของ years ในช่วง var sheetsToScan = []; if (req.date_from && req.date_to) { var yFrom = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_from)); var yTo = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_to)); for (var y = yFrom; y <= yTo; y++) { var s = ss.getSheetByName('meta_' + y); if (s) sheetsToScan.push(s); } } else { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(getMetaSheet(pivotDate)); if (sh) sheetsToScan.push(sh); } if (sheetsToScan.length === 0) { return jsonOk({ summary: {}, cash_count: { __byDate: {} }, change_data: {}, rows: 0 }); } var all = []; sheetsToScan.forEach(function(s) { all = all.concat(sheetToObjects(s)); }); var matchDate = filterByDateFlex(req, 'date'); var matched = all.filter(matchDate); // Reconstruct ออกมาเป็น object frontend ใช้ได้เลย var summary = { __byDate: {} }; var cashCount = { __byDate: {} }; var changeData = {}; matched.forEach(function(r) { // r.date = dd/mm/yyyy → แปลงกลับ ISO var parts = String(r.date).split('/'); if (parts.length !== 3) return; var y = parts[2]; if (Number(y) > 2400) y = String(Number(y) - 543); var dateISO = y + '-' + String(parts[1]).padStart(2,'0') + '-' + String(parts[0]).padStart(2,'0'); if (r.meta_type === 'summary') { // flat keys สำหรับวัน pivotDate (compat กับ frontend แบบเก่า) if (req.date && dateISO === (String(req.date).substring(0,4)+'-'+String(req.date).substring(4,6)+'-'+String(req.date).substring(6,8))) { summary[r.key + '_' + r.station] = Number(r.value) || 0; } // __byDate เสมอ if (!summary.__byDate[dateISO]) summary.__byDate[dateISO] = {}; // map กลับ Thai label var REVERSE_MAP = {cashIn:'นำเงินเข้า', sales:'ยอดขาย', cashOut:'นำเงินออก', returns:'ยอดคืนสินค้า', cashDrawer:'Cash Drawer', transfer:'Transfer', card:'Card', cheque:'Cheque', refund:'Refund'}; var thLabel = REVERSE_MAP[r.key] || r.key; if (!summary.__byDate[dateISO][thLabel]) summary.__byDate[dateISO][thLabel] = {}; summary.__byDate[dateISO][thLabel][r.station] = Number(r.value) || 0; } else if (r.meta_type === 'cash_count') { if (!cashCount.__byDate[dateISO]) cashCount.__byDate[dateISO] = {}; cashCount.__byDate[dateISO][r.key + '_' + r.station] = Number(r.value) || 0; } else if (r.meta_type === 'change') { if (!changeData[dateISO]) changeData[dateISO] = {}; if (!changeData[dateISO][r.station]) changeData[dateISO][r.station] = {}; changeData[dateISO][r.station][r.key] = Number(r.value) || 0; } }); return jsonOk({ summary: summary, cash_count: cashCount, change_data: changeData, rows: matched.length }); } function importDailySales(req) { // สำหรับ import จาก Excel ที่วางลง // Frontend จะ parse Excel เป็น JSON แล้วส่งมาทาง rows return saveDailySales(req); } // ── Statement ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function getStatements(req) { const bankFilter = req.bank || 'ALL'; const ss = getSpreadsheet(); const pivotDate = req.date || req.date_from || (req.month ? req.month + '01' : '') || (req.year ? req.year + '0101' : todayKey()); // หา sheets ที่ต้องอ่าน (รองรับ cross-year range) var sheetsToScan = []; if (req.date_from && req.date_to) { var yFrom = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_from)); var yTo = Number(getYearFromKey(req.date_to)); for (var y = yFrom; y <= yTo; y++) { var s = ss.getSheetByName('stmts_' + y); if (s) sheetsToScan.push(s); } } else { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(getStmtSheet(pivotDate)); if (sh) sheetsToScan.push(sh); } if (sheetsToScan.length === 0) { return jsonOk({ scope: req, data: [], totals: {count:0, total_amount:0, matched:0, unmatched:0} }); } var all = []; sheetsToScan.forEach(function(s) { all = all.concat(sheetToObjects(s)); }); var matchDate = filterByDateFlex(req, 'date'); var allData = all.filter(function(r) { if (!matchDate(r)) return false; if (bankFilter !== 'ALL' && r.bank !== bankFilter) return false; return true; }); var totals = { count: allData.length, total_amount: allData.reduce(function(s, d) { return s + (Number(d.amount) || 0); }, 0), matched: allData.filter(function(d) { return d.match_status === 'paid'; }).length, unmatched: allData.filter(function(d) { return d.match_status !== 'paid'; }).length, }; return jsonOk({ scope: { date: req.date, month: req.month, year: req.year, date_from: req.date_from, date_to: req.date_to }, data: allData, totals: totals }); } // ── Parse Statement ตาม format ธนาคารจริง ── function parseStatementSheet(sheet, bank) { var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var rows = []; if (bank === 'KTB') { // KTB: headers row 11 (index 10), data from row 12 (index 11) var headerRow = -1; for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(15, data.length); i++) { if (data[i] && String(data[i][0] || '').includes('Date')) { headerRow = i; break; } } if (headerRow === -1) headerRow = 10; // default for (var i = headerRow + 1; i < data.length; i++) { var r = data[i]; var dateVal = r[0]; var amount = Number(r[5]) || 0; if (!dateVal || !amount) continue; rows.push({ bank: 'KTB', date: convertDate(dateVal), time: extractTime(dateVal), ref_no: String(r[3] || ''), description: String(r[3] || ''), amount: amount, balance: Number(r[7]) || 0, match_status: 'unpaid', match_station: '', match_bill: '', }); } } else if (bank === 'KBANK') { // KBANK: headers row 11 (index 10), data from row 12 var headerRow = -1; for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(15, data.length); i++) { if (data[i] && (String(data[i][1] || '').includes('วันที่') || String(data[i][0] || '').includes('วันที่'))) { headerRow = i; break; } } if (headerRow === -1) headerRow = 10; for (var i = headerRow + 1; i < data.length; i++) { var r = data[i]; var dateVal = r[0] || r[1]; var deposit = Number(r[6]) || Number(r[5]) || 0; var withdraw = Number(r[4]) || Number(r[3]) || 0; var amount = deposit || 0; if (!dateVal || !amount) continue; rows.push({ bank: 'KBANK', date: convertDate(dateVal), time: String(r[1] || r[2] || ''), ref_no: String(r[12] || r[11] || ''), description: String(r[3] || r[2] || ''), amount: amount, balance: Number(r[8]) || Number(r[7]) || 0, match_status: 'unpaid', match_station: '', match_bill: '', }); } } else if (bank === 'BBL') { // BBL: headers row 4 (index 3), data from row 5 var headerRow = -1; for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(8, data.length); i++) { if (data[i] && (String(data[i][1] || '').includes('วันที่') || String(data[i][2] || '').includes('รายละเอียด'))) { headerRow = i; break; } } if (headerRow === -1) headerRow = 3; for (var i = headerRow + 1; i < data.length; i++) { var r = data[i]; var dateVal = r[1]; var credit = Number(r[4]) || 0; var debit = Number(r[3]) || 0; if (!dateVal || (!credit && !debit)) continue; // ข้ามแถวสรุป if (String(r[2] || '').includes('ยอดรวม')) continue; if (String(r[0] || '').includes('หมายเหตุ')) continue; rows.push({ bank: 'BBL', date: convertDate(dateVal), time: extractTime(dateVal), ref_no: String(r[7] || ''), description: String(r[2] || ''), amount: credit || -debit, balance: Number(r[5]) || 0, match_status: 'unpaid', match_station: '', match_bill: '', }); } } return rows; } // ── แปลงวันที่ + พ.ศ. → ค.ศ. ── function convertDate(val) { if (!val) return ''; // ถ้าเป็น Date object if (val instanceof Date) { return formatDateDisplay(val); } var str = String(val).trim(); // แปลง พ.ศ. → ค.ศ. (ปี 25xx → 20xx) // รองรับ format: 2569-04-06, 06/04/2569, ฯลฯ str = str.replace(/(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/, function(m, y, mo, d) { var year = Number(y); if (year > 2400) year -= 543; return d + '/' + mo + '/' + year; }); // รองรับ dd/mm/yyyy ที่ปีเป็น พ.ศ. str = str.replace(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/, function(m, d, mo, y) { var year = Number(y); if (year > 2400) year -= 543; return String(d).padStart(2,'0') + '/' + String(mo).padStart(2,'0') + '/' + year; }); // ตัดเวลาออก (ถ้ามี) var datePart = str.split(' ')[0]; // ถ้ายังเป็น YYYY-MM-DD → แปลงเป็น dd/mm/yyyy if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(datePart)) { var parts = datePart.split('-'); return parts[2] + '/' + parts[1] + '/' + parts[0]; } return datePart; } function extractTime(val) { if (!val) return ''; var str = String(val); var match = str.match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(:\d{2})?/); return match ? match[0] : ''; } function normalizeStmtRow(r, bank) { return { bank: bank, date: convertDate(r.date || r.Date || r['วันที่'] || ''), time: r.time || extractTime(r.date || r.Date || ''), ref_no: r.ref_no || r['Ref.'] || r['Description'] || '', description: r.description || r['Description'] || r['รายละเอียด'] || r['รายการ'] || '', amount: Number(r.amount || r.Amount || r['ฝากเงิน'] || r['เข้าบัญชี'] || 0), balance: Number(r.balance || r.Balance || r['ยอดคงเหลือ'] || 0), match_status: r.match_status || 'unpaid', match_station: r.match_station || '', match_bill: r.match_bill || '', }; } function saveStatements(req) { const date = req.date || todayKey(); const rows = req.rows; if (!rows || !Array.isArray(rows)) return jsonError('ไม่มีข้อมูล rows'); // ── FULL REPLACE MODE ── // ถ้า req.replace_all === true → clear sheet stmts_YYYY ของทุกปีที่ถูก push const replaceAll = req.replace_all === true || req.replace_all === 'true'; // Helper: composite key สำหรับ dedup function stmtKey(r) { return [ String(r.bank || '').trim(), String(r.date || '').trim(), String(r.time || '').trim(), String(Number(r.amount) || 0), String(r.ref_no || '').trim(), ].join('|'); } const newKeySet = {}; rows.forEach(function(r) { newKeySet[stmtKey(r)] = true; }); // Group rows ตามปี (รองรับ cross-year) const rowsByYear = {}; rows.forEach(function(r) { const dateStr = String(r.date || '').trim(); const match = dateStr.match(/\/(\d{4})$/); const year = match ? match[1] : String(new Date().getFullYear()); const sheetKey = 'stmts_' + year; if (!rowsByYear[sheetKey]) rowsByYear[sheetKey] = []; rowsByYear[sheetKey].push(r); }); let totalAdded = 0; let totalPreserved = 0; Object.keys(rowsByYear).forEach(function(sheetName) { const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, STMT_HEADERS); const sheetHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); const existing = (replaceAll || lastRow <= 1) ? [] : sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sheetHeaders.length).getValues(); const bankIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('bank'); const dateIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('date'); const timeIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('time'); const amtIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('amount'); const refIdx = sheetHeaders.indexOf('ref_no'); function rowToKey(r) { return [r[bankIdx], r[dateIdx], r[timeIdx], Number(r[amtIdx])||0, r[refIdx]].join('|'); } const preserved = existing.filter(function(r) { return !newKeySet[rowToKey(r)]; }); let stmtId = preserved.length; const yearRows = rowsByYear[sheetName]; const newRows = yearRows.map(function(row) { stmtId++; var amount = row.amount != null ? Number(row.amount) : 0; if (amount === 0 && (row.withdraw || row.deposit)) { amount = (Number(row.deposit) || 0) - (Number(row.withdraw) || 0); } const entry = { stmt_id: stmtId, bank: row.bank || '', date: row.date || formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(date)), time: row.time || '', ref_no: row.ref_no || '', description: row.description || '', amount: amount, balance: Number(row.balance) || 0, match_status: row.match_status || 'unpaid', match_station: row.match_station || '', match_bill: row.match_bill || '', match_sp: row.match_sp || '', matched_by: row.matched_by || '', matched_at: row.matched_at || '', notes: row.notes || row.remarks || row.description || '', }; return sheetHeaders.map(function(h) { return entry[h] !== undefined ? entry[h] : ''; }); }); if (lastRow > 1) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sheetHeaders.length).clearContent(); } const finalRows = preserved.concat(newRows); if (finalRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, finalRows.length, sheetHeaders.length).setValues(finalRows); } totalAdded += newRows.length; totalPreserved += preserved.length; }); return jsonOk({ rows_added: totalAdded, preserved: totalPreserved, total_rows: totalAdded + totalPreserved, }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Bill Type Registry — ทุกเลขเอกสารในระบบ // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // // AR (ลูกหนี้): // SS = บิลขาย VAT · SR = ใบกำกับภาษี Credit // OS = บิลขาย NOVAT · OR = บิลขาย NOVAT Credit // SC = ใบลดหนี้ลูกหนี้ VAT // JS / JR = ใบลดหนี้ลูกหนี้ NOVAT // SP = เลขตัดชำระลูกหนี้ // // AP (เจ้าหนี้): // PR = ซื้อเชื่อ (VAT) · PS = ซื้อสด/โอน (VAT) // MR = ซื้อเชื่อ (NOVAT) · MS = ซื้อสด/โอน (NOVAT) // PC = ใบลดหนี้เจ้าหนี้ VAT // BR / BS = ใบลดหนี้เจ้าหนี้ NOVAT // PX = เอกสารอื่นๆ (VAT หรือ NOVAT ตาม context) // PP = เลขตัดชำระเจ้าหนี้ const BILL_TYPE_META = { // AR VAT 'SS': { side: 'AR', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'บิลขาย' }, 'SR': { side: 'AR', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'ใบกำกับภาษี Credit' }, 'SC': { side: 'AR', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้ลูกหนี้' }, // AR NOVAT 'OS': { side: 'AR', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'บิลขาย NOVAT' }, 'OR': { side: 'AR', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'บิลขาย NOVAT Credit' }, 'JS': { side: 'AR', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้ลูกหนี้ NOVAT' }, 'JR': { side: 'AR', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้ลูกหนี้ NOVAT (Credit)' }, // AR Payment 'SP': { side: 'AR', vat: null, desc: 'เลขตัดชำระลูกหนี้' }, // AP VAT 'PR': { side: 'AP', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'ซื้อเชื่อ' }, 'PS': { side: 'AP', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'ซื้อสด/โอน' }, 'PC': { side: 'AP', vat: 'VAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้เจ้าหนี้' }, // AP NOVAT 'MR': { side: 'AP', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ซื้อเชื่อ NOVAT' }, 'MS': { side: 'AP', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ซื้อสด/โอน NOVAT' }, 'BR': { side: 'AP', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้เจ้าหนี้ NOVAT' }, 'BS': { side: 'AP', vat: 'NOVAT', desc: 'ใบลดหนี้เจ้าหนี้ NOVAT (Sale)' }, // AP Other 'PX': { side: 'AP', vat: 'MIXED', desc: 'เอกสารอื่นๆ' }, // AP Payment 'PP': { side: 'AP', vat: null, desc: 'เลขตัดชำระเจ้าหนี้' }, }; // Priority order: ตัวที่ยาวกว่าต้องเช็คก่อน (แต่ทั้งหมด 2 ตัวอักษร) → sort ชัดๆ ตามหมวด const BILL_TYPE_PREFIXES = Object.keys(BILL_TYPE_META); // ── Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────── function extractBillType(billNo) { if (!billNo) return ''; const str = String(billNo).toUpperCase(); for (const t of BILL_TYPE_PREFIXES) { if (str.startsWith(t)) return t; } return ''; } // สำหรับ Revenue Department export — เอาเฉพาะ VAT bills ของ AP function isVatApDoc(billType) { const meta = BILL_TYPE_META[billType]; return meta && meta.side === 'AP' && (meta.vat === 'VAT' || meta.vat === 'MIXED'); } // สำหรับ Revenue Department export — เอาเฉพาะ VAT bills ของ AR function isVatArDoc(billType) { const meta = BILL_TYPE_META[billType]; return meta && meta.side === 'AR' && meta.vat === 'VAT'; } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Export สำหรับสรรพากร — PP Report (เจ้าหนี้) และ SP Report (ลูกหนี้) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Business Rules: // 1. Filter เฉพาะ VAT docs // - PP Export: PS, PR, PX, PC (AP VAT) // - SP Export: SS, SR, SC (AR VAT) // 2. Renumber สด: PPYYYYMM-#### / SPYYYYMM-#### // - รันต่อเนื่อง (ไม่ reset ทุกเดือน) — prefix + seq ต่อเนื่องในเดือน // 3. เก็บเลขเดิมไว้ใน pp_no_original / sp_no_original (ไม่แสดงในเลขหลัก) // 4. Tree: PP แม่ → bills ลูกที่เป็น VAT เท่านั้น // ── Export PP Report (เจ้าหนี้) สำหรับยื่น ภ.พ.30 ── function exportPPReportForTax(req) { var month = req.month || ''; // 'YYYYMM' — รอบที่ต้องการ export if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(month)) { return jsonError('ต้องระบุ month เป็น YYYYMM เช่น "202603"'); } // อ่าน pp_payments (parent + children) + pp_buy_bills var payments = getSheet('pp_payments') ? sheetToObjects(getSheet('pp_payments')) : []; var buyBills = getSheet('pp_buy_bills') ? sheetToObjects(getSheet('pp_buy_bills')) : []; var supplierPayments = getSheet('supplier_payments') ? sheetToObjects(getSheet('supplier_payments')) : []; // ── 1. Group: แต่ละ PP แม่ (pp_no ไม่ว่าง) + children (rows ที่ไม่มี pp_no แต่มี bill_no ต่อ) var ppMap = {}; // original pp_no → { parent, children } var currentParent = null; payments.forEach(function(r) { var cleanPpNo = String(r.pp_no || '').replace(/^'/, ''); if (cleanPpNo) { ppMap[cleanPpNo] = { parent: r, children: [] }; currentParent = cleanPpNo; } else if (r.bill_no && currentParent) { // Child row (ไม่มี pp_no แต่มี bill_no) ppMap[currentParent].children.push(r); } }); // ── 2.
