Filter PP ที่อยู่ในเดือน req.month (ใช้ transfer_date หรือ supplier_payment.transfer_date) ── // ดึง transfer_date จาก supplier_payments (เพราะ pp_payments อาจเก็บ date ต่างกัน) var spByPpNo = {}; supplierPayments.forEach(function(p) { var pn = String(p.pp_no || '').replace(/^'/, ''); if (pn) spByPpNo[pn] = p; }); function extractMonthFromDate(d) { if (!d) return ''; var s = String(d).trim(); // dd/mm/yyyy var m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[3]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return String(y) + String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0'); } // yyyy-mm-dd m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[1]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return String(y) + m[2]; } return ''; } var filteredPPs = []; Object.keys(ppMap).forEach(function(origPpNo) { var entry = ppMap[origPpNo]; // หา transfer_date จาก supplier_payments ก่อน (ถ้ามี) → fallback ไปใช้ parent.date var sp = spByPpNo[origPpNo]; var refDate = (sp && sp.transfer_date) || entry.parent.date || ''; var dateMonth = extractMonthFromDate(refDate); if (dateMonth === month) { // Filter children: เอาเฉพาะ VAT AP docs (PS, PR, PC, PX) var vatChildren = entry.children.filter(function(c) { var type = extractBillType(c.bill_no); return isVatApDoc(type); }); // ถ้า parent เป็น PP และมี VAT children — include if (vatChildren.length > 0 || !entry.children.length) { filteredPPs.push({ parent: entry.parent, children: vatChildren, ref_date: refDate, supplier_payment: sp || null, orig_pp_no: origPpNo, }); } } }); // ── 3. Sort by ref_date + orig_pp_no เพื่อให้ stable ── filteredPPs.sort(function(a, b) { // convert dd/mm/yyyy → YYYYMMDD function toKey(d) { var s = String(d).trim(); var m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[3]); if (y>2400) y-=543; return String(y)+String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')+String(m[1]).padStart(2,'0'); } m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[1]); if (y>2400) y-=543; return String(y)+m[2]+m[3]; } return s; } var aK = toKey(a.ref_date) + a.orig_pp_no; var bK = toKey(b.ref_date) + b.orig_pp_no; return aK < bK ? -1 : aK > bK ? 1 : 0; }); // ── 4. Renumber: PP{YYYYMM}-#### ต่อเนื่อง ── var output = filteredPPs.map(function(entry, i) { var newPpNo = 'PP' + month + '-' + String(i + 1).padStart(4, '0'); // หา bill info จาก pp_buy_bills ของ VAT children var enrichedChildren = entry.children.map(function(c) { var cleanBillNo = String(c.bill_no || '').replace(/^'/, ''); var buyBill = buyBills.find(function(b) { return String(b.inv_no || '').replace(/^'/, '') === cleanBillNo || String(b.doc_no || '').replace(/^'/, '') === cleanBillNo; }); return { bill_no: cleanBillNo, bill_type: extractBillType(cleanBillNo), vat: c.bill_vat || (buyBill ? buyBill.vat : 'VAT'), bill_date: c.bill_date || (buyBill ? buyBill.bill_date : ''), due_date: c.bill_due || '', amount: Number(c.bill_amount) || 0, net: Number(c.bill_net) || 0, inv_no: c.bill_inv_no || cleanBillNo, supplier_tax_id: buyBill ? (buyBill.tax_id || '') : '', }; }); // ข้อมูลสำหรับ print (col A-E) // พิจารณาใช้ bill ตัวแรกเป็น "เลขที่บิลใบกำกับภาษี" หลัก var firstBill = enrichedChildren[0] || {}; return { pp_no: newPpNo, // ← เลขใหม่สำหรับ print pp_no_original: entry.orig_pp_no, // ← เลขเดิม (เก็บไว้ reference) ref_date: entry.ref_date, supplier_bill_date: firstBill.bill_date || '', // col B supplier_invoice_no: firstBill.inv_no || '', // col C supplier_name: entry.parent.supplier_name || '', supplier_code: String(entry.parent.supplier_code || '').replace(/^'/, ''), supplier_tax_id: firstBill.supplier_tax_id || '', amount: Number(entry.parent.amount) || 0, discount: Number(entry.parent.discount) || 0, net: Number(entry.parent.net) || 0, bills: enrichedChildren, // ← bills ลูก (VAT only) }; }); return jsonOk({ month: month, total_pp: output.length, rows: output, }); } // ── Export SP Report (ลูกหนี้) — same logic but for SP ── function exportSPReportForTax(req) { var month = req.month || ''; if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(month)) { return jsonError('ต้องระบุ month เป็น YYYYMM เช่น "202603"'); } var payments = getSheet('sp_payments') ? sheetToObjects(getSheet('sp_payments')) : []; // Parse bills_json payments.forEach(function(p) { try { p._bills = p.bills_json ? JSON.parse(p.bills_json) : []; } catch (e) { p._bills = []; } }); function extractMonthFromDate(d) { if (!d) return ''; var s = String(d).trim(); var m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[3]); if (y>2400) y-=543; return String(y)+String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0'); } m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[1]); if (y>2400) y-=543; return String(y)+m[2]; } return ''; } // Filter by month (use payment date) var filtered = payments.filter(function(p) { return extractMonthFromDate(p.date) === month; }); // Filter bills: เอาเฉพาะ VAT AR docs (SS, SR, SC) filtered = filtered.map(function(p) { var vatBills = (p._bills || []).filter(function(b) { var type = extractBillType(b.bill_no || b.bill_number || ''); return isVatArDoc(type); }); return { orig: p, vatBills: vatBills, }; }).filter(function(e) { return e.vatBills.length > 0 || !(e.orig._bills && e.orig._bills.length); }); // Sort by date + orig sp_no filtered.sort(function(a, b) { function toKey(d) { var s = String(d).trim(); var m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[3]); if (y>2400) y-=543; return String(y)+String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')+String(m[1]).padStart(2,'0'); } return s; } return toKey(a.orig.date).localeCompare(toKey(b.orig.date)); }); // Renumber var output = filtered.map(function(entry, i) { var newSpNo = 'SP' + month + '-' + String(i + 1).padStart(4, '0'); var p = entry.orig; var firstBill = entry.vatBills[0] || {}; return { sp_no: newSpNo, sp_no_original: String(p.sp_no || '').replace(/^'/, ''), pay_date: p.date || '', customer_bill_date: firstBill.bill_date || firstBill.date || '', customer_invoice_no: firstBill.bill_no || firstBill.invoice_no || '', customer_name: p.cust_name || '', cust_id: String(p.cust_id || '').replace(/^'/, ''), amount: Number(p.total) || 0, bills: entry.vatBills.map(function(b) { return { bill_no: b.bill_no || '', bill_type: extractBillType(b.bill_no || ''), bill_date: b.bill_date || b.date || '', amount: Number(b.amount) || 0, }; }), }; }); return jsonOk({ month: month, total_sp: output.length, rows: output, }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Matching Engine // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const MATCH_LOG_HEADERS = ['log_id', 'action', 'stmt_date', 'stmt_id', 'stmt_bank', 'stmt_ref', 'stmt_amount', 'old_station', 'old_bill', 'new_station', 'new_bill', 'action_by', 'action_at', 'reason']; // ── Auto Match ────────────────────────────────────────────── // จับคู่อัตโนมัติโดยเทียบ ยอดเงิน + ชื่อลูกค้า/คำอธิบาย function autoMatch(req) { const date = req.date || todayKey(); const actionBy = req.action_by || 'System'; // หา daily sheet (ลองหลายชื่อ) const dailyNames = [getDailySheet(date), 'Main', 'daily_' + date, 'Daily']; var dailySheet = null; for (var i = 0; i < dailyNames.length; i++) { dailySheet = getSheet(dailyNames[i]); if (dailySheet) break; } // หา statement sheets (ลองหลายชื่อ) const stmtNames = [getStmtSheet(date), 'stmt_KTB_' + formatDateKey(date), 'stmt_KBANK_' + formatDateKey(date), 'stmt_BBL_' + formatDateKey(date), 'KTB', 'Kasikorn', 'BBL NW']; var stmtSheets = []; for (var i = 0; i < stmtNames.length; i++) { var s = getSheet(stmtNames[i]); if (s) stmtSheets.push({ name: stmtNames[i], sheet: s }); } if (!dailySheet) { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var allNames = ss.getSheets().map(function(s) { return s.getName(); }); return jsonError('ไม่พบ Daily sheet — ลองหา: ' + dailyNames.join(', ') + ' | มี sheets: ' + allNames.join(', ')); } if (stmtSheets.length === 0) { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var allNames = ss.getSheets().map(function(s) { return s.getName(); }); return jsonError('ไม่พบ Statement sheet — ลองหา: ' + stmtNames.join(', ') + ' | มี sheets: ' + allNames.join(', ')); } // อ่านข้อมูล daily (รองรับทั้ง field เก่าและใหม่) var sales = sheetToObjects(dailySheet); // ── Year-based: filter เฉพาะวันที่ขอ match (ไม่งั้น match ข้ามวันได้) ── var targetDateDisplay = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(date)); sales = sales.filter(function(s) { var sDate = String(s.date || s['Bill Date'] || '').trim(); return sDate === targetDateDisplay || sDate === ''; // รับ row ที่ไม่มี date ด้วย (บิลเก่า) }); // รวม statement จากทุก sheet ที่เจอ + filter by date var allStmts = []; stmtSheets.forEach(function(ss) { var stmts = sheetToObjects(ss.sheet); // filter by date stmts = stmts.filter(function(s) { return String(s.date || '').trim() === targetDateDisplay; }); stmts.forEach(function(s) { s._sheet_name = ss.name; // Normalize field names if (!s.amount && s.Amount) s.amount = s.Amount; if (!s.bank) { if (ss.name.includes('KTB')) s.bank = 'KTB'; else if (ss.name.includes('Kasikorn') || ss.name.includes('KBANK')) s.bank = 'KBANK'; else if (ss.name.includes('BBL')) s.bank = 'BBL'; } }); allStmts = allStmts.concat(stmts); }); // Normalize sales fields var pendingSales = sales.filter(function(s) { var type = s.pay_method || s.Type || s.type || ''; var status = s.match_status || s['match_status'] || ''; return type.toLowerCase() === 'transfer' && status !== 'paid'; }); var unmatchedStmts = allStmts.filter(function(s) { var status = s.match_status || s['match_status'] || ''; return status !== 'paid'; }); var matched = 0; var results = []; unmatchedStmts.forEach(function(stmt) { var stmtAmount = Number(stmt.amount || stmt.Amount || stmt['ฝากเงิน'] || stmt['เข้าบัญชี'] || 0); if (!stmtAmount || stmtAmount <= 0) return; var match = null; for (var i = 0; i < pendingSales.length; i++) { var sale = pendingSales[i]; if (sale._matched) continue; var saleAmount = Number(sale.amount || sale.Baht || sale['Baht'] || 0); if (Math.abs(saleAmount - stmtAmount) < 0.01) { match = sale; break; } } if (match) { match._matched = true; matched++; results.push({ stmt_ref: stmt.ref_no || stmt['Description'] || stmt['รายละเอียด'] || '', bill_no: match.bill_no || match['Bill Number'] || '', amount: stmtAmount, station: match.station || match['Station'] || '', bank: stmt.bank || '', }); } }); // บันทึก log results.forEach(function(r) { writeMatchLog({ action: 'auto_match', stmt_date: formatDateDisplay(date), stmt_id: '', stmt_bank: r.bank, stmt_ref: r.stmt_ref, stmt_amount: r.amount, old_station: '', old_bill: '', new_station: r.station, new_bill: r.bill_no, action_by: actionBy, reason: 'Auto match by amount (' + r.amount + ')', }); }); return jsonOk({ date: date, daily_sheet: dailySheet.getName(), stmt_sheets: stmtSheets.map(function(s) { return s.name; }), total_sales_transfer: pendingSales.length, total_stmts_unmatched: unmatchedStmts.length, matched: matched, unmatched: unmatchedStmts.length - matched, results: results, }); } // ── Manual Match ──────────────────────────────────────────── function manualMatch(req) { const { date, stmt_id, station, bill_no, action_by } = req; if (!date || !stmt_id || !station || !bill_no) return jsonError('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ (date, stmt_id, station, bill_no)'); const stmtSheet = getSheet(getStmtSheet(date)); const dailySheet = getSheet(getDailySheet(date)); if (!stmtSheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Statement sheet'); // ดึงข้อมูล statement const stmts = sheetToObjects(stmtSheet); const stmt = stmts.find(s => String(s.stmt_id) === String(stmt_id)); if (!stmt) return jsonError('ไม่พบ stmt_id: ' + stmt_id); // อัปเดต statement updateStmtMatch(stmtSheet, stmt_id, { match_status: 'paid', match_station: station, match_bill: bill_no, match_sp: req.sp_no || '', matched_by: action_by || 'Admin', matched_at: nowDisplay(), notes: '', }); // อัปเดต daily (ถ้าเจอ bill_no) if (dailySheet) { const sales = sheetToObjects(dailySheet); const sale = sales.find(s => s.bill_no === bill_no); if (sale) { updateDailyMatch(dailySheet, sale.row_id, { match_status: 'paid', match_bank: stmt.bank, match_date: stmt.date, match_ref: stmt.ref_no, }); } } // บันทึก log writeMatchLog({ action: 'manual_match', stmt_date: stmt.date, stmt_id, stmt_bank: stmt.bank, stmt_ref: stmt.ref_no, stmt_amount: Number(stmt.amount), old_station: '', old_bill: '', new_station: station, new_bill: bill_no, action_by: action_by || 'Admin', reason: req.reason || 'Manual match', }); return jsonOk({ stmt_id, station, bill_no, matched: true }); } // ── Unmatch ───────────────────────────────────────────────── function unmatchEntry(req) { const { date, stmt_id, action_by, reason } = req; if (!date || !stmt_id) return jsonError('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ'); const stmtSheet = getSheet(getStmtSheet(date)); if (!stmtSheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Statement sheet'); const stmts = sheetToObjects(stmtSheet); const stmt = stmts.find(s => String(s.stmt_id) === String(stmt_id)); if (!stmt) return jsonError('ไม่พบ stmt_id: ' + stmt_id); const oldStation = stmt.match_station; const oldBill = stmt.match_bill; // ยกเลิก match ใน statement updateStmtMatch(stmtSheet, stmt_id, { match_status: 'unpaid', match_station: '', match_bill: '', match_sp: '', matched_by: '', matched_at: '', notes: '⚠️ Unmatched โดย ' + (action_by || 'Admin'), }); // ยกเลิก match ใน daily (ถ้ามี) if (oldBill) { const dailySheet = getSheet(getDailySheet(date)); if (dailySheet) { const sales = sheetToObjects(dailySheet); const sale = sales.find(s => s.bill_no === oldBill); if (sale) { updateDailyMatch(dailySheet, sale.row_id, { match_status: 'pending', match_bank: '', match_date: '', match_ref: '', }); } } } // บันทึก log (เก็บข้อมูลเดิมไว้) writeMatchLog({ action: 'unmatch', stmt_date: stmt.date, stmt_id, stmt_bank: stmt.bank, stmt_ref: stmt.ref_no, stmt_amount: Number(stmt.amount), old_station: oldStation, old_bill: oldBill, new_station: '', new_bill: '', action_by: action_by || 'Admin', reason: reason || 'Unmatch', }); return jsonOk({ stmt_id, unmatched: true, old_station: oldStation, old_bill: oldBill }); } // ── Re-match ──────────────────────────────────────────────── // Unmatch เดิม + Match ใหม่ ในคำสั่งเดียว ไม่กระทบข้อมูลเก่า (เก็บ log ไว้) function rematch(req) { const { date, stmt_id, new_station, new_bill, action_by, reason } = req; if (!date || !stmt_id || !new_station || !new_bill) return jsonError('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ'); const stmtSheet = getSheet(getStmtSheet(date)); if (!stmtSheet) return jsonError('ไม่พบ Statement sheet'); const stmts = sheetToObjects(stmtSheet); const stmt = stmts.find(s => String(s.stmt_id) === String(stmt_id)); if (!stmt) return jsonError('ไม่พบ stmt_id: ' + stmt_id); const oldStation = stmt.match_station; const oldBill = stmt.match_bill; // Unmatch bill เดิมใน daily if (oldBill) { const dailySheet = getSheet(getDailySheet(date)); if (dailySheet) { const sales = sheetToObjects(dailySheet); const oldSale = sales.find(s => s.bill_no === oldBill); if (oldSale) { updateDailyMatch(dailySheet, oldSale.row_id, { match_status: 'pending', match_bank: '', match_date: '', match_ref: '', }); } } } // Match bill ใหม่ updateStmtMatch(stmtSheet, stmt_id, { match_status: 'paid', match_station: new_station, match_bill: new_bill, match_sp: req.sp_no || '', matched_by: action_by || 'Admin', matched_at: nowDisplay(), notes: '', }); // Match daily ใหม่ const dailySheet = getSheet(getDailySheet(date)); if (dailySheet) { const sales = sheetToObjects(dailySheet); const newSale = sales.find(s => s.bill_no === new_bill); if (newSale) { updateDailyMatch(dailySheet, newSale.row_id, { match_status: 'paid', match_bank: stmt.bank, match_date: stmt.date, match_ref: stmt.ref_no, }); } } // บันทึก log writeMatchLog({ action: 're_match', stmt_date: stmt.date, stmt_id, stmt_bank: stmt.bank, stmt_ref: stmt.ref_no, stmt_amount: Number(stmt.amount), old_station: oldStation, old_bill: oldBill, new_station, new_bill, action_by: action_by || 'Admin', reason: reason || 'Re-match จาก ' + oldBill + ' เป็น ' + new_bill, }); return jsonOk({ stmt_id, old: { station: oldStation, bill: oldBill }, new: { station: new_station, bill: new_bill } }); } // ── Match Log ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function getMatchLog(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.MATCH_LOG); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); let data = sheetToObjects(sheet); if (req.date) data = data.filter(d => d.stmt_date === req.date || d.stmt_date === formatDateDisplay(new Date(req.date))); if (req.stmt_id) data = data.filter(d => String(d.stmt_id) === String(req.stmt_id)); data.reverse(); // ล่าสุดก่อน if (req.limit) data = data.slice(0, Number(req.limit)); return jsonOk(data); } function writeMatchLog(entry) { entry.log_id = generateId('L'); entry.action_at = nowDisplay(); appendRow(SHEETS.MATCH_LOG, entry, MATCH_LOG_HEADERS); } // ── Sheet Update Helpers ──────────────────────────────────── function updateDailyMatch(sheet, rowId, updates) { const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('row_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (String(data[r][idCol]) === String(rowId)) { Object.keys(updates).forEach(field => { const col = headers.indexOf(field); if (col !== -1) sheet.getRange(r + 1, col + 1).setValue(updates[field]); }); // แถบแดงสำหรับ unmatched if (updates.match_status === 'unpaid') { sheet.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#3d1a1a').setFontColor('#f87171'); } else if (updates.match_status === 'paid') { sheet.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground(null).setFontColor(null); } return; } } } function updateStmtMatch(sheet, stmtId, updates) { const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('stmt_id'); for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (String(data[r][idCol]) === String(stmtId)) { Object.keys(updates).forEach(field => { const col = headers.indexOf(field); if (col !== -1) sheet.getRange(r + 1, col + 1).setValue(updates[field]); }); // แถบแดงสำหรับ unmatched if (updates.match_status === 'unpaid') { sheet.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#3d1a1a').setFontColor('#f87171'); } else if (updates.match_status === 'paid') { sheet.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground(null).setFontColor(null); } return; } } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Monthly / Yearly Summary // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const MONTHLY_HEADERS = ['date', 'total_bills', 'total_sales', 'total_returns', 'net_sales', 'cash', 'transfer', 'cheque', 'credit_card', 'qr_payment', 'sp_count', 'pp_count', 'matched_count', 'unmatched_count', 'match_rate']; function generateMonthly(req) { const month = req.month; // format: YYYYMM หรือ YYYY-MM if (!month) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุเดือน'); const yearMonth = month.replace('-', ''); const year = yearMonth.substring(0, 4); const mon = yearMonth.substring(4, 6); const daysInMonth = new Date(Number(year), Number(mon), 0).getDate(); const sheetName = 'monthly_' + yearMonth; const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, MONTHLY_HEADERS); // ล้างข้อมูลเก่า (ถ้ามี) แล้วสร้างใหม่ if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) { sheet.deleteRows(2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1); } // Year-based: อ่าน bills_YYYY ครั้งเดียว แล้ว group by date const yearSheet = getSheet('bills_' + year); if (!yearSheet) { return jsonOk({ month: yearMonth, sheet_name: sheetName, days: 0, rows: [], note: 'ไม่พบ bills_' + year }); } const allYearSales = sheetToObjects(yearSheet); // Group ตามวันในเดือนนี้ const salesByDay = {}; const monPrefix = String(mon).padStart(2, '0') + '/' + year; allYearSales.forEach(function(s) { // s.date คือ dd/mm/yyyy var d = String(s.date || ''); if (!d.endsWith(monPrefix)) return; var day = d.substring(0, 2); if (!salesByDay[day]) salesByDay[day] = []; salesByDay[day].push(s); }); const summaryRows = []; const batchRows = []; for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) { const dayKey = String(d).padStart(2, '0'); const sales = salesByDay[dayKey] || []; if (sales.length === 0) continue; const dateDisplay = dayKey + '/' + mon + '/' + year; const totalSales = sales.filter(s => Number(s.amount) > 0).reduce((a, s) => a + Number(s.amount), 0); const totalReturns = sales.filter(s => Number(s.amount) < 0).reduce((a, s) => a + Number(s.amount), 0); const net = totalSales + totalReturns; const byMethod = (method) => sales.filter(s => s.pay_method === method).reduce((a, s) => a + Number(s.amount), 0); const spCount = sales.filter(s => s.sp_no && s.sp_no !== '').length; const ppCount = sales.filter(s => s.pp_no && s.pp_no !== '').length; const matchedCount = sales.filter(s => s.match_status === 'paid').length; const unmatchedCount = sales.filter(s => s.match_status === 'unpaid').length; const matchRate = sales.length > 0 ? Math.round(matchedCount / sales.length * 100) + '%' : '0%'; batchRows.push([ dateDisplay, sales.length, totalSales, totalReturns, net, byMethod('Cash'), byMethod('Transfer'), byMethod('Cheque'), byMethod('Credit Card'), byMethod('QR Payment'), spCount, ppCount, matchedCount, unmatchedCount, matchRate, ]); summaryRows.push({ date: dateDisplay, bills: sales.length, net_sales: net }); } // Batch write if (batchRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, batchRows.length, MONTHLY_HEADERS.length).setValues(batchRows); } // ตั้งค่า format ตัวเลข if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) { const numRange = sheet.getRange(2, 3, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 8); numRange.setNumberFormat('#,##0.00'); } return jsonOk({ month: yearMonth, sheet_name: sheetName, days: summaryRows.length, rows: summaryRows }); } function getMonthlySummary(req) { const month = req.month; if (!month) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุเดือน'); const yearMonth = month.replace('-', ''); const sheet = getSheet('monthly_' + yearMonth); if (!sheet) return jsonOk({ month: yearMonth, data: [] }); return jsonOk({ month: yearMonth, data: sheetToObjects(sheet) }); } function generateYearly(req) { const year = req.year; if (!year) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุปี'); const sheetName = 'yearly_' + year; const yearlyHeaders = ['month', 'total_bills', 'total_sales', 'total_returns', 'net_sales', 'sp_count', 'pp_count', 'matched_count', 'unmatched_count', 'match_rate']; const sheet = getOrCreateSheet(sheetName, yearlyHeaders); if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) { sheet.deleteRows(2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1); } for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) { const monthKey = year + String(m).padStart(2, '0'); const monthSheet = getSheet('monthly_' + monthKey); if (!monthSheet) continue; const data = sheetToObjects(monthSheet); if (data.length === 0) continue; const totalBills = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.total_bills || 0), 0); const totalSales = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.total_sales || 0), 0); const totalReturns = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.total_returns || 0), 0); const netSales = totalSales + totalReturns; const spCount = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.sp_count || 0), 0); const ppCount = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.pp_count || 0), 0); const matchedCount = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.matched_count || 0), 0); const unmatchedCount = data.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.unmatched_count || 0), 0); const matchRate = totalBills > 0 ? Math.round(matchedCount / totalBills * 100) + '%' : '0%'; const monthDisplay = String(m).padStart(2, '0') + '/' + year; sheet.appendRow([monthDisplay, totalBills, totalSales, totalReturns, netSales, spCount, ppCount, matchedCount, unmatchedCount, matchRate]); } return jsonOk({ year, sheet_name: sheetName }); } function getYearlySummary(req) { const year = req.year; if (!year) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุปี'); const sheet = getSheet('yearly_' + year); if (!sheet) return jsonOk({ year, data: [] }); return jsonOk({ year, data: sheetToObjects(sheet) }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Reports — Matched Archive สำหรับส่งสำนักงานบัญชี // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const REPORT_ARCHIVE_HEADERS = ['report_id', 'report_type', 'date_from', 'date_to', 'bank_filter', 'station_filter', 'total_records', 'total_amount', 'generated_by', 'generated_at', 'sent_to', 'sent_at', 'file_name']; // Internal helper — ดึง matched data โดยไม่ wrap ContentService function _getMatchedData(req) { const { date_from, date_to, bank, station } = req; const startDate = parseDate(date_from); const endDate = parseDate(date_to); if (!startDate || !endDate) return { error: 'รูปแบบวันที่ไม่ถูกต้อง' }; // Year-based: อ่าน stmts_YYYY ของปีในช่วง แล้ว filter const startYear = startDate.getFullYear(); const endYear = endDate.getFullYear(); const results = []; // Pre-compute target date range as dd/mm/yyyy for string comparison const dateInRange = function(dStr) { if (!dStr) return false; var parts = String(dStr).split('/'); if (parts.length !== 3) return false; var d = new Date(Number(parts[2]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[0])); return d >= startDate && d <= endDate; }; for (var y = startYear; y <= endYear; y++) { var stmtSheet = getSheet('stmts_' + y); if (!stmtSheet) continue; var stmts = sheetToObjects(stmtSheet); stmts.forEach(function(s) { if (s.match_status !== 'paid') return; if (!dateInRange(s.date)) return; if (bank && bank !== 'ALL' && s.bank !== bank) return; if (station && station !== 'ALL' && s.match_station !== station) return; results.push({ date: s.date, bank: s.bank, ref: s.ref_no, amount: Number(s.amount), description: s.description, station: s.match_station, bill: s.match_bill, sp_no: s.match_sp, matched_by: s.matched_by, matched_at: s.matched_at, }); }); } const totalAmount = results.reduce((a, r) => a + r.amount, 0); const byBank = {}; results.forEach(r => { if (!byBank[r.bank]) byBank[r.bank] = { count: 0, total: 0 }; byBank[r.bank].count++; byBank[r.bank].total += r.amount; }); return { total_records: results.length, total_amount: totalAmount, by_bank: byBank, data: results }; } function getMatchedArchive(req) { const { date_from, date_to } = req; if (!date_from || !date_to) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุช่วงวันที่'); const result = _getMatchedData(req); if (result.error) return jsonError(result.error); return jsonOk({ date_from, date_to, ...result }); } function generateReport(req) { const { date_from, date_to, bank, station, generated_by, sent_to } = req; if (!date_from || !date_to) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุช่วงวันที่'); const matchedData = _getMatchedData(req); if (matchedData.error) return jsonError(matchedData.error); const report = { report_id: generateId('RPT'), report_type: req.report_type || 'matched', date_from, date_to, bank_filter: bank || 'ALL', station_filter: station || 'ALL', total_records: matchedData.total_records, total_amount: matchedData.total_amount, generated_by: generated_by || 'Admin', generated_at: nowDisplay(), sent_to: sent_to || '', sent_at: '', file_name: 'matched_' + date_from.replace(/\//g, '') + '_' + date_to.replace(/\//g, '') + '.xlsx', }; appendRow(SHEETS.REPORT_ARCHIVE, report, REPORT_ARCHIVE_HEADERS); return jsonOk({ report_id: report.report_id, file_name: report.file_name, total_records: report.total_records, total_amount: report.total_amount, }); } function getReportList(req) { const sheet = getSheet(SHEETS.REPORT_ARCHIVE); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); let data = sheetToObjects(sheet); data.reverse(); if (req.limit) data = data.slice(0, Number(req.limit)); return jsonOk(data); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Dashboard // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function getDashboard(req) { const date = req.date || todayKey(); const targetDateDisplay = formatDateDisplay(parseDateKey(date)); // ยอดหนี้คงค้าง const arSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.AR_LEDGER); const arData = arSheet ? sheetToObjects(arSheet) : []; const totalDebt = arData.reduce((a, d) => a + (Number(d.total_outstanding) || 0), 0); const overdueCount = arData.filter(d => d.credit_status === 'overdue' || d.credit_status === 'credit_break').length; // ยอดขายวันนี้ — year sheet → filter เฉพาะวันนี้ const dailySheet = getSheet(getDailySheet(date)); const allBills = dailySheet ? sheetToObjects(dailySheet) : []; const dailyData = allBills.filter(d => String(d.date || '').trim() === targetDateDisplay); const todaySales = dailyData.reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.amount), 0); // Station summary const byStation = {}; dailyData.forEach(d => { if (!byStation[d.station]) byStation[d.station] = 0; byStation[d.station] += Number(d.amount); }); // Statement matching — year sheet → filter เฉพาะวันนี้ const stmtSheet = getSheet(getStmtSheet(date)); const allStmts = stmtSheet ? sheetToObjects(stmtSheet) : []; const stmtData = allStmts.filter(s => String(s.date || '').trim() === targetDateDisplay); const matchedStmts = stmtData.filter(s => s.match_status === 'paid').length; const totalStmts = stmtData.length; // Recent alerts const alertSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.ALERTS_LOG); const alerts = alertSheet ? sheetToObjects(alertSheet).reverse().slice(0, 5) : []; return jsonOk({ date: formatDateDisplay(date), debt: { total: totalDebt, debtors: arData.length, overdue: overdueCount }, sales: { total: todaySales, count: dailyData.length, by_station: byStation }, matching: { matched: matchedStmts, total: totalStmts }, recent_alerts: alerts, }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Config Data (สำหรับ Frontend ดึง dropdown) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function getConfigData(req) { const configSheet = getSheet(SHEETS.CONFIG); // Return static config ถ้าไม่มี sheet const stations = [ { id: 'FRONT1', name: 'POS Station 1', type: 'pos' }, { id: 'FRONT2', name: 'POS Station 2', type: 'pos' }, { id: 'FRONT3', name: 'POS Station 3', type: 'pos' }, { id: 'FRONT4', name: 'POS Station 4', type: 'pos' }, { id: 'SS', name: 'Invoice', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'SR', name: 'Invoice Credit', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'OS', name: 'Order', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'OR', name: 'Order Credit', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'JS', name: 'Order CN', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'JR', name: 'Order Credit CN', type: 'smart' }, { id: 'SC', name: 'Invoice + Credit CN', type: 'smart' }, ]; const banks = [ { id: 'KTB', name: 'ธนาคารกรุงไทย', color: '#00a4e4' }, { id: 'KBANK', name: 'ธนาคารกสิกรไทย', color: '#138f2d' }, { id: 'BBL', name: 'ธนาคารกรุงเทพ', color: '#1e3a7b' }, ]; const payMethods = ['Cash', 'Transfer', 'Cheque', 'Credit Card', 'QR Payment']; const docTypes = [ { id: 'SP', name: 'เลขตัดชำระลูกหนี้' }, { id: 'PP', name: 'เลขตัดชำระเจ้าหนี้' }, ]; return jsonOk({ stations, banks, payMethods, docTypes }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Triggers (ตั้ง Time-driven trigger) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // เรียกทุกวันตอน 08:00 เพื่อตรวจสอบลูกหนี้ function dailyAlertCheck() { checkAlerts({}); } // ตั้ง trigger ครั้งแรก (รันฟังก์ชันนี้ครั้งเดียว) function setupTriggers() { // ลบ trigger เดิม ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(t => ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t)); // ตั้ง trigger ตรวจสอบลูกหนี้ทุกวัน 08:00 ScriptApp.newTrigger('dailyAlertCheck') .timeBased() .atHour(8) .everyDays(1) .inTimezone(CONFIG.TIMEZONE) .create(); Logger.log('Triggers setup complete'); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Initial Setup (รันครั้งแรกเพื่อสร้าง Sheets ทั้งหมด) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // 🔧 MIGRATION — รันเมื่อ deploy backend ใหม่ // เพิ่มคอลัมน์ใหม่ใน sheet ที่มีอยู่ (cheque fields etc.) // วิธีใช้: ใน Apps Script Editor เลือก migrateSchemaV2 → กด Run // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function migrateSchemaV2() { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var results = []; function migrate(name, newHeaders) { var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sheet) { results.push('⚠ ไม่พบ sheet: ' + name + ' (ข้าม)'); return; } var existing = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1)).getValues()[0]; var existingSet = {}; existing.forEach(function(h) { if (h) existingSet[String(h).trim()] = true; }); var missing = newHeaders.filter(function(h) { return !existingSet[h]; }); if (missing.length === 0) { results.push('✓ ' + name + ': schema ล่าสุดแล้ว (' + existing.filter(function(h){return h;}).length + ' คอลัมน์)'); return; } var startCol = existing.length + 1; if (existing.filter(function(h){return h;}).length === 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, newHeaders.length).setValues([newHeaders]); } else { sheet.getRange(1, startCol, 1, missing.length).setValues([missing]); } sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()) .setFontWeight('bold') .setBackground('#1a1d27') .setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); if (sheet.getFrozenRows() === 0) sheet.setFrozenRows(1); results.push('✅ ' + name + ': เพิ่ม ' + missing.length + ' คอลัมน์ — ' + missing.join(', ')); } // Migrate all schemas to latest migrate('supplier_payments', SUPPLIER_PAY_HEADERS); migrate('transfer_log_vat', TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS); migrate('transfer_log_novat', TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS); migrate('transfer_master_vat', TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS); migrate('transfer_master_novat', TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS); migrate('transfer_checks', TRANSFER_CHECKS_HEADERS); migrate('cashflow', CASHFLOW_HEADERS); migrate('suppliers', SUPPLIER_HEADERS); migrate('pp_main', PP_MAIN_HEADERS); migrate('pp_payments', PP_PAYMENTS_HEADERS); migrate('pp_payment_types', PP_PAYMENT_TYPES_HEADERS); migrate('pp_buy_bills', PP_BUY_BILLS_HEADERS); var msg = '╔═══ Schema Migration Results ═══╗\n' + results.join('\n') + '\n╚════════════════════════════════╝'; Logger.log(msg); // Show toast in spreadsheet try { ss.toast('Migration สำเร็จ — ดูรายละเอียดใน Log (View → Logs)', 'Schema Migration', 10); } catch(e) {} return msg; } function getSupplierPayments(req) { var sheet = getSheet('supplier_payments'); if (!sheet) return jsonOk([]); var data = sheetToObjects(sheet); if (req.status) data = data.filter(function(p) { return p.status === req.status; }); if (req.vat) data = data.filter(function(p) { return p.vat === req.vat; }); if (req.supplier_id) data = data.filter(function(p) { return p.supplier_id === req.supplier_id; }); return jsonOk(data); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // PP Report — 3 sheets + Main (รวม) // pp_payments: PP parent + children // pp_payment_types: วิธีการชำระ // pp_buy_bills: ภาษีซื้อ // pp_main: Main รวม (export) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const PP_PAYMENTS_HEADERS = ['pp_no', 'date', 'supplier_code', 'supplier_name', 'amount', 'discount', 'net', 'bill_no', 'bill_vat', 'bill_date', 'bill_due', 'bill_note', 'bill_amount', 'bill_net', 'bill_inv_no']; const PP_PAYMENT_TYPES_HEADERS = ['pp_no', 'date', 'supplier_code', 'supplier_name', 'method', 'method_date', 'bank', 'amount']; const PP_BUY_BILLS_HEADERS = ['no', 'bill_date', 'inv_no', 'doc_no', 'vat', 'supplier_code', 'supplier_name', 'tax_id', 'branch', 'amount', 'vat_amount', 'other', 'total']; const PP_MAIN_HEADERS = ['#', 'vat', 'pp_no', 'date', 'supplier_code', 'supplier_name', 'amount', 'discount', 'net', 'cash_date', 'cash_amount', 'transfer_date', 'transfer_bank', 'transfer_amount', 'cheque_date', 'cheque_bank', 'cheque_amount']; function savePPReport(req) { var r = req.data || {}; // ── Helper: clear + batch write ── function writeSheet(sheet, headers, rows) { var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow > 1) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow-1, headers.length).clearContent(); } if (rows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, headers.length).setValues(rows); } } // ── pp_payments ── var shPay = getOrCreateSheet('pp_payments', PP_PAYMENTS_HEADERS); var payRows = []; (r.payments || []).forEach(function(pp) { payRows.push(["'"+(pp.pp_no||''), pp.date||'', "'"+(pp.supplier_code||''), pp.supplier_name||'', Number(pp.amount)||0, Number(pp.discount)||0, Number(pp.net)||0, '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '']); (pp.bills || []).forEach(function(b) { payRows.push(['', '', '', '', '', '', '', "'"+(b.bill_no||''), b.vat||'', b.bill_date||'', b.due_date||'', b.note||'', Number(b.amount)||0, Number(b.net)||0, b.inv_no||'']); }); }); writeSheet(shPay, PP_PAYMENTS_HEADERS, payRows); shPay.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); shPay.getRange('C:C').setNumberFormat('@'); shPay.getRange('H:H').setNumberFormat('@'); // ── pp_payment_types ── var shType = getOrCreateSheet('pp_payment_types', PP_PAYMENT_TYPES_HEADERS); }); }); writeSheet(shType, PP_PAYMENT_TYPES_HEADERS, typeRows); shType.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); shType.getRange('C:C').setNumberFormat('@'); // ── pp_buy_bills ── var shBuy = getOrCreateSheet('pp_buy_bills', PP_BUY_BILLS_HEADERS); var buyRows = (r.buyBills || []).map(function(b) { return [Number(b.no)||0, b.bill_date||'', "'"+(b.inv_no||''), "'"+(b.doc_no||''), b.vat||'', "'"+(b.supplier_code||''), b.supplier_name||'', "'"+(b.tax_id||''), b.branch||'', Number(b.amount)||0, Number(b.vat_amount)||0, Number(b.other)||0, Number(b.total)||0]; }); writeSheet(shMain, PP_MAIN_HEADERS, mainRows); shMain.getRange('C:C').setNumberFormat('@'); shMain.getRange('E:E').setNumberFormat('@'); return jsonOk({ payments: (r.payments || []).length, paymentTypes: (r.paymentTypes || []).length, buyBills: (r.buyBills || []).length, merged: (r.merged || []).length, period: r.period || '', }); } function getPPReport(req) { var shMain = getSheet('pp_main'); return jsonOk({ main: shMain ? sheetToObjects(shMain) : [], payments_count: getSheet('pp_payments') ? getSheet('pp_payments').getLastRow() - 1 : 0, types_count: getSheet('pp_payment_types') ? getSheet('pp_payment_types').getLastRow() - 1 : 0, buybills_count: getSheet('pp_buy_bills') ? getSheet('pp_buy_bills').getLastRow() - 1 : 0, }); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Transfer Log — 2026_รายการโอนเงิน_JKN.xlsx // transfer_log_vat: Vat sheet (รายการโอน VAT) // transfer_log_novat: NoVat sheet (รายการโอน NOVAT) // transfer_master_vat: DropdownVat (Master Supplier VAT) // transfer_master_novat: DropdownNoVat (Master Supplier NOVAT) // transfer_checks: ตรวจ YYYY-MM (flattened) // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS = ['id','seq','transfer_date','category','nickname','supplier_name','supplier_bank','supplier_account','invoice_no','receipt_no','amount_billed','deduct_shipping','deduct_claim','deduct_other','deduct_total','amount','from_bank','vat','pp_no','match_status','stmt_ref']; const TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS = ['id','seq','nickname','name','bank','account_no','note','vat']; const TRANSFER_CHECKS_HEADERS = ['month','transfer_date','nickname','supplier_name','invoice_no','amount_paid','from_bank','diff','pp_no','pp_ref','receive_date','amount_pp','note']; function saveTransferLog(req) { var d = req.data || {}; // Helper: accept both new field names (from JSON import) and old ones function normalizeTransfer(t) { return { id: t.id || '', // seq ← accept 'order' (from JSON) or 'seq' seq: Number(t.seq || t.order) || 0, transfer_date: t.transfer_date || '', category: t.category || '', nickname: t.nickname || '', supplier_name: t.supplier_name || '', supplier_bank: t.supplier_bank || '', supplier_account: t.supplier_account || '', invoice_no: t.invoice_no || '', receipt_no: t.receipt_no || '', // amount_billed ← old name was amount_billed, new JSON uses amount (ยอดแจ้ง) amount_billed: Number(t.amount_billed != null ? t.amount_billed : t.amount) || 0, deduct_shipping: Number(t.deduct_shipping) || 0, deduct_claim: Number(t.deduct_claim) || 0, deduct_other: Number(t.deduct_other) || 0, deduct_total: Number(t.deduct_total) || 0, // amount (ยอดโอนจริง) ← new JSON uses transfer_amount, old uses amount amount: Number(t.transfer_amount != null ? t.transfer_amount : t.amount) || 0, from_bank: t.from_bank || '', vat: t.vat || 'VAT', pp_no: t.pp_no || '', match_status: t.match_status || 'pending', // stmt_ref ← accept 'match_stmt_ref' stmt_ref: t.stmt_ref || t.match_stmt_ref || '', }; } function normalizeSupplier(s, defaultVat) { return { id: s.id || '', seq: Number(s.seq || s.order) || 0, nickname: s.nickname || '', // name ← accept 'name' or 'account_name' name: s.name || s.account_name || '', bank: s.bank || '', account_no: s.account_no || '', note: s.note || '', vat: s.vat || defaultVat, }; } function normalizeCheck(c) { return { transfer_date: c.transfer_date || '', nickname: c.nickname || '', supplier_name: c.supplier_name || '', invoice_no: c.invoice_no || '', amount_paid: Number(c.amount_paid) || 0, from_bank: c.from_bank || '', diff: Number(c.diff) || 0, pp_no: c.pp_no || '', // pp_ref ← new JSON uses 'doc_sale', old uses 'pp_ref' pp_ref: c.pp_ref || c.doc_sale || '', receive_date: c.receive_date || '', amount_pp: Number(c.amount_pp) || 0, note: c.note || '', }; } // ── Helper: clear + batch write ── function writeSheetBatch(sheet, headers, rows) { var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow > 1) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow-1, headers.length).clearContent(); } if (rows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, headers.length).setValues(rows); } } // ── VAT transfers ── var shV = getOrCreateSheet('transfer_log_vat', TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS); var vatRows = (d.vatTransfers || []).map(function(raw) { var t = normalizeTransfer(raw); }); writeSheetBatch(shV, TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS, vatRows); shV.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); shV.getRange('H:J').setNumberFormat('@'); shV.getRange('S:S').setNumberFormat('@'); shV.getRange('U:U').setNumberFormat('@'); // ── NOVAT transfers ── var shN = getOrCreateSheet('transfer_log_novat', TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS); var novatRows = (d.novatTransfers || []).map(function(raw) { var t = normalizeTransfer(raw); if (!t.vat || t.vat === 'VAT') t.vat = 'NOVAT'; return ["'"+t.id, t.seq, t.transfer_date, t.category, t.nickname, t.supplier_name, t.supplier_bank, "'"+t.supplier_account, "'"+t.invoice_no, "'"+t.receipt_no, t.amount_billed, t.deduct_shipping, t.deduct_claim, t.deduct_other, t.deduct_total, t.amount, t.from_bank, t.vat, "'"+t.pp_no, t.match_status, "'"+t.stmt_ref]; }); writeSheetBatch(shN, TRANSFER_LOG_HEADERS, novatRows); shN.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); shN.getRange('H:J').setNumberFormat('@'); shN.getRange('S:S').setNumberFormat('@'); shN.getRange('U:U').setNumberFormat('@'); // ── Master Supplier VAT ── var shMV = getOrCreateSheet('transfer_master_vat', TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS); var mvRows = (d.vatSuppliers || []).map(function(raw) { var s = normalizeSupplier(raw, 'VAT'); return ["'"+s.id, s.seq, s.nickname, s.name, s.bank, "'"+s.account_no, s.note, s.vat]; }); writeSheetBatch(shMV, TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS, mvRows); shMV.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); shMV.getRange('F:F').setNumberFormat('@'); // ── Master Supplier NOVAT ── var shMN = getOrCreateSheet('transfer_master_novat', TRANSFER_MASTER_HEADERS); var mnRows = (d.novatSuppliers || []).map(function(raw) { var s = normalizeSupplier(raw, 'NOVAT'); // ── Monthly checks (flatten) ── var shC = getOrCreateSheet('transfer_checks', TRANSFER_CHECKS_HEADERS); var checkRows = []; Object.keys(d.monthlyChecks || {}).forEach(function(month) { (d.monthlyChecks[month] || []).forEach(function(raw) { var c = normalizeCheck(raw); shC.getRange('E:E').setNumberFormat('@'); shC.getRange('I:I').setNumberFormat('@'); var total = (d.vatTransfers||[]).length + (d.novatTransfers||[]).length; return jsonOk({ total: total, vatTransfers: (d.vatTransfers||[]).length, novatTransfers: (d.novatTransfers||[]).length, vatSuppliers: (d.vatSuppliers||[]).length, novatSuppliers: (d.novatSuppliers||[]).length, checkMonths: Object.keys(d.monthlyChecks||{}).length, }); } function getTransferLog(req) { var shV = getSheet('transfer_log_vat'); var shN = getSheet('transfer_log_novat'); var shMV = getSheet('transfer_master_vat'); var shMN = getSheet('transfer_master_novat'); var shC = getSheet('transfer_checks'); var monthlyChecks = {}; if (shC) { var checks = sheetToObjects(shC); checks.forEach(function(c) { if (!monthlyChecks[c.month]) monthlyChecks[c.month] = []; monthlyChecks[c.month].push(c); }); } return jsonOk({ vatTransfers: shV ? sheetToObjects(shV) : [], novatTransfers: shN ? sheetToObjects(shN) : [], vatSuppliers: shMV ? sheetToObjects(shMV) : [], novatSuppliers: shMN ? sheetToObjects(shMN) : [], monthlyChecks: monthlyChecks, }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SHEET HEALTH TOOLS // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const REQUIRED_SHEETS_FIXED = [ { name: 'users' }, { name: 'customers' }, { name: 'suppliers' }, { name: 'credit_rules' }, { name: 'ar_ledger' }, { name: 'alerts_log' }, { name: 'match_log' }, { name: 'report_archive' }, { name: 'sp_payments' }, { name: 'supplier_payments' }, { name: 'cashflow' }, { name: 'payments_log' }, { name: 'bill_status_overrides' }, ]; const DYNAMIC_SHEET_PATTERNS = ['bills_', 'stmts_', 'meta_']; // Web API: ให้ frontend เรียกเช็คผ่าน action=listSheets function listSheets(req) { const ss = getSpreadsheet(); const all = ss.getSheets().map(function(s) { return { name: s.getName(), rows: s.getLastRow(), cols: s.getLastColumn() }; }); const existingNames = {}; all.forEach(function(s) { existingNames[s.name] = true; }); const requiredFixed = REQUIRED_SHEETS_FIXED.map(function(s) { return s.name; }); const missing = requiredFixed.filter(function(n) { return !existingNames[n]; }); const dynamic = all.filter(function(s) { return DYNAMIC_SHEET_PATTERNS.some(function(p) { return s.name.indexOf(p) === 0; }); }).sort(function(a,b) { return a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1; }); const fixedStatus = REQUIRED_SHEETS_FIXED.map(function(s) { const found = all.filter(function(a) { return a.name === s.name; })[0]; return { name: s.name, exists: !!found, rows: found ? found.rows : 0 }; }); const extras = all.filter(function(s) { if (requiredFixed.indexOf(s.name) >= 0) return false; if (DYNAMIC_SHEET_PATTERNS.some(function(p) { return s.name.indexOf(p) === 0; })) return false; return true; }); return jsonOk({ spreadsheet_id: CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID, total_sheets: all.length, fixed: fixedStatus, dynamic: dynamic, extras: extras, missing: missing, }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // UUID-BASED SYNC MODULE — v1.0 // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // วิธีติดตั้ง (3 ขั้นตอน): // 1. ใน Apps Script → กดปุ่ม "+" ข้าง Files → เลือก "Script" // 2. ตั้งชื่อไฟล์ว่า "uuid_sync" (ไม่ต้องใส่ .gs) // 3. Paste ไฟล์นี้ทั้งหมด → Save → Deploy (ดูขั้นตอน 4 ด้านล่าง) // // ขั้นตอน 4: แก้ไข code.gs เดิม — เพิ่ม 7 cases ใน handleRequest // หาบรรทัดที่มี: // case 'listSheets': return listSheets(req); // แล้วเพิ่มหลังจากบรรทัดนั้น: // // // ═══ UUID-based sync (v1.0) ═══ // case 'prepareSheet': return prepareSheet(req); // case 'upsertBills': return upsertBills(req); // case 'upsertStatements': return upsertStatements(req); // case 'clearSheetData': return clearSheetData(req); // case 'getBillsWithUuid': return getBillsWithUuid(req); // case 'getStatementsWithUuid': return getStatementsWithUuid(req); // case 'verifySheetCount': return verifySheetCount(req); // // ขั้นตอน 5: Deploy → Manage deployments → Edit → New version → Deploy // // ขั้นตอน 6: ทดสอบโดยรัน function TEST_uuid_sync_basic (ดูใน Executions log) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Module นี้ใช้ helpers จาก code.gs เดิม (ไม่ต้องประกาศซ้ำ): // - getSpreadsheet() → เปิด Sheet ปัจจุบันตาม CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID // - jsonOk(data) → wrap success response // - jsonError(msg, code) → wrap error response // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Helpers ภายใน — fallback ถ้าไม่เจอ function จาก code.gs // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function _uuidOk(data) { if (typeof jsonOk === 'function') return jsonOk(data); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: true, data: data })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _uuidErr(msg, code) { if (typeof jsonError === 'function') return jsonError(msg, code || 400); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: msg, code: code || 400 })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _uuidSS() { if (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') return getSpreadsheet(); if (typeof CONFIG !== 'undefined' && CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID) { return SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); } throw new Error('Cannot find spreadsheet — getSpreadsheet() or CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID not defined'); } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 1: prepareSheet // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // เตรียม Sheet ให้พร้อมสำหรับ UUID-based sync // - ถ้า column A ยังไม่ใช่ "bill_uuid"/"stmt_uuid" → insert column A // - ถ้า column A เป็น uuid อยู่แล้ว → skip // // Payload: { action: 'prepareSheet', kind: 'bills'|'stmts', year: '2026' } // Response: { success: true, data: { added: true|false, headers: [...] } } function prepareSheet(p) { try { var kind = p.kind; var year = String(p.year); if (['bills', 'stmts', 'meta'].indexOf(kind) < 0) { return _uuidErr('Invalid kind: ' + kind + ' (ต้องเป็น bills, stmts, หรือ meta)'); } var sheetName = kind + '_' + year; var uuidCol; if (kind === 'bills') uuidCol = 'bill_uuid'; else if (kind === 'stmts') uuidCol = 'stmt_uuid'; else uuidCol = 'meta_uuid'; var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetName); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); // สำหรับ meta ตั้ง header ครบเลย if (kind === 'meta') { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([[uuidCol, 'date', 'meta_type', 'data_json', 'updated_at']]); } sheet.setFrozenRows(1); return _uuidOk({ added: true, headers: kind === 'meta' ? [uuidCol, 'date', 'meta_type', 'data_json', 'updated_at'] : [uuidCol], created: true }); } var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) { if (kind === 'meta') { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([[uuidCol, 'date', 'meta_type', 'data_json', 'updated_at']]); return _uuidOk({ added: true, headers: [uuidCol, 'date', 'meta_type', 'data_json', 'updated_at'] }); } sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); return _uuidOk({ added: true, headers: [uuidCol] }); } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; if (String(headers[0]).trim() === uuidCol) { return _uuidOk({ added: false, headers: headers }); } // Insert column A + set header name sheet.insertColumnBefore(1); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); var newHeaders = [uuidCol].concat(headers); return _uuidOk({ added: true, headers: newHeaders }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // COLUMN ↔ FIELD TRANSLATION MAPS // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Sheet column (header) → Frontend field (in JS bill object) // ใช้ใน _upsertGeneric (push) และ _getWithUuid (pull) // สำคัญ: bills_2026 sheet มี header บาง column ที่ไม่ตรงกับ field name ใน frontend // เช่น sheet header = 'match_bank' แต่ frontend field = 'confirmation' // ถ้าไม่แปลง → push จะเขียน '' ทับ (data loss), pull จะได้ field ชื่อผิด var BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD = { // Aliases (legacy column names → current field names) 'bill_type': 'type', 'pay_method': 'type', // mirror ของ type 'bill_date': 'date', 'cust_id': 'customer_code', // ⚠ ถูกลบแล้ว — keep mapping ไว้เผื่อ sheet เก่าตกค้าง 'sp_no': 'paid_by_sp', // ⚠ ถูกลบแล้ว — keep mapping 'match_bank': 'confirmation', // ⚠️ REMOVED: 'match_date': 'pay_date' — caused ghost pay_date from stale legacy column // User ลบ pay_date ใน sheet → match_date ค้าง → alias ดึงกลับมาทับ pay_date // Fix: drop alias + drop column ผ่าน BILLS_DROP_LIST 'match_ref': 'matched_stmt_ref', // ⭐ paid_at sheet column → matched_at frontend field // หมายเหตุ: paid_by, paid_bills, match_method, match_reason ถูกลบจาก bills_YYYY // (เป็น stmt fields ไม่ใช่ bill — ยังคงอยู่ใน stmts__YYYY) 'paid_at': 'matched_at', }; // Stmts sheet: ไม่มี legacy columns (header ตรงกับ frontend field อยู่แล้ว) → map ว่าง var STMTS_COL_TO_FIELD = { // ⭐ paid_* sheet columns → matched_* frontend fields (จากการ rename matched→paid) 'paid_at': 'matched_at', 'paid_by': 'matched_by', 'paid_bills': 'matched_bills', }; // Columns ที่ควรถูกลบจาก stmts_YYYY — cross-checked กับ frontend แล้ว var STMTS_DROP_LIST = [ 'stmt_id', // demo-only (line 10695 ใน index.html) — frontend จริงไม่ใช้ 'balance', // 0 hits 'notes', // 0 hits บน statement ]; // Columns ที่ frontend เขียนแต่ sheet อาจยังไม่มี → append ให้ var STMTS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS = [ 'match_method', // 'quick_multi' | 'auto_sameday' | 'manual_diff' | ... 'match_reason', // audit text 'matched_bills', // array of bill_no สำหรับ multi-bill match (สำคัญสำหรับ unmatch) 'overpay_amount', // + = ลูกค้าโอนเกิน (คืนเงินสด), − = โอนขาด — ใช้เมื่อ match ยอดต่าง // ── Sender metadata (parsed from description ตอน import) ── 'sender_bank_code', // รหัสธนาคารผู้โอน (ถ้า parse ได้) 'sender_bank_name', // ชื่อธนาคารผู้โอน 'sender_account', // เลขบัญชีผู้โอน 'sender_name', // ชื่อผู้โอน — ใช้ match ชื่อลูกค้าในอนาคต (SP auto-match) ]; // Desired column order — sender_* block อยู่หลัง description ก่อน match info var STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER = [ 'stmt_uuid', // system key (col 1) // UI display 'bank', 'date', 'time', 'ref_no', 'description', 'amount', // Sender metadata (parsed from description) 'sender_bank_code', 'sender_bank_name', 'sender_account', 'sender_name', // Match info 'match_status', 'match_station', 'match_bill', 'matched_bills', // multi-bill 'match_sp', // SP auto-match target 'match_method', 'match_reason', 'overpay_amount', // ยอดต่าง (+ over / − under) 'matched_at', 'matched_by', ]; // แปลง sheet column name → frontend field name function _colToField(kind, colName) { var map = (kind === 'bills') ? BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD : (kind === 'stmts') ? STMTS_COL_TO_FIELD : null; if (map && map[colName]) return map[colName]; return colName; } // Columns ที่ frontend ใช้แต่ sheet bills_2026 อาจยังไม่มี → ใช้ใน ensureBillsColumns() // Columns ที่ frontend อาจไม่ส่งครบ แต่ Sheet ควรมี (auto-add ตอน finalizeBillsSchema) // ใช้ Sheet column names (ไม่ใช่ frontend field) เพื่อให้ตรงกับ BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER var BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS = [ 'remarks2', 'bank', 'source', 'created_at', ]; // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 2 & 3: upsertBills / upsertStatements // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Upsert: UPDATE ถ้า uuid มีอยู่, INSERT ถ้าไม่มี + DELETE by uuid list // // Payload: // { action: 'upsertBills', year: '2026', // records: [{bill_uuid:'...', bill_no:'...', amount:100, ...}], // deletes: ['uuid1', 'uuid2'] } // Response: // { success: true, data: { updated: N, inserted: N, deleted: N, skipped: N } } function upsertBills(p) { return _upsertGeneric(p, 'bills', 'bill_uuid'); } function upsertStatements(p) { return _upsertGeneric(p, 'stmts', 'stmt_uuid'); } function upsertMeta(p) { return _upsertGeneric(p, 'meta', 'meta_uuid'); } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: deleteMetaByDateRange // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ลบ rows ใน meta_YYYY ที่ date อยู่นอกช่วง [from, to] // ใช้ลบ "ghost rows" — ข้อมูลที่ไม่ตรง business reality // Idempotent — keep rows in [from, to], remove อื่นๆ // Payload: { action: 'deleteMetaByDateRange', year: 2026, keep_from: '2026-01-01', keep_to: '2026-04-24', dryRun?: true } // - keep_from: lower bound (inclusive) // - keep_to: upper bound (inclusive) — ลบทุก row ที่ date > keep_to // - lock rows (meta_type='locks') ไม่ถูกลบ (date เป็น year anchor ไม่ใช่ business date) function deleteMetaByDateRange(req) { try { var dryRun = !!(req && req.dryRun); var year = String(req.year || '').trim(); var keepFrom = String(req.keep_from || '').trim(); var keepTo = String(req.keep_to || '').trim(); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); if (!keepFrom && !keepTo) return _uuidErr('Need at least keep_from or keep_to'); var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var sheetName = 'meta_' + year; var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ deleted: 0, kept: 0, message: 'empty sheet' }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var dateColIdx = headers.indexOf('date'); var typeColIdx = headers.indexOf('meta_type'); if (dateColIdx < 0) return _uuidErr('No "date" column found'); var keepRows = []; var deleteRowsByDate = []; // { date, meta_type } — for report data.forEach(function(row) { // Skip empty if (!row[0]) return; var dateRaw = row[dateColIdx]; var dateStr = ''; if (dateRaw instanceof Date && !isNaN(dateRaw)) { dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(dateRaw, CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } else if (typeof dateRaw === 'string') { dateStr = dateRaw.trim().substring(0, 10); // Convert dd/mm/yyyy → yyyy-mm-dd for comparison var m = dateStr.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[3]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; dateStr = y + '-' + m[2].padStart(2, '0') + '-' + m[1].padStart(2, '0'); } } var metaType = typeColIdx >= 0 ? String(row[typeColIdx] || '') : ''; // ⭐ Lock rows preserved — date เป็น year anchor (e.g., 2026-01-01) ไม่ใช่ business date if (metaType === 'locks') { keepRows.push(row); return; } // Check range var inRange = true; if (keepFrom && dateStr < keepFrom) inRange = false; if (keepTo && dateStr > keepTo) inRange = false; if (inRange) { keepRows.push(row); } else { deleteRowsByDate.push({ date: dateStr, meta_type: metaType }); } }); // Group deletes by month for report var byMonth = {}; deleteRowsByDate.forEach(function(r) { var m = (r.date || 'unknown').substring(0, 7); byMonth[m] = (byMonth[m] || 0) + 1; }); var report = { dry_run: dryRun, sheet: sheetName, keep_from: keepFrom, keep_to: keepTo, kept: keepRows.length, to_delete: deleteRowsByDate.length, delete_by_month: byMonth, }; if (!dryRun && deleteRowsByDate.length > 0) { // Backup before destructive op var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var backupName = (sheetName + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100); try { var backup = sheet.copyTo(ss); backup.setName(backupName); report.backup = backupName; } catch (e) { report.backup_error = e.message; } // Strategy: clear all data rows, write back keepRows sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).clearContent(); if (keepRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, keepRows.length, lastCol).setValues(keepRows); } report.deleted = deleteRowsByDate.length; } return _uuidOk(report); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: dropBillsColumns // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ลบ dead/unused columns ใน bills_YYYY sheet // 10 cols ที่ไม่มี data จริง: sp_date, note, note2, created_by, row_id, net_amount, // pp_no, source_file, imported_from, imported_at // Idempotent — รันซ้ำไม่ error // Payload: { action: 'dropBillsColumns', year: 2026, dryRun?: true } var BILLS_COLS_TO_DROP = [ 'sp_date', // 0% data 'note', // 0% data — frontend ใช้ remarks1 'note2', // 0% data — frontend ใช้ remarks2 'created_by', // 0% data 'row_id', // 0% data 'net_amount', // 0% data — calc on-the-fly 'pp_no', // 0% data 'source_file', // 0% data 'imported_from', // 0% data 'imported_at', // 0% data ]; function dropBillsColumns(req) { try { var dryRun = !!(req && req.dryRun); var year = String(req.year || new Date().getFullYear()).trim(); var sheetName = 'bills_' + year; var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol < 1) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); // Find indices to drop (1-indexed for sheet API) var dropList = []; BILLS_COLS_TO_DROP.forEach(function(c) { var idx = headers.indexOf(c); if (idx >= 0) dropList.push({ col: c, idx: idx + 1 }); // 1-indexed }); var report = { dry_run: dryRun, sheet: sheetName, cols_before: headers.length, to_drop: dropList.map(function(x) { return x.col; }), not_found: BILLS_COLS_TO_DROP.filter(function(c) { return headers.indexOf(c) < 0; }), }; // Safety check: verify no data in cols (paranoid) if (lastRow >= 2 && dropList.length > 0) { var dataInCols = {}; dropList.forEach(function(x) { var col = sheet.getRange(2, x.idx, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); var nonEmpty = 0; col.forEach(function(r) { if (r[0] !== null && r[0] !== undefined && String(r[0]).trim() !== '') nonEmpty++; }); dataInCols[x.col] = nonEmpty; }); report.non_empty_check = dataInCols; // Abort if any col has data var hasData = Object.keys(dataInCols).some(function(k) { return dataInCols[k] > 0; }); if (hasData) { report.aborted = 'some columns have data — refusing to delete'; return _uuidOk(report); } } if (!dryRun && dropList.length > 0) { // Backup var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var backupName = (sheetName + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100); try { var backup = sheet.copyTo(ss); backup.setName(backupName); report.backup = backupName; } catch (e) { report.backup_error = e.message; } // Sort indices descending — delete from right to left to keep indices stable dropList.sort(function(a, b) { return b.idx - a.idx; }); dropList.forEach(function(x) { sheet.deleteColumn(x.idx); }); report.dropped = dropList.length; report.cols_after = sheet.getLastColumn(); } return _uuidOk(report); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } function TEST_dropBillsColumns_dryRun() { var r = dropBillsColumns({ year: 2026, dryRun: true }); Logger.log(r.getContent()); } function TEST_dropBillsColumns_apply() { var r = dropBillsColumns({ year: 2026, dryRun: false }); Logger.log(r.getContent()); } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: fixMatchStatus // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Rename match_status values across all bills/stmts sheets: // 'matched' → 'paid' // 'unmatched' → 'unpaid' // Idempotent — safe to run multiple times // Payload: { action: 'fixMatchStatus', dryRun?: true } function fixMatchStatus(req) { try { var dryRun = !!(req && req.dryRun); var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var report = { dry_run: dryRun, sheets: {} }; var allSheets = ss.getSheets(); // Target sheets: bills_YYYY, stmts_YYYY, stmts__YYYY, payments(*) var pattern = /^(bills_\d{4}|stmts_\d{4}|stmts_[A-Z_]+_\d{4})$/; var targets = allSheets.filter(function(sh) { return pattern.test(sh.getName()); }); targets.forEach(function(sheet) { var sheetName = sheet.getName(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 1) { report.sheets[sheetName] = { skipped: 'empty' }; return; } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var msIdx = headers.indexOf('match_status'); if (msIdx < 0) { report.sheets[sheetName] = { skipped: 'no match_status column' }; return; } // Read whole column at once var colData = sheet.getRange(2, msIdx + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); var matchedToPaid = 0; var unmatchedToUnpaid = 0; var newCol = colData.map(function(r) { var v = String(r[0] || '').trim(); if (v === 'matched') { matchedToPaid++; return ['paid']; } if (v === 'unmatched') { unmatchedToUnpaid++; return ['unpaid']; } return r; // unchanged }); report.sheets[sheetName] = { col: 'match_status (col ' + (msIdx + 1) + ')', matched_to_paid: matchedToPaid, unmatched_to_unpaid: unmatchedToUnpaid, rows: lastRow - 1, }; if (!dryRun && (matchedToPaid + unmatchedToUnpaid) > 0) { // Backup var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var backupName = (sheetName + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100); try { var backup = sheet.copyTo(ss); backup.setName(backupName); report.sheets[sheetName].backup = backupName; } catch (e) { report.sheets[sheetName].backup_error = e.message; } // Write back sheet.getRange(2, msIdx + 1, newCol.length, 1).setValues(newCol); } }); return _uuidOk(report); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } function TEST_fixMatchStatus_dryRun() { var r = fixMatchStatus({ dryRun: true }); Logger.log(r.getContent()); } function TEST_fixMatchStatus_apply() { var r = fixMatchStatus({ dryRun: false }); Logger.log(r.getContent()); } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: fixMetaDates // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Normalize meta_YYYY sheets: // - Date objects in cells → ISO string (yyyy-MM-dd or yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss) // - พ.ศ. format (27/4/2569) → ค.ศ. ISO (2026-04-27T11:47:11+07:00) // - dd/mm/yyyy → yyyy-mm-dd // Idempotent — safe to run multiple times // Payload: { action: 'fixMetaDates', dryRun?: true, year?: 2026 } function fixMetaDates(req) { try { var dryRun = !!(req && req.dryRun); var targetYear = req && req.year ? String(req.year) : ''; var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var report = { dry_run: dryRun, sheets: {} }; var sheets = ss.getSheets(); var metaSheets = []; sheets.forEach(function(sh) { var n = sh.getName(); if (/^meta_\d{4}$/.test(n)) { if (!targetYear || n === 'meta_' + targetYear) { metaSheets.push(sh); } } }); metaSheets.forEach(function(sheet) { var sheetName = sheet.getName(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 1) { report.sheets[sheetName] = { skipped: 'empty' }; return; } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var dateColIdx = headers.indexOf('date'); var updatedAtIdx = headers.indexOf('updated_at'); var createdAtIdx = headers.indexOf('created_at'); var changes = { date: 0, updated_at: 0, created_at: 0, total_rows: data.length }; var newData = data.map(function(row) { var newRow = row.slice(); // Fix date col → yyyy-MM-dd if (dateColIdx >= 0) { var v = row[dateColIdx]; if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) { newRow[dateColIdx] = _formatDateForSheet(v, 'date'); changes.date++; } else if (typeof v === 'string') { var fixed = _normalizeDateString(v, 'date'); if (fixed !== v) { newRow[dateColIdx] = fixed; changes.date++; } } } // Fix updated_at → ISO datetime if (updatedAtIdx >= 0) { var u = row[updatedAtIdx]; if (u instanceof Date && !isNaN(u)) { newRow[updatedAtIdx] = _formatDateForSheet(u, 'updated_at'); changes.updated_at++; } else if (typeof u === 'string') { var fu = _normalizeDateString(u, 'updated_at'); if (fu !== u) { newRow[updatedAtIdx] = fu; changes.updated_at++; } } } // Fix created_at → ISO datetime if (createdAtIdx >= 0) { var c = row[createdAtIdx]; if (c instanceof Date && !isNaN(c)) { newRow[createdAtIdx] = _formatDateForSheet(c, 'created_at'); changes.created_at++; } else if (typeof c === 'string') { var fc = _normalizeDateString(c, 'created_at'); if (fc !== c) { newRow[createdAtIdx] = fc; changes.created_at++; } } } return newRow; }); report.sheets[sheetName] = changes; if (!dryRun && (changes.date + changes.updated_at + changes.created_at) > 0) { // Backup var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var backupName = (sheetName + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100); try { var backup = sheet.copyTo(ss); backup.setName(backupName); changes.backup = backupName; } catch (e) { changes.backup_error = e.message; } // Write back sheet.getRange(2, 1, newData.length, lastCol).setValues(newData); // Force plain text format on date columns if (dateColIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(2, dateColIdx + 1, newData.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); if (updatedAtIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(2, updatedAtIdx + 1, newData.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); if (createdAtIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(2, createdAtIdx + 1, newData.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } }); return _uuidOk(report); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // DATE HELPERS — ใช้ใน _upsertGeneric เพื่อกัน พ.ศ. format // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // _formatDateForSheet: Date object → string เก็บลง Sheet // - date column → 'yyyy-MM-dd' (ISO date only) // - updated_at, created_at, *_at column → 'yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ssZ' (ISO datetime) // - อื่นๆ → ISO datetime function _formatDateForSheet(d, colName) { if (!(d instanceof Date) || isNaN(d)) return String(d || ''); var tz = (typeof CONFIG !== 'undefined' && CONFIG.TIMEZONE) ? CONFIG.TIMEZONE : 'Asia/Bangkok'; var col = String(colName || '').toLowerCase(); if (col === 'date' || col === 'transfer_date' || col === 'pay_date' || col === 'due_date' || col === 'trans_date') { return Utilities.formatDate(d, tz, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } // datetime — keep timezone offset for clarity return Utilities.formatDate(d, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); } // _normalizeDateString: string → string (normalize ก่อนเขียน Sheet) // - พ.ศ. dd/mm/yyyy (yyyy > 2400) → ค.ศ. yyyy-mm-dd // - dd/mm/yyyy (yyyy < 2400) → yyyy-mm-dd // - yyyy-mm-dd → ผ่านตรง // - อื่นๆ → ผ่านตรง function _normalizeDateString(s, colName) { if (!s) return s; var col = String(colName || '').toLowerCase(); // เฉพาะ column ที่เป็นวันที่ var isDateCol = (col === 'date' || col === 'transfer_date' || col === 'pay_date' || col === 'due_date' || col === 'trans_date' || col.indexOf('_at') === col.length - 3); if (!isDateCol) return s; s = String(s).trim(); if (!s) return s; // ISO yyyy-mm-dd already → check ปี พ.ศ. var iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (iso) { var y = Number(iso[1]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return y + '-' + iso[2] + '-' + iso[3] + s.substring(10); } // dd/mm/yyyy or dd/mm/yy var m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{2,4})/); if (m) { var dd = String(m[1]).padStart(2, '0'); var mm = String(m[2]).padStart(2, '0'); var yy = Number(m[3]); if (yy < 100) yy += 2000; if (yy > 2400) yy -= 543; // พ.ศ. → ค.ศ. var rest = s.substring(m[0].length); // " 11:47:11" return yy + '-' + mm + '-' + dd + rest; } return s; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // MIGRATION: Fix Date Columns (Datetime → Text ISO String) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Run ครั้งเดียวใน Apps Script Editor — แก้ Date object ใน sheet เก่า // ที่อาจถูก auto-coerce + swap day/month (เช่น 2026-04-06 → 2026-06-04) // // Usage (Apps Script Editor): // fixDateColumnsAllSheets() // fix ทั้ง bills + stmts ทุกปี // fixDateColumnsBills('2026') // fix เฉพาะ bills_2026 // fixDateColumnsStmts('2026') // fix เฉพาะ stmts_*_2026 // // What it does: // 1. Force date columns to text format (@) // 2. Convert any Date objects to ISO string 'yyyy-mm-dd' // 3. Detect swap bug (date > 12 in DD position) → cannot auto-fix, just log function fixDateColumnsAllSheets() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var report = []; ['2024','2025','2026','2027'].forEach(function(year) { try { report.push(fixDateColumnsBills(year)); } catch(e) { report.push('bills_'+year+': error '+e.message); } try { report.push(fixDateColumnsStmts(year)); } catch(e) { report.push('stmts_'+year+': error '+e.message); } }); Logger.log(report.join('\n')); return report.join('\n'); } function fixDateColumnsBills(year) { return _fixDateColumnsForSheet('bills_' + year); } function fixDateColumnsStmts(year) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var sheets = ss.getSheets(); var report = []; sheets.forEach(function(sh) { var name = sh.getName(); if (name === 'stmts_' + year) { report.push(_fixDateColumnsForSheet(name)); } else if (name.match(new RegExp('^stmts_.+_' + year + '$'))) { report.push(_fixDateColumnsForSheet(name)); } }); return report.join('\n') || 'stmts_'+year+': no sheets found'; } function _fixDateColumnsForSheet(sheetName) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sh) return sheetName + ': sheet not found'; var lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); var lastRow = sh.getLastRow(); if (lastCol === 0 || lastRow < 2) return sheetName + ': empty sheet'; var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var DATE_COL_NAMES = ['date','transfer_date','pay_date','due_date','trans_date','time']; var tz = (typeof CONFIG !== 'undefined' && CONFIG.TIMEZONE) ? CONFIG.TIMEZONE : 'Asia/Bangkok'; var totalConverted = 0; var totalSuspectSwap = 0; var dateColsFound = []; for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) { var colName = String(headers[c]).toLowerCase().trim(); var isDate = DATE_COL_NAMES.indexOf(colName) >= 0 || colName.indexOf('_at') === colName.length - 3; if (!isDate) continue; dateColsFound.push(colName); // Step 1: Force text format sh.getRange(1, c + 1, sh.getMaxRows(), 1).setNumberFormat('@'); // Step 2: Read column data var rng = sh.getRange(2, c + 1, lastRow - 1, 1); var vals = rng.getValues(); var changed = false; for (var r = 0; r < vals.length; r++) { var v = vals[r][0]; if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) { // Convert to ISO string var isoStr; if (colName.indexOf('_at') === colName.length - 3) { // datetime column isoStr = Utilities.formatDate(v, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); } else { // date column isoStr = Utilities.formatDate(v, tz, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } vals[r][0] = isoStr; totalConverted++; changed = true; // Heuristic: detect swap bug — month > 12 should never happen // (already converted to valid date, so swap already happened — log for awareness) // We can't detect retroactively. Log if `date` column exists for cross-check } } if (changed) rng.setValues(vals); } return sheetName + ': converted ' + totalConverted + ' Date→string in cols [' + dateColsFound.join(',') + ']'; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CLEAN GHOST META ROWS — ลบ rows ที่เกิดจาก day-month swap // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Ghost rows pattern: // uuid='2026-06-01|summary' มี data_json เหมือน '2026-01-06|summary' (swap) // เกิดจาก legacy bug ที่ Sheet เก็บ datetime ใน timezone ผิด → // pull กลับ → frontend ได้ dd/MM/yyyy → parse swap → save back ใหม่ // // Logic: // 1. Group rows by data_json fingerprint // 2. ถ้า fingerprint ซ้ำ → keep row ที่ uuid_date มีบิลใน bills_YYYY (วันที่จริง) // หรือถ้าตัดสินไม่ได้ → keep older updated_at // 3. ลบ duplicates // // Usage: // cleanGhostMetaRows() // ทุกปี — dry run โชว์ที่จะลบ // cleanGhostMetaRows({ apply: true }) // จริง — ลบ rows // cleanGhostMetaRows({ year: '2026' }) // เฉพาะปี function cleanGhostMetaRows(opts) { opts = opts || {}; var apply = opts.apply === true; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var years = opts.year ? [opts.year] : ['2024','2025','2026','2027']; var report = []; years.forEach(function(year) { var sheetName = 'meta_' + year; var sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sh) return; var lastRow = sh.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 4) return; var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var uuidIdx = headers.indexOf('meta_uuid'); var dateIdx = headers.indexOf('date'); var typeIdx = headers.indexOf('meta_type'); var dataIdx = headers.indexOf('data_json'); var updatedIdx = headers.indexOf('updated_at'); if (uuidIdx < 0 || dataIdx < 0) { report.push(sheetName + ': missing required columns'); return; } var rows = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); // Get bills sheet for this year — ใช้เป็น reference ว่าวันไหนมี business activity var billsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); var validBusinessDates = {}; if (billsSheet && billsSheet.getLastRow() > 1) { var bHdrs = billsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, billsSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; var bDateIdx = bHdrs.indexOf('date'); if (bDateIdx >= 0) { var bDates = billsSheet.getRange(2, bDateIdx + 1, billsSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues(); bDates.forEach(function(r) { var v = r[0]; var iso = ''; if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) { iso = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } else if (typeof v === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(v)) { iso = v.substring(0, 10); } if (iso) validBusinessDates[iso] = true; }); } } // Group by data_json fingerprint (only summary + cash_count, not locks) var byFingerprint = {}; var rowsToDelete = []; for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var uuid = String(rows[i][uuidIdx] || '').trim(); if (!uuid) continue; var type = String(rows[i][typeIdx] || '').trim(); if (type !== 'summary' && type !== 'cash_count') continue; var uuidDate = uuid.split('|')[0]; var data = String(rows[i][dataIdx] || ''); var updated = rows[i][updatedIdx]; // Fingerprint = type + data_json (same data = same business day) var fp = type + '||' + data; if (!byFingerprint[fp]) byFingerprint[fp] = []; byFingerprint[fp].push({ rowIdx: i + 2, uuid: uuid, uuidDate: uuidDate, updated: updated, hasBills: !!validBusinessDates[uuidDate], }); } // For each fingerprint group with > 1 row: pick winner, mark losers for deletion var ghostsFound = 0; Object.keys(byFingerprint).forEach(function(fp) { var group = byFingerprint[fp]; if (group.length <= 1) return; // unique → keep // Sort: rows with bills first, then oldest updated_at group.sort(function(a, b) { if (a.hasBills && !b.hasBills) return -1; if (!a.hasBills && b.hasBills) return 1; // Both same on bills — older updated_at wins (probably original) var ua = a.updated instanceof Date ? a.updated.getTime() : 0; var ub = b.updated instanceof Date ? b.updated.getTime() : 0; return ua - ub; }); var winner = group[0]; var losers = group.slice(1); losers.forEach(function(loser) { rowsToDelete.push(loser.rowIdx); ghostsFound++; report.push(' ghost: row ' + loser.rowIdx + ' uuid=' + loser.uuid + ' (winner=' + winner.uuid + (winner.hasBills ? ' [hasBills]' : '') + ')'); }); }); report.push(sheetName + ': found ' + ghostsFound + ' ghost rows'); if (apply && rowsToDelete.length > 0) { // Sort descending → delete bottom-up (preserve indices) rowsToDelete.sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; }); rowsToDelete.forEach(function(rowNum) { sh.deleteRow(rowNum); }); report.push(sheetName + ': DELETED ' + rowsToDelete.length + ' rows'); } else if (rowsToDelete.length > 0) { report.push(sheetName + ': DRY RUN — would delete ' + rowsToDelete.length + ' rows. Pass {apply:true} to execute.'); } }); Logger.log(report.join('\n')); return report.join('\n'); } function _upsertGeneric(p, kind, uuidField) { try { var year = String(p.year); var records = p.records || []; var deletes = p.deletes || []; var sheetName = kind + '_' + year; if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetName); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidField); } var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidField); lastCol = 1; } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0];
