if (String(headers[0]).trim() !== uuidField) { return _uuidErr('Column A = "' + headers[0] + '" แต่ต้องเป็น "' + uuidField + '" — รัน prepareSheet ก่อน'); } var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); // Build uuid → row-number map + pre-load existing values (สำหรับ preserve-on-update) // ⭐ DUPLICATE DETECTION: ถ้าพบ uuid ซ้ำใน sheet → mark row อื่นๆ เป็น "to-delete" // (race condition ใน push เก่าอาจทำให้ append แทน update → uuid ซ้ำ) // เก็บ row แรกสุด (oldest) เพราะมีโอกาสมี data ที่ user แก้แล้วในนั้น var uuidToRow = {}; var existingRows = {}; // row-number → [values...] var duplicateRowsToDelete = []; // rows ที่ uuid ซ้ำกับ row ก่อนหน้า if (lastRow >= 2) { var allData = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, headers.length).getValues(); for (var i = 0; i < allData.length; i++) { var u = String(allData[i][0] || '').trim(); if (u) { if (uuidToRow[u]) { // Duplicate detected — mark this row for deletion duplicateRowsToDelete.push(i + 2); } else { uuidToRow[u] = i + 2; existingRows[i + 2] = allData[i]; } } } } if (duplicateRowsToDelete.length > 0) { Logger.log('[_upsertGeneric] ' + sheetName + ': found ' + duplicateRowsToDelete.length + ' duplicate uuid rows — will be cleaned up'); } var updateCount = 0; var skippedCount = 0; var toAppend = []; var updateOps = []; // ── 1) Upserts ── for (var r = 0; r < records.length; r++) { var rec = records[r]; var uuid = String(rec[uuidField] || '').trim(); if (!uuid) { skippedCount++; continue; } var isUpdate = !!uuidToRow[uuid]; var existing = isUpdate ? existingRows[uuidToRow[uuid]] : null; // Build row values ตาม headers order // สำหรับแต่ละ column: ลอง rec[translated_field] ก่อน, ถ้าไม่มี → rec[column_name] (fallback) // ถ้ายังไม่มีอีก และเป็น update → เก็บค่าเดิม (preserve), เป็น insert → '' (empty) var rowVals = new Array(headers.length); for (var h = 0; h < headers.length; h++) { var colName = String(headers[h]).trim(); var fieldName = _colToField(kind, colName); // ลอง field name ที่แปลงแล้วก่อน (confirmation) → ถ้าไม่มี ลอง col name ดิบ (match_bank) var v = rec[fieldName]; if ((v === undefined || v === null) && fieldName !== colName) { v = rec[colName]; } if (v === undefined || v === null) { // Preserve on update: เก็บค่าเดิมใน sheet (ไม่ล้างข้อมูลที่ frontend ไม่ได้ส่งมา) var preserved = isUpdate ? existing[h] : ''; // ⭐ ถ้า preserved เป็น Date object → แปลงเป็น ISO string ก่อนเขียนกลับ // กัน Sheet auto-format เป็น พ.ศ. (27/4/2569) if (preserved instanceof Date && !isNaN(preserved)) { preserved = _formatDateForSheet(preserved, colName); } rowVals[h] = preserved; } else if (v instanceof Date) { // ⭐ Date object → ISO string เสมอ (กัน พ.ศ. format) rowVals[h] = _formatDateForSheet(v, colName); } else if (typeof v === 'object') { rowVals[h] = JSON.stringify(v); } else if (typeof v === 'boolean') { rowVals[h] = v ? 'Y' : 'N'; } else if (typeof v === 'string') { // ⭐ ถ้า string เป็น พ.ศ. dd/mm/yyyy → normalize เป็น yyyy-mm-dd ค.ศ. rowVals[h] = _normalizeDateString(v, colName); } else { rowVals[h] = v; } } if (isUpdate) { updateOps.push({ row: uuidToRow[uuid], values: rowVals }); } else { toAppend.push(rowVals); } } // ⭐ Pre-collect date column indices สำหรับ post-write formatting var DATE_COL_NAMES = ['date','transfer_date','pay_date','due_date','trans_date','time']; var dateColIndices = []; for (var hh = 0; hh < headers.length; hh++) { var colN = String(headers[hh]).toLowerCase().trim(); var isDate = DATE_COL_NAMES.indexOf(colN) >= 0 || colN.indexOf('_at') === colN.length - 3; if (isDate) dateColIndices.push(hh); } // ⭐ Force date columns to TEXT format BEFORE writing // ป้องกัน Apps Script auto-coerce string ISO '2026-04-06' เป็น Date object // ใน script timezone → swap day/month bug (เช่น 2026-04-06 → 2026-06-04) // Note: ต้อง setNumberFormat 2 ครั้ง — ก่อน + หลัง — เพราะ setValues บางทีรีเซ็ต format try { for (var di = 0; di < dateColIndices.length; di++) { var colIdx = dateColIndices[di] + 1; // Set entire column to text format (covers existing data + future writes) sheet.getRange(1, colIdx, sheet.getMaxRows(), 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } catch(_e) { /* swallow — non-critical */ } // ── 2) Apply updates ── for (var u = 0; u < updateOps.length; u++) { sheet.getRange(updateOps[u].row, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([updateOps[u].values]); updateCount++; } // ⭐ Re-apply text format after updates (setValues sometimes resets cell format) try { for (var di2 = 0; di2 < dateColIndices.length; di2++) { var colIdx2 = dateColIndices[di2] + 1; sheet.getRange(1, colIdx2, sheet.getMaxRows(), 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } catch(_e2) {} // ── 3) Batch append ── var insertCount = 0; if (toAppend.length > 0) { var startRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1; // ⭐ Pre-format new rows to text BEFORE setValues (gives Sheets explicit instruction) try { for (var di3 = 0; di3 < dateColIndices.length; di3++) { var colIdx3 = dateColIndices[di3] + 1; sheet.getRange(startRow, colIdx3, toAppend.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } catch(_e3) {} sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, toAppend.length, headers.length).setValues(toAppend); // ⭐ Re-apply text format AFTER setValues (some Apps Script versions reset format on write) try { for (var di4 = 0; di4 < dateColIndices.length; di4++) { var colIdx4 = dateColIndices[di4] + 1; sheet.getRange(startRow, colIdx4, toAppend.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } catch(_e4) {} insertCount = toAppend.length; } // ── 4) Deletes (bottom-up) ── var deleteCount = 0; if (deletes.length > 0) { var delRows = []; for (var d = 0; d < deletes.length; d++) { var du = String(deletes[d] || '').trim(); if (du && uuidToRow[du]) delRows.push(uuidToRow[du]); } delRows.sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; }); for (var dr = 0; dr < delRows.length; dr++) { sheet.deleteRow(delRows[dr]); } deleteCount = delRows.length; } // ── 5) Auto-cleanup duplicate uuid rows (delete bottom-up) ── var dedupCount = 0; if (duplicateRowsToDelete.length > 0) { duplicateRowsToDelete.sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; }); for (var dd = 0; dd < duplicateRowsToDelete.length; dd++) { sheet.deleteRow(duplicateRowsToDelete[dd]); } dedupCount = duplicateRowsToDelete.length; } return _uuidOk({ updated: updateCount, inserted: insertCount, deleted: deleteCount, dedup: dedupCount, skipped: skippedCount, sheet: sheetName, total_rows: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 4: clearSheetData // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ล้าง data rows ทั้งหมด (เก็บ header row 1) — ใช้ตอน migrate // Payload: { action: 'clearSheetData', sheet: 'bills_2026' } function clearSheetData(p) { try { var sheetName = p.sheet; if (!sheetName) return _uuidErr('Missing sheet name'); // Safety: อนุญาตเฉพาะ bills_/stmts_/match_/meta_ var allowed = /^(bills|stmts|match|meta)_\d{4}$/; if (!allowed.test(sheetName)) { return _uuidErr('Sheet name not allowed: ' + sheetName + ' (รับเฉพาะ bills_/stmts_/match_/meta_YYYY)'); } var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ cleared: 0, message: 'Already empty' }); var lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1); var deleteCount = lastRow - 1; // ✅ Freeze header ก่อน (กันกรณียังไม่ frozen → ลบไม่ได้) if (sheet.getFrozenRows() === 0) { sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } // ✅ ใช้ clearContent + deleteRows แบบปลอดภัย // Google Sheets ไม่ยอม deleteRows ถ้าจะเหลือ 0 non-frozen rows // → clear content ทุกแถวก่อน (กันข้อมูลเก่าค้าง) // → แล้ว delete rows ให้เหลือแค่ header (ถ้าทำได้) sheet.getRange(2, 1, deleteCount, lastCol).clearContent(); // พยายาม delete rows ด้วย (shrink sheet) — ถ้าทำไม่ได้ก็ปล่อยไว้เฉยๆ // ข้อมูลถูก clear แล้ว ดังนั้น getLastRow() จะ return 1 แทน → upsert ทำงานปกติ try { if (deleteCount > 0) sheet.deleteRows(2, deleteCount); } catch (delErr) { // ignore — content cleared แล้ว ไม่มีผลต่อ upsert } return _uuidOk({ cleared: deleteCount, sheet: sheetName }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 5 & 6: getBillsWithUuid / getStatementsWithUuid // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ดึงข้อมูลทั้งหมดพร้อม uuid column — ใช้ verify หลัง push // Payload: { action: 'getBillsWithUuid', year: '2026' } function getBillsWithUuid(p) { return _getWithUuid(p, 'bills'); } function getStatementsWithUuid(p) { return _getWithUuid(p, 'stmts'); } function getMetaWithUuid(p) { return _getWithUuid(p, 'meta'); } function _getWithUuid(p, kind) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var sheetName = kind + '_' + year; // ⭐ Fix: add 'meta' case — ก่อนแก้ meta จะตก default = 'stmt_uuid' → ไม่ match → return 0 rows var uuidField = (kind === 'bills') ? 'bill_uuid' : (kind === 'stmts') ? 'stmt_uuid' : (kind === 'meta') ? 'meta_uuid' : 'stmt_uuid'; var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) return _uuidOk({ rows: [], count: 0 }); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol === 0) return _uuidOk({ rows: [], count: 0 }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var data = []; for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var colName = String(headers[j]).trim(); if (!colName) continue; // แปลง sheet column → frontend field ก่อนใส่ใน obj // เช่น column 'match_bank' → obj.confirmation var fieldName = _colToField(kind, colName); var v = rows[i][j]; if (v instanceof Date) { // ⭐ Use ISO format consistently — keep date-only as 'yyyy-MM-dd' // แยก date column vs datetime column (*_at) ให้ตรง type // เดิม: dd/MM/yyyy ทั้งหมด → frontend ได้ format ผสม → match/sort/dedup พัง try { v = _formatDateForSheet(v, colName); } catch (e) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } catch(_e) { v = String(v); } } } // กันเคส 2 columns map ไปที่ field เดียวกัน (เช่น bill_type + pay_method → type) // → อันแรกที่มีค่า win (ไม่ overwrite ด้วยค่าว่าง) if (obj[fieldName] !== undefined && obj[fieldName] !== '' && (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined)) { continue; } obj[fieldName] = v; } if (!obj[uuidField]) continue; // skip empty rows data.push(obj); } return _uuidOk({ rows: data, count: data.length, sheet: sheetName }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 7: verifySheetCount // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ดึงจำนวน row ของ sheets ในปี — verify หลัง migrate // Payload: { action: 'verifySheetCount', year: '2026' } function verifySheetCount(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var counts = {}; var sheetNames = ['bills_' + year, 'stmts_' + year, 'meta_' + year]; for (var i = 0; i < sheetNames.length; i++) { var s = ss.getSheetByName(sheetNames[i]); counts[sheetNames[i]] = s ? Math.max(0, s.getLastRow() - 1) : null; } return _uuidOk({ year: year, counts: counts }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: ensureBillsColumns // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // เช็คว่า bills_YYYY มี column ที่ frontend ต้องใช้ครบไหม — ถ้าขาดก็ append เข้า header // ข้อมูลเดิมทุก row ไม่กระทบ (append เฉพาะ column ใหม่ท้ายสุด) // Payload: { action: 'ensureBillsColumns', year: '2026' } // Response: { success: true, data: { added: ['remarks2', 'bank'], already_exists: [...] } } function ensureBillsColumns(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet bills_' + year + ' ไม่เจอ — รัน prepareSheet ก่อน'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var existing = {}; for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) { existing[String(headers[i]).trim()] = true; } var added = []; var alreadyExists = []; for (var j = 0; j < BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS.length; j++) { var col = BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS[j]; if (existing[col]) { alreadyExists.push(col); } else { // Append เป็น column ใหม่ท้ายสุด sheet.getRange(1, lastCol + 1 + added.length).setValue(col); added.push(col); } } return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, added: added, already_exists: alreadyExists, total_cols: lastCol + added.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: alignBillsSchema // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // แก้ sheet header ให้ตรงกับ frontend field name: // 1. Rename legacy columns (bill_type → type, match_bank → confirmation, ...) // 2. Append columns ที่ frontend ใช้แต่ sheet ไม่มี (bank, remarks2, ...) // ไม่แตะข้อมูลในแถวทำแค่ row 1 (header) // // Payload: { action: 'alignBillsSchema', year: '2026' } // Response: { renamed: [{col, from, to}], added: ['bank',...], final_headers: [...] } function alignBillsSchema(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet bills_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var updatedHeaders = headers.slice(); var renames = []; // ── Step 1: Rename legacy columns ── // ใช้ BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD เป็น source of truth // เช่น 'bill_type' → 'type', 'match_bank' → 'confirmation' // แต่ถ้า target name มีอยู่แล้ว (เช่น 'pay_method' ก็ map → 'type' แต่ 'type' มีจาก rename ของ 'bill_type' แล้ว) → skip for (var i = 0; i < updatedHeaders.length; i++) { var col = String(updatedHeaders[i]).trim(); var target = BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD[col]; if (!target || target === col) continue; // เช็ค duplicate — ถ้า target name มีใน updatedHeaders แล้ว (หลังอาจถูก rename จาก col อื่น) → skip var existsIdx = -1; for (var k = 0; k < updatedHeaders.length; k++) { if (k !== i && String(updatedHeaders[k]).trim() === target) { existsIdx = k; break; } } if (existsIdx !== -1) { // Target ซ้ำ → ไม่ rename (user อาจจะลบ column นี้ทีหลังเอง) renames.push({ col: i + 1, from: col, to: target, skipped: true, reason: 'duplicate target' }); continue; } renames.push({ col: i + 1, from: col, to: target }); updatedHeaders[i] = target; } // Apply rename ใน batch (1 call) var anyRenamed = renames.some(function(r) { return !r.skipped; }); if (anyRenamed) { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, updatedHeaders.length).setValues([updatedHeaders]); } // ── Step 2: Append missing columns ── var existingSet = {}; updatedHeaders.forEach(function(h) { existingSet[String(h).trim()] = true; }); var additions = []; for (var m = 0; m < BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS.length; m++) { var extra = BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS[m]; if (!existingSet[extra]) { sheet.getRange(1, updatedHeaders.length + 1 + additions.length).setValue(extra); additions.push(extra); } } return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, renamed: renames, added: additions, final_headers: updatedHeaders.concat(additions), }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: reorderBillsSchema // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Reorder columns ใน bills_YYYY ให้ตรงกับ UI order // ⚠️ DESTRUCTIVE — อ่าน data ทั้งหมด, clear sheet, แล้วเขียนใหม่ตาม order ใหม่ // ⚠️ ควรมี backup JSON ก่อนรัน // // Desired order: [bill_uuid, UI columns ตรงลำดับ UI, secondary fields, legacy fields] // - Columns ไม่อยู่ใน desired list → ต่อท้ายตาม order เดิม // - Columns ใน desired list ที่ไม่มีใน sheet → skip (ไม่ append ใหม่ — ใช้ alignBillsSchema/ensureBillsColumns สำหรับ add) // // Payload: { action: 'reorderBillsSchema', year: '2026' } // Columns ที่ควรถูกลบจาก bills_YYYY sheet — cross-checked กับ frontend แล้วไม่ใช้ // ⚠️ แก้ list นี้เมื่อ field ถูกนำกลับมาใช้ เช็คด้วย grep ใน index.html ก่อน var BILLS_DROP_LIST = [ // ── User-confirmed dead columns (manual delete + drop endpoint) ── 'row_id', // 0% data 'net_amount', // 0% data 'source_file', // 0% data 'pp_no', // 0% data 'sp_no', // 0% data (alias ของ paid_by_sp) 'cust_id', // 0% data (alias ของ customer_code) 'is_vat', // SP Recon-only field 'bill_date', // ซ้ำกับ 'date' 'imported_from', // 0% data 'imported_at', // 0% data 'sp_date', // 0% data 'note', // 0% data (singular) 'note2', // 0% data 'notes', // mirror ของ remarks1 — user ลบแล้ว 'created_by', // 0% data (frontend ไม่ได้ส่งสำหรับ bills) 'vat', // 0% data ใน bills (cashflow ใช้ field นี้) 'paid_stmt_ref', // mirror ของ match_ref — เก็บ match_ref 'customer', // 0% data 'customer_code', // 0% data // ── stmt-only fields (ลบจาก bills sheet — ยังอยู่ใน stmts__YYYY) ── 'paid_by', // 0% ใน bills (frontend เขียนใน stmt object เท่านั้น) 'paid_bills', // 0% ใน bills 'match_method', // 0% ใน bills 'match_reason', // 0% ใน bills 'expected_clear_date', // 0% data — feature Cheque ใช้ due_date แทน // ── Legacy mirror columns (alias mapping ถูกลบแล้ว → ปลอดภัยที่จะ drop) ── 'match_date', // ghost pay_date คาบาง user ลบ pay_date ใน sheet ไม่หาย 'match_bank', // ghost confirmation 'bill_type', // ghost type 'pay_method', // ghost type ]; // Desired column order สำหรับ bills sheet (เรียงตาม UI Main page) // ใช้ Sheet column names (ไม่ใช่ frontend field name) var BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER = [ // System PK (col 1 เสมอ) 'bill_uuid', // ── UI columns (Main page table order) ── 'bill_no', // Bill Number 'date', // Bill Date 'station', // Station 'type', // Type 'amount', // Baht 'wht_enabled', // (hidden) → ใช้คำนวณ "หัก" 'wht_amount', // (hidden) 'transfer_fee', // (hidden) 'card_fee', // (hidden) 'confirmation', // Confirmation 'pay_date', // Pay Date 'remarks1', // Remarks1 'remarks2', // Remarks2 'bank', // Bank 'due_date', // Due Date (cheque date เก็บที่นี่ — ใช้แจ้งเตือน) // ── Match group (เฉพาะของ bill — ตัดที่เป็น stmt-only ออกไป) ── 'match_status', 'match_ref', // = matched_stmt_ref ใน frontend (ref_no string) 'matched_stmt_uuid', // ⭐ FK → stmts.stmt_uuid (stable PK link, สำคัญสำหรับ frontend match) 'paid_at', // = matched_at ใน frontend 'paid_by_sp', // เลข SP ที่ตัดบิลนี้ // ── Metadata ── 'source', // pos / smart / manual 'created_at', ]; function reorderBillsSchema(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet bills_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); // 1. อ่านทั้ง sheet ก่อน (header + data) var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var data = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues() : []; // 2. Build index: column name → current column index var currentIdxByName = {}; headers.forEach(function(h, i) { var name = String(h).trim(); if (name) currentIdxByName[name] = i; }); // 3. สร้าง new column order // - เอา desired ที่มีใน sheet ไปก่อน (ตามลำดับ desired) // - แล้วต่อด้วย column ที่มีใน sheet แต่ไม่ได้อยู่ใน desired list (ตามลำดับเดิม) var newOrder = []; var usedSet = {}; for (var d = 0; d < BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER.length; d++) { var colName = BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER[d]; if (currentIdxByName[colName] !== undefined && !usedSet[colName]) { newOrder.push(colName); usedSet[colName] = true; } } for (var h2 = 0; h2 < headers.length; h2++) { var name2 = String(headers[h2]).trim(); if (name2 && !usedSet[name2]) { newOrder.push(name2); usedSet[name2] = true; } } // 4. Check: order ตรงแล้วหรือยัง? ถ้าตรง → no-op var alreadyCorrect = true; for (var c = 0; c < newOrder.length; c++) { if (String(headers[c] || '').trim() !== newOrder[c]) { alreadyCorrect = false; break; } } if (alreadyCorrect && newOrder.length === headers.length) { return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, reordered: false, message: 'Already in correct order', headers: newOrder, }); } // 5. Build new data matrix (header row + data rows) var newMatrix = []; newMatrix.push(newOrder.slice()); // header for (var r = 0; r < data.length; r++) { var newRow = new Array(newOrder.length); for (var nc = 0; nc < newOrder.length; nc++) { var srcIdx = currentIdxByName[newOrder[nc]]; newRow[nc] = (srcIdx !== undefined) ? data[r][srcIdx] : ''; } newMatrix.push(newRow); } // 6. Clear sheet + write new matrix // ⚠️ ถ้า setValues fail หลัง clear → ข้อมูลหาย → user ต้อง restore จาก backup sheet.clear(); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); sheet.getRange(1, 1, newMatrix.length, newOrder.length).setValues(newMatrix); return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, reordered: true, old_order: headers, new_order: newOrder, rows_written: data.length, total_cols: newOrder.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: finalizeBillsSchema ⭐ ONE-SHOT CLEANUP // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // รวม 4 operations ในครั้งเดียว — สำหรับปรับ sheet ให้ตรงกับ frontend schema // 1. Rename legacy columns (bill_type → type, ...) // 2. Drop unused columns (BILLS_DROP_LIST — cross-checked แล้ว) // 3. Add missing columns (BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS) // 4. Reorder ตาม UI layout (BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER) // // ⚠️ DESTRUCTIVE — อ่าน→clear→เขียนใหม่ ต้อง backup ก่อน // ⚠️ Idempotent — รันซ้ำได้ ผลลัพธ์เหมือนเดิม // // Payload: { action: 'finalizeBillsSchema', year: '2026' } function finalizeBillsSchema(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet bills_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); // 1. Read everything var origHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var data = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues() : []; // 2. Normalize header names (trim) และ apply rename var renames = []; var normalizedHeaders = origHeaders.map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); // ใช้ BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD เป็น rename rule for (var i = 0; i < normalizedHeaders.length; i++) { var col = normalizedHeaders[i]; var target = BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD[col]; if (target && target !== col) { // Check duplicate target var dup = false; for (var k = 0; k < normalizedHeaders.length; k++) { if (k !== i && normalizedHeaders[k] === target) { dup = true; break; } } if (!dup) { renames.push({ from: col, to: target, col: i + 1 }); normalizedHeaders[i] = target; } } } // 3. Build drop set (รวม BILLS_DROP_LIST + renamed-source ที่ยังมี duplicate) var dropSet = {}; BILLS_DROP_LIST.forEach(function(c) { dropSet[c] = true; }); // Identify which columns to keep (not in drop set) var keepCols = []; // list of { name, origIdx } var dropped = []; for (var j = 0; j < normalizedHeaders.length; j++) { var name = normalizedHeaders[j]; if (!name) { dropped.push({ name: '(empty)', origIdx: j }); continue; } if (dropSet[name]) { dropped.push({ name: name, origIdx: j }); } else { keepCols.push({ name: name, origIdx: j }); } } // 4. Add missing columns var keepNames = {}; keepCols.forEach(function(c) { keepNames[c.name] = true; }); var added = []; for (var m = 0; m < BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS.length; m++) { var extra = BILLS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS[m]; if (!keepNames[extra]) { keepCols.push({ name: extra, origIdx: -1 }); // -1 = new column (empty data) keepNames[extra] = true; added.push(extra); } } // 5. Reorder: put desired first (in desired order), then others var nameToCol = {}; keepCols.forEach(function(c) { nameToCol[c.name] = c; }); var finalOrder = []; var usedSet = {}; for (var d2 = 0; d2 < BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER.length; d2++) { var dname = BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER[d2]; if (nameToCol[dname] && !usedSet[dname]) { finalOrder.push(nameToCol[dname]); usedSet[dname] = true; } } keepCols.forEach(function(c) { if (!usedSet[c.name]) { finalOrder.push(c); usedSet[c.name] = true; } }); // 6. Build new data matrix var newHeaders = finalOrder.map(function(c) { return c.name; }); var newMatrix = [newHeaders]; for (var r = 0; r < data.length; r++) { var newRow = new Array(finalOrder.length); for (var nc = 0; nc < finalOrder.length; nc++) { var src = finalOrder[nc].origIdx; newRow[nc] = (src >= 0) ? data[r][src] : ''; } newMatrix.push(newRow); } // 7. Write back sheet.clear(); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); sheet.getRange(1, 1, newMatrix.length, finalOrder.length).setValues(newMatrix); return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, old_headers: origHeaders, new_headers: newHeaders, renamed: renames, dropped: dropped.map(function(d) { return d.name; }), added: added, rows_preserved: data.length, total_cols: finalOrder.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: checkBillsSchemaDrift — PREVENTION (Rule 2 & 3) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Cross-check ระหว่าง fields ใน payload (frontend) กับ columns ใน sheet // เตือนก่อน push ว่ามี field ไหนไม่มี column → ข้อมูลจะหาย // หรือมี column ไหนใน sheet ที่ frontend ไม่รู้จัก → ควรลบ // // Payload: { action: 'checkBillsSchemaDrift', year: '2026', sample_fields: ['bill_no', 'type', ...] } // Response: // { // missing_columns: ['newField'], // field ใน payload แต่ไม่มี column → data จะหาย // extra_columns: ['legacyCol'], // column ใน sheet แต่ไม่มี field → ควรลบ // legacy_aliases: [{col, should_be}], // column ชื่อ legacy ที่ควร rename // ok: bool // } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: finalizeStmtsSchema ⭐ ONE-SHOT CLEANUP for stmts_YYYY // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // เหมือน finalizeBillsSchema แต่สำหรับ stmts_YYYY: // 1. Rename legacy columns (ถ้ามีใน STMTS_COL_TO_FIELD) // 2. Drop unused columns (STMTS_DROP_LIST) // 3. Add missing columns (STMTS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS) // 4. Reorder ตาม STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER // 5. Generate stmt_uuid สำหรับ row ที่ยังไม่มี (ป้องกัน duplicate ตอน sync) // // ⚠️ DESTRUCTIVE — อ่าน→clear→เขียนใหม่ ต้อง backup ก่อน // Payload: { action: 'finalizeStmtsSchema', year: '2026' } function finalizeStmtsSchema(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('stmts_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet stmts_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); // 1. Read everything var origHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; var data = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues() : []; // 2. Normalize + apply renames var normalizedHeaders = origHeaders.map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var renames = []; for (var i = 0; i < normalizedHeaders.length; i++) { var col = normalizedHeaders[i]; var target = STMTS_COL_TO_FIELD[col]; if (target && target !== col) { var dup = false; for (var k = 0; k < normalizedHeaders.length; k++) { if (k !== i && normalizedHeaders[k] === target) { dup = true; break; } } if (!dup) { renames.push({ from: col, to: target, col: i + 1 }); normalizedHeaders[i] = target; } } } // 3. Build drop set + identify keep columns var dropSet = {}; STMTS_DROP_LIST.forEach(function(c) { dropSet[c] = true; }); var keepCols = []; var dropped = []; for (var j = 0; j < normalizedHeaders.length; j++) { var name = normalizedHeaders[j]; if (!name) { dropped.push('(empty)'); continue; } if (dropSet[name]) { dropped.push(name); } else { keepCols.push({ name: name, origIdx: j }); } } // 4. Add missing columns var keepNames = {}; keepCols.forEach(function(c) { keepNames[c.name] = true; }); var added = []; for (var m = 0; m < STMTS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS.length; m++) { var extra = STMTS_EXPECTED_EXTRA_COLS[m]; if (!keepNames[extra]) { keepCols.push({ name: extra, origIdx: -1 }); keepNames[extra] = true; added.push(extra); } } // 5. Reorder by STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER var nameToCol = {}; keepCols.forEach(function(c) { nameToCol[c.name] = c; }); var finalOrder = []; var usedSet = {}; for (var d2 = 0; d2 < STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER.length; d2++) { var dname = STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER[d2]; if (nameToCol[dname] && !usedSet[dname]) { finalOrder.push(nameToCol[dname]); usedSet[dname] = true; } } keepCols.forEach(function(c) { if (!usedSet[c.name]) { finalOrder.push(c); usedSet[c.name] = true; } }); // 6. Find uuid column index in final order (should be col 0) var uuidColIdx = -1; for (var u = 0; u < finalOrder.length; u++) { if (finalOrder[u].name === 'stmt_uuid') { uuidColIdx = u; break; } } // 7. Build new data matrix + generate uuid for rows missing it var newHeaders = finalOrder.map(function(c) { return c.name; }); var newMatrix = [newHeaders]; var uuidGenerated = 0; for (var r = 0; r < data.length; r++) { var newRow = new Array(finalOrder.length); for (var nc = 0; nc < finalOrder.length; nc++) { var src = finalOrder[nc].origIdx; newRow[nc] = (src >= 0) ? data[r][src] : ''; } // Generate stmt_uuid if missing (ป้องกัน duplicate ตอน sync) if (uuidColIdx >= 0) { var existingUuid = String(newRow[uuidColIdx] || '').trim(); if (!existingUuid) { newRow[uuidColIdx] = 's-' + new Date().getTime() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10) + '-' + r; uuidGenerated++; } } newMatrix.push(newRow); } // 8. Write back sheet.clear(); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); sheet.getRange(1, 1, newMatrix.length, finalOrder.length).setValues(newMatrix); return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'stmts_' + year, old_headers: origHeaders, new_headers: newHeaders, renamed: renames, dropped: dropped, added: added, rows_preserved: data.length, uuid_generated: uuidGenerated, total_cols: finalOrder.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: dedupStmtsByUuid // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ลบ row ที่มี stmt_uuid ซ้ำกัน (เก็บตัวแรก, ลบที่เหลือ) // หรือถ้า 2 rows "ดูเหมือนซ้ำ" (bank+date+time+amount+ref เหมือนกัน) แต่คนละ uuid // → ลบตัวที่ไม่มี match_status หรือที่ match_status="unmatched" // // Payload: { action: 'dedupStmtsByUuid', year: '2026', by_composite: bool (default false) } function dedupStmtsByUuid(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var byComposite = !!p.by_composite; var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('stmts_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet stmts_' + year + ' not found'); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ removed: 0, message: 'Sheet empty' }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var uuidIdx = headers.indexOf('stmt_uuid'); var bankIdx = headers.indexOf('bank'); var dateIdx = headers.indexOf('date'); var timeIdx = headers.indexOf('time'); var amtIdx = headers.indexOf('amount'); var refIdx = headers.indexOf('ref_no'); var msIdx = headers.indexOf('match_status'); if (uuidIdx < 0) return _uuidErr('stmt_uuid column not found — run finalizeStmtsSchema first'); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); // Pass 1: dedupe by uuid (exact) var seenUuid = {}; var keepByUuid = []; // rows to keep (with original row index for analysis) var removedUuid = 0; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var u = String(data[i][uuidIdx] || '').trim(); if (!u) { keepByUuid.push({ row: data[i], origIdx: i }); // no uuid → keep } else if (!seenUuid[u]) { seenUuid[u] = true; keepByUuid.push({ row: data[i], origIdx: i }); } else { removedUuid++; } } // Pass 2: optional — dedupe by composite (bank|date|time|amount|ref_no) var removedComposite = 0; var finalKeep = keepByUuid; if (byComposite && bankIdx >= 0 && dateIdx >= 0 && amtIdx >= 0) { var seenComp = {}; var kept2 = []; for (var j = 0; j < keepByUuid.length; j++) { var row = keepByUuid[j].row; var key = String(row[bankIdx] || '') + '|' + String(row[dateIdx] || '') + '|' + String(timeIdx >= 0 ? row[timeIdx] : '') + '|' + String(row[amtIdx] || '') + '|' + String(refIdx >= 0 ? row[refIdx] : ''); if (!seenComp[key]) { seenComp[key] = { row: row, matched: msIdx >= 0 && row[msIdx] === 'paid' }; kept2.push(keepByUuid[j]); } else { // prefer matched row over unmatched var newMatched = msIdx >= 0 && row[msIdx] === 'paid'; if (newMatched && !seenComp[key].matched) { // replace: remove previously kept, add this one var prevIdx = -1; for (var p2 = 0; p2 < kept2.length; p2++) { if (kept2[p2].row === seenComp[key].row) { prevIdx = p2; break; } } if (prevIdx >= 0) kept2.splice(prevIdx, 1); kept2.push(keepByUuid[j]); seenComp[key] = { row: row, matched: true }; } removedComposite++; } } finalKeep = kept2; } // Write back sheet.clear(); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); var outMatrix = [headers]; finalKeep.forEach(function(k) { outMatrix.push(k.row); }); if (outMatrix.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1, outMatrix.length, headers.length).setValues(outMatrix); } return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'stmts_' + year, original_rows: data.length, kept: finalKeep.length, removed_by_uuid: removedUuid, removed_by_composite: removedComposite, total_removed: removedUuid + removedComposite, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } function checkBillsSchemaDrift(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('bills_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet bills_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0] .map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }) .filter(function(h) { return h; }); var headerSet = {}; headers.forEach(function(h) { headerSet[h] = true; }); var sampleFields = p.sample_fields || []; var sampleSet = {}; sampleFields.forEach(function(f) { sampleSet[f] = true; }); // 1. Missing columns — frontend field ที่ sheet ไม่มี (data จะหาย) var missing = []; sampleFields.forEach(function(f) { if (!headerSet[f]) missing.push(f); }); // 2. Extra columns — sheet column ที่ frontend ไม่มี field รองรับ (ควรลบ) // ยกเว้น: bill_uuid (system) + fields ใน BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER (รู้จัก) var knownSet = { 'bill_uuid': true }; BILLS_DESIRED_ORDER.forEach(function(c) { knownSet[c] = true; }); var extra = []; headers.forEach(function(h) { if (!knownSet[h] && !sampleSet[h]) extra.push(h); }); // 3. Legacy aliases — column ที่อยู่ใน translation map (ควร rename) var aliases = []; headers.forEach(function(h) { if (BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD[h] && BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD[h] !== h) { aliases.push({ col: h, should_be: BILLS_COL_TO_FIELD[h] }); } }); return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'bills_' + year, missing_columns: missing, extra_columns: extra, legacy_aliases: aliases, ok: missing.length === 0 && aliases.length === 0, total_headers: headers.length, total_sample_fields: sampleFields.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: checkStmtsSchemaDrift — PREVENTION // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'checkStmtsSchemaDrift', year: '2026', sample_fields: [...] } function checkStmtsSchemaDrift(p) { try { var year = String(p.year); if (!year) return _uuidErr('Missing year'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('stmts_' + year); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet stmts_' + year + ' not found'); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet has no headers'); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0] .map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }) .filter(function(h) { return h; }); var headerSet = {}; headers.forEach(function(h) { headerSet[h] = true; }); var sampleFields = p.sample_fields || []; var sampleSet = {}; sampleFields.forEach(function(f) { sampleSet[f] = true; }); var missing = []; sampleFields.forEach(function(f) { if (!headerSet[f]) missing.push(f); }); var knownSet = { 'stmt_uuid': true }; STMTS_DESIRED_ORDER.forEach(function(c) { knownSet[c] = true; }); var extra = []; headers.forEach(function(h) { if (!knownSet[h] && !sampleSet[h]) extra.push(h); }); return _uuidOk({ sheet: 'stmts_' + year, missing_columns: missing, extra_columns: extra, ok: missing.length === 0, total_headers: headers.length, total_sample_fields: sampleFields.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // TEST FUNCTIONS — รันใน Apps Script editor // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SP-TEMP (Temporary SP) BACKEND MODULE — v1.0 // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SP-TEMP คือ "SP ชั่วคราว" — ใช้ track partial payment // ก่อนที่ SP จริง (SP20YYMM-XXXX) จะมาจากโปรแกรม Smart // // Flow: // 1. ลูกค้าโอนเงินเข้ามา แต่ยังไม่มีเลข SP จริง // 2. User สร้าง SP-TEMP-001 ที่ frontend (กรอก expected total) // 3. User เพิ่ม partial payment ที่เข้ามาเรื่อยๆ (track ด้วย progress bar) // 4. เมื่อ import SP จริงจาก Smart → frontend เทียบ candidate → // user เลือก "Graduate" = replace SP-TEMP-001 ด้วย SP จริง // // วิธีติดตั้ง: // 1. Apps Script → "+" → Script → ตั้งชื่อ "sp_temps" → paste ไฟล์นี้ → Save // 2. แก้ code.gs: เพิ่ม 3 cases ใน handleRequest หลัง UUID sync cases // 3. Deploy → Manage → Edit → New version → Deploy // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const SP_TEMP_SHEET = 'sp_temps'; // ⭐ 26-col FLAT schema — 1 row = 1 (bill metadata) OR 1 (partial event) // Composite PK: (sp_temp_uuid, sb_no, bill_no, partial_uuid) // Push behavior: replace-per-uuid (delete all existing rows for uuid → re-insert) const SP_TEMP_HEADERS = [ 'sp_temp_uuid', 'no', 'date_created', 'customer', 'expected_total', 'status', 'linked_sp', 'notes', 'sb_no', 'bill_no', 'bill_type', 'bill_amount', 'partial_uuid', 'type', 'amount', 'pay_date', 'confirmation', 'remarks1', 'remarks2', 'bank', 'due_date', 'match_status', 'matched_stmt_uuid', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'created_by' ]; // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Helpers — ใช้ helpers จาก code.gs เดิม // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function _spTempOk(data) { if (typeof jsonOk === 'function') return jsonOk(data); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: true, data: data })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _spTempErr(msg, code) { if (typeof jsonError === 'function') return jsonError(msg, code || 400); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: msg, code: code || 400 })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _spTempSheet() { var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SP_TEMP_SHEET); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SP_TEMP_SHEET); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length).setValues([SP_TEMP_HEADERS]); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length) .setFontWeight('bold') .setBackground('#1a1d27') .setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } return sheet; } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 1: saveSpTemps (26-col FLAT schema, replace-per-uuid) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'saveSpTemps', // records: [{sp_temp_uuid, no, sb_no, bill_no, partial_uuid, ...}], // flat rows // deletes: [no, no, ...], // SP-TEMP nos to fully delete // created_by: 'Admin' // } // // Behavior: REPLACE-PER-UUID — for each unique sp_temp_uuid in records: // 1. Delete all existing rows for that uuid (cleans stale rows) // 2. Insert all new rows for that uuid // This guarantees clean state — no zombie rows from old schema migrations. function saveSpTemps(req) { try { var records = req.records || []; var deletes = req.deletes || []; if (!Array.isArray(records)) return _spTempErr('records must be array'); var sheet = _spTempSheet(); var nowIso = new Date().toISOString(); var createdBy = req.created_by || 'Admin'; // ⭐ Auto-init/migrate header if needed (idempotent) var actualLastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var headerOk = false; if (actualLastCol >= SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length) { var actualHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length).getValues()[0] .map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); headerOk = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.every(function(h, i) { return actualHeaders[i] === h; }); } if (!headerOk) { // Backup ก่อน clear try { if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) { var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var bk = sheet.copyTo(sheet.getParent()); bk.setName((SP_TEMP_SHEET + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100)); } } catch(_) {} sheet.clear(); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length).setValues([SP_TEMP_HEADERS]); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length) .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#1a1d27').setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } var headerLen = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length; var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); // Build map: sp_temp_uuid → [rowNum, rowNum, ...] // (1 uuid อาจมีหลาย row เพราะ flat schema) var uuidToRows = {}; var noToRows = {}; // for delete-by-no if (lastRow >= 2) { var uuidCol = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 2).getValues(); // col 1=uuid, col 2=no for (var i = 0; i < uuidCol.length; i++) { var u = String(uuidCol[i][0] || '').trim(); var n = String(uuidCol[i][1] || '').replace(/^'/, '').trim(); if (u) { if (!uuidToRows[u]) uuidToRows[u] = []; uuidToRows[u].push(i + 2); } if (n) { if (!noToRows[n]) noToRows[n] = []; noToRows[n].push(i + 2); } } } // Group records by sp_temp_uuid var recordsByUuid = {}; var skippedCount = 0; for (var r = 0; r < records.length; r++) { var rec = records[r] || {}; var u2 = String(rec.sp_temp_uuid || '').trim(); if (!u2) { skippedCount++; continue; } if (!recordsByUuid[u2]) recordsByUuid[u2] = []; recordsByUuid[u2].push(rec); } // Collect ALL rows to delete (uuids being replaced + no's in deletes) var rowsToDelete = {}; Object.keys(recordsByUuid).forEach(function(u) { (uuidToRows[u] || []).forEach(function(rn) { rowsToDelete[rn] = true; }); }); if (Array.isArray(deletes)) { deletes.forEach(function(d) { var dn = String(d || '').trim(); if (!dn) return; (noToRows[dn] || []).forEach(function(rn) { rowsToDelete[rn] = true; }); }); } // Delete bottom-up to avoid row shifting var sortedRows = Object.keys(rowsToDelete).map(Number).sort(function(a,b){return b-a;}); var deletedRowCount = 0; sortedRows.forEach(function(rn) { sheet.deleteRow(rn); deletedRowCount++; }); // Build all new rows to append var toAppend = []; Object.keys(recordsByUuid).forEach(function(u) { recordsByUuid[u].forEach(function(rec) { var row = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.map(function(field) { var v = rec[field]; if (v === undefined || v === null) return ''; // Plain-text columns (prefix with ') if (field === 'sp_temp_uuid' || field === 'no' || field === 'linked_sp' || field === 'sb_no' || field === 'bill_no' || field === 'partial_uuid' || field === 'matched_stmt_uuid') { var s = String(v).trim(); return s ? "'" + s : ''; } // Set audit timestamps if missing if (field === 'created_at' && !v) return nowIso; if (field === 'updated_at' && !v) return nowIso; if (field === 'created_by' && !v) return createdBy; return v; }); toAppend.push(row); }); }); // Append in single batch var insertedCount = 0; if (toAppend.length > 0) { var startRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1; sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, toAppend.length, headerLen).setValues(toAppend); insertedCount = toAppend.length; } // Apply text format to PK columns sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); sheet.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@'); sheet.getRange('I:I').setNumberFormat('@'); // sb_no sheet.getRange('J:J').setNumberFormat('@'); // bill_no sheet.getRange('M:M').setNumberFormat('@'); // partial_uuid sheet.getRange('W:W').setNumberFormat('@'); // matched_stmt_uuid return _spTempOk({ uuids_replaced: Object.keys(recordsByUuid).length, inserted: insertedCount, deleted: deletedRowCount, skipped: skippedCount, total_rows: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), }); } catch (e) { return _spTempErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 2: getSpTemps (returns 26-col flat array) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Frontend handles grouping (spTempGroupFlat) into nested SP-TEMP objects. function getSpTemps(req) { try { var sheet = _spTempSheet(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _spTempOk([]); var actualLastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (actualLastCol < 1) return _spTempOk([]); var actualHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, actualLastCol).getValues()[0] .map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, actualLastCol).getValues(); // Plain-text fields — strip leading apostrophe var STRIP_QUOTE = ['sp_temp_uuid', 'no', 'linked_sp', 'sb_no', 'bill_no', 'partial_uuid', 'matched_stmt_uuid']; var DATE_FIELDS = ['date_created', 'pay_date', 'due_date']; var ISO_FIELDS = ['created_at', 'updated_at']; var data = []; for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var obj = {}; var hasUuid = false; for (var j = 0; j < actualHeaders.length; j++) { var key = actualHeaders[j]; if (!key) continue; var v = rows[i][j]; if (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) { obj[key] = ''; continue; } if (v instanceof Date) { if (ISO_FIELDS.indexOf(key) >= 0) { try { v = v.toISOString(); } catch(_) {} } else if (DATE_FIELDS.indexOf(key) >= 0) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } catch(_) {} } } else if (typeof v === 'string') { if (STRIP_QUOTE.indexOf(key) >= 0) v = v.replace(/^'/, ''); } obj[key] = v; if (key === 'sp_temp_uuid' && obj[key]) hasUuid = true; } // Skip rows with no uuid (corrupt/blank rows) if (!hasUuid) continue; data.push(obj); } return _spTempOk(data); } catch (e) { return _spTempErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT: migrateSpTempSchema // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Migrate sp_temps sheet to new schema: // - Add 'sp_temp_uuid' column at col A (gen UUID v4 for existing rows) // - Add 'bills_json' column at end (default '[]') // Idempotent — safe to run multiple times // Payload: { action: 'migrateSpTempSchema', dryRun?: true } function migrateSpTempSchema(req) { try { var dryRun = !!(req && req.dryRun); var ss = (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') ? getSpreadsheet() : SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SP_TEMP_SHEET); var report = { dry_run: dryRun, sheet: SP_TEMP_SHEET }; if (!sheet) { report.skipped = 'sheet not found — will be created on first save'; return _spTempOk(report); } var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol < 1) { report.skipped = 'empty sheet'; return _spTempOk(report); } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var data = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues() : []; var hMap = {}; headers.forEach(function(h, i) { hMap[h] = i; }); report.old_headers = headers.join(','); report.old_cols = headers.length; report.old_rows = data.length; // Already migrated check if (hMap.sp_temp_uuid != null && hMap.bills_json != null) { // Verify all rows have uuid var emptyUuid = 0; var uuidIdx = hMap.sp_temp_uuid; data.forEach(function(r) { if (!String(r[uuidIdx] || '').trim()) emptyUuid++; }); if (emptyUuid === 0) { report.skipped = 'already migrated'; return _spTempOk(report); } report.note = 'partial migration — backfilling ' + emptyUuid + ' uuids'; } // Build new rows in target order var newRows = data.map(function(oldRow) { var newRow = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.map(function(field) { if (field === 'sp_temp_uuid') { // Use existing uuid if present, else generate new var existing = hMap.sp_temp_uuid != null ? String(oldRow[hMap.sp_temp_uuid] || '').trim() : ''; return existing || Utilities.getUuid(); } if (field === 'bills_json') { // Use existing bills_json if present, else default '[]' var existing = hMap.bills_json != null ? String(oldRow[hMap.bills_json] || '').trim() : ''; return existing || '[]'; } // Other fields — copy from old position if exists if (hMap[field] != null) return oldRow[hMap[field]]; return ''; }); return newRow; }); report.new_headers = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.join(','); report.new_cols = SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length; report.new_rows = newRows.length; if (!dryRun) { // Backup current sheet var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE || 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'); var backupName = (SP_TEMP_SHEET + '_backup_' + ts).substring(0, 100); try { var backup = sheet.copyTo(ss); backup.setName(backupName); report.backup = backupName; } catch (e) { report.backup_error = e.message; } // Clear + write new schema sheet.clear(); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length).setValues([SP_TEMP_HEADERS]); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length) .setFontWeight('bold') .setBackground('#1a1d27') .setFontColor('#e8eaf0'); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); if (newRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, newRows.length, SP_TEMP_HEADERS.length).setValues(newRows); } // Plain-text format on key columns sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@'); // sp_temp_uuid sheet.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@'); // no sheet.getRange('D:D').setNumberFormat('@'); // cust_code sheet.getRange('K:K').setNumberFormat('@'); // linked_sp } return _spTempOk(report); } catch (e) { return _spTempErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ENDPOINT 3: deleteSpTemp // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ลบ SP-TEMP ตาม no // Payload: { action: 'deleteSpTemp', no: 'SP-TEMP-001' } function deleteSpTemp(req) { try { var no = String(req.no || '').trim(); if (!no) return _spTempErr('Missing no'); var sheet = _spTempSheet(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _spTempOk({ deleted: false, reason: 'empty sheet' }); var noValues = sheet.getRange(2, 2, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); for (var i = 0; i < noValues.length; i++) { var n = String(noValues[i][0] || '').replace(/^'/, '').trim(); if (n === no) { sheet.deleteRow(i + 2); return _spTempOk({ deleted: true, no: no }); } } return _spTempOk({ deleted: false, reason: 'not found' }); } catch (e) { return _spTempErr(e.message); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // TEST FUNCTIONS — รันใน Apps Script editor // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Smart Finance — code.gs v2 Extensions (additive) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // PURPOSE: เพิ่ม endpoints + features ใหม่ โดยไม่แตะ code.gs เดิม // - Bank split: stmts_KTB_2026, stmts_KBANK_2026, ... // - Date-range queries: getBillsByDateRange, getStatementsByBank, ... // - Audit log: track ทุก mutation // - Migration script: แยก stmts_2026 → per-bank sheets // // DEPLOYMENT (3 ขั้นตอน): // 1. Apps Script editor → "+" ข้าง Files → Script → ชื่อ "sf_v2_extensions" → paste ไฟล์นี้ → Save // 2. เปิด code.gs เดิม → หา `default: return jsonError('Unknown action:` ใน handleRequest // เพิ่ม **ก่อน** บรรทัดนั้น: // // ═══ v2 extensions ═══ // var _v2 = (typeof routeV2 === 'function') ? routeV2(action, req) : null; // if (_v2) return _v2; // 3. Deploy → Manage deployments → Edit → New version → Deploy // // ROLLBACK: ลบไฟล์ sf_v2_extensions.gs + ลบ 3 บรรทัดที่เพิ่มใน code.gs → คืนค่าเดิม // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SECTION 1 — CONFIG + SCHEMA (single source of truth) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // V2_CONFIG: self-contained config — works even without code.gs // ⚠ IMPORTANT: ใส่ SPREADSHEET_ID ของคุณตรงนี้ (หรือเก็บเป็น default) // ถ้า code.gs เดิมมี CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID — v2 จะใช้ของเดิม (override นี้) var V2_CONFIG = { SPREADSHEET_ID: '1wVBPXR7YCwDCsSXsJKi9vh-jbFz8zQvPH2isyPlxbKU', // ← ใส่ ID ตรงนี้ ถ้าจำเป็น TIMEZONE: 'Asia/Bangkok', }; var V2_SCHEMA = { bills: { sheetPrefix: 'bills_', // bills_2026 uuidColumn: 'bill_uuid', }, statements: { sheetPrefix: 'stmts_', // legacy: stmts_2026 (single) sheetPrefixPerBank: 'stmts_', // new: stmts_KTB_2026, ... uuidColumn: 'stmt_uuid', banks: ['KTB', 'KBANK', 'BBL_NW', 'BBL', 'TTB'], // Bank name normalization (sheet/incoming → canonical) bankNormalize: { 'KTB': 'KTB', 'KBANK': 'KBANK', 'KASIKORN': 'KBANK', 'KASIKORNBANK': 'KBANK', 'BBL': 'BBL', 'BANGKOK': 'BBL', 'BBL_MAIN': 'BBL', 'BBL_NW': 'BBL_NW', 'BBL NW': 'BBL_NW', 'BBLNW': 'BBL_NW', 'BBL-NW': 'BBL_NW', 'TTB': 'TTB', 'TMB': 'TTB', 'THANACHART': 'TTB', }, }, meta: { sheetPrefix: 'meta_', uuidColumn: 'meta_uuid', types: ['summary', 'cash_count', 'change', 'locks'], // 'locks' is new }, audit: { sheetName: 'audit_log', headers: ['ts', 'user', 'action', 'entity', 'uuid', 'before_json', 'after_json', 'metadata'], }, }; // Normalize bank name → canonical form function v2NormalizeBank(name) { if (!name) return ''; var key = String(name).toUpperCase().trim(); return V2_SCHEMA.statements.bankNormalize[key] || key; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SECTION 2 — Helpers (use existing code.gs helpers if available) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _v2Ok(data) { if (typeof jsonOk === 'function') return jsonOk(data); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: true, data: data })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _v2Err(msg, code) { if (typeof jsonError === 'function') return jsonError(msg, code || 400); return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: msg, code: code || 400 })) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } function _v2SS() { // Priority 1: code.gs เดิมมี CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID → ใช้ของเดิม if (typeof CONFIG !== 'undefined' && CONFIG && CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID) { return SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); } // Priority 2: code.gs เดิมมี getSpreadsheet() → เรียกใช้ if (typeof getSpreadsheet === 'function') return getSpreadsheet(); // Priority 3: ใช้ V2_CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID ของไฟล์นี้ (self-contained) if (V2_CONFIG && V2_CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID) { return SpreadsheetApp.openById(V2_CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); } throw new Error('Cannot access spreadsheet — set V2_CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID at top of sf_v2_extensions.gs'); } function _v2Now() { if (typeof nowDisplay === 'function') return nowDisplay(); return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'); } // Parse date string (dd/mm/yyyy or yyyy-mm-dd) → Date object function _v2ParseDate(s) { if (!s) return null; if (s instanceof Date) return s; var str = String(s).trim(); // yyyy-mm-dd var m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[1]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return new Date(y, Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])); } // dd/mm/yyyy m = str.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/); if (m) { var y2 = Number(m[3]); if (y2 > 2400) y2 -= 543; return new Date(y2, Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[1])); } return null; } // Date in range [from, to] inclusive (works on dd/mm/yyyy strings) function _v2DateInRange(dateStr, fromISO, toISO) { var d = _v2ParseDate(dateStr); if (!d) return false; if (fromISO) { var from = _v2ParseDate(fromISO); if (from && d < from) return false; } if (toISO) { var to = _v2ParseDate(toISO); if (to && d > to) return false; } return true; } // Get/create sheet helper (use existing if available) function _v2GetOrCreate(name, headers) { if (typeof getOrCreateSheet === 'function') return getOrCreateSheet(name, headers); var ss = _v2SS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(name); if (headers && headers.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } } return sheet; } function _v2SheetToObjects(sheet) { if (typeof sheetToObjects === 'function') return sheetToObjects(sheet); if (!sheet) return []; var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return []; var headers = data[0]; var out = []; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var v = data[i][j]; if (v instanceof Date) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } obj[headers[j]] = v; } var hasValue = false; for (var k in obj) { if (obj[k] !== '' && obj[k] != null) { hasValue = true; break; } } if (hasValue) out.push(obj); } return out; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SECTION 3 — Bank Split (per-bank statement sheets) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Sheet naming: stmts_KTB_2026, stmts_KBANK_2026, stmts_BBL_NW_2026, ... function _v2BankSheetName(bank, year) { var canonical = v2NormalizeBank(bank); if (!canonical) throw new Error('Empty bank name'); return V2_SCHEMA.statements.sheetPrefixPerBank + canonical + '_' + year; } // API: prepare a bank-specific sheet with stmt_uuid column function v2PrepareBankSheet(req) { try { var bank = v2NormalizeBank(req.bank); var year = String(req.year); if (!bank) return _v2Err('Missing/invalid bank: ' + req.bank); if (!year) return _v2Err('Missing year'); var sheetName = _v2BankSheetName(bank, year); var ss = _v2SS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); var uuidCol = V2_SCHEMA.statements.uuidColumn; if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetName); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); return _v2Ok({ created: true, sheet: sheetName, headers: [uuidCol] }); } var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); return _v2Ok({ created: false, added_uuid: true, sheet: sheetName }); } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; if (String(headers[0]).trim() === uuidCol) { return _v2Ok({ created: false, sheet: sheetName, headers: headers }); } sheet.insertColumnBefore(1); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); return _v2Ok({ created: false, added_uuid: true, sheet: sheetName }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message); } } // API: upsert statements to a specific bank sheet function v2UpsertStatementsByBank(req) { try { var bank = v2NormalizeBank(req.bank); var year = String(req.year); var records = req.records || []; var deletes = req.deletes || []; if (!bank) return _v2Err('Missing bank'); if (!year) return _v2Err('Missing year'); var sheetName = _v2BankSheetName(bank, year); var ss = _v2SS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); var uuidCol = V2_SCHEMA.statements.uuidColumn; if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetName); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastCol === 0) { sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue(uuidCol); lastCol = 1; } var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; if (String(headers[0]).trim() !== uuidCol) { return _v2Err('Sheet ' + sheetName + ' col A != ' + uuidCol); } var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var uuidToRow = {}; var existingRows = {}; if (lastRow >= 2) { var allData = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, headers.length).getValues(); for (var i = 0; i < allData.length; i++) { var u = String(allData[i][0] || '').trim(); if (u) { uuidToRow[u] = i + 2; existingRows[i + 2] = allData[i]; } } } var updateCount = 0, skippedCount = 0; var toAppend = []; var updateOps = []; for (var r = 0; r < records.length; r++) { var rec = records[r]; var uuid = String(rec[uuidCol] || '').trim(); if (!uuid) { skippedCount++; continue; } var isUpdate = !!uuidToRow[uuid]; var existing = isUpdate ? existingRows[uuidToRow[uuid]] : null; var rowVals = new Array(headers.length); for (var h = 0; h < headers.length; h++) { var colName = String(headers[h]).trim(); var v = rec[colName]; if (v === undefined || v === null) { rowVals[h] = isUpdate ? existing[h] : ''; } else if (v instanceof Date) { // ⭐ Format Date object → yyyy-mm-dd string (ISO ค.ศ.) rowVals[h] = _formatDateForSheet(v, colName); } else if (typeof v === 'object') { rowVals[h] = JSON.stringify(v); } else if (typeof v === 'boolean') { rowVals[h] = v ? 'Y' : 'N'; } else { // ⭐ String value — normalize date columns (พ.ศ.→ค.ศ., dd/mm→ISO) rowVals[h] = _normalizeDateString(v, colName); } } if (isUpdate) { updateOps.push({ row: uuidToRow[uuid], values: rowVals }); } else { toAppend.push(rowVals); } } for (var u2 = 0; u2 < updateOps.length; u2++) { sheet.getRange(updateOps[u2].row, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([updateOps[u2].values]); updateCount++; } var insertCount = 0; if (toAppend.length > 0) { var startRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1; sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, toAppend.length, headers.length).setValues(toAppend); insertCount = toAppend.length; } var deleteCount = 0; }); for (var dr = 0; dr < delRows.length; dr++) { sheet.deleteRow(delRows[dr]); } deleteCount = delRows.length; } // ⭐ Apply plain-text format to date columns (กัน Sheet auto-parse "01/02" → 2-Jan) try { for (var hi = 0; hi < headers.length; hi++) { var hn = String(headers[hi]).toLowerCase().trim(); if (hn === 'date' || hn === 'pay_date' || hn === 'due_date' || hn === 'transfer_date' || hn === 'trans_date') { var colNum = hi + 1; var lastRowFmt = Math.max(2, sheet.getLastRow()); sheet.getRange(2, colNum, lastRowFmt - 1, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } } catch(_) {} // Audit log _v2WriteAudit({ action: 'upsert_stmts', entity: 'statement', uuid: bank + '_' + year, metadata: { bank: bank, year: year, updated: updateCount, inserted: insertCount, deleted: deleteCount }, user: req.user || 'system', }); return _v2Ok({ sheet: sheetName, bank: bank, year: year, updated: updateCount, inserted: insertCount, deleted: deleteCount, skipped: skippedCount, total_rows: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // API: get statements for one bank function v2GetStatementsByBank(req) { try { var bank = v2NormalizeBank(req.bank); var year = String(req.year); if (!bank) return _v2Err('Missing bank'); if (!year) return _v2Err('Missing year'); var sheetName = _v2BankSheetName(bank, year); var sheet = _v2SS().getSheetByName(sheetName); if (!sheet) return _v2Ok({ rows: [], count: 0, sheet: sheetName }); var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var data = []; for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var obj = { bank: bank }; // inject bank from sheet name for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var name = String(headers[j]).trim(); if (!name) continue; var v = rows[i][j]; if (v instanceof Date) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } obj[name] = v; } if (!obj[V2_SCHEMA.statements.uuidColumn]) continue; data.push(obj); } return _v2Ok({ rows: data, count: data.length, sheet: sheetName, bank: bank }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message); } } // API: get all banks for a year (for refresh) function v2GetAllStatementsByYear(req) { try { var year = String(req.year); if (!year) return _v2Err('Missing year'); var result = {}; var totalCount = 0; V2_SCHEMA.statements.banks.forEach(function(bank) { var resp = v2GetStatementsByBank({ bank: bank, year: year }); var parsed; try { parsed = JSON.parse(resp.getContent()); } catch (e) { parsed = { data: { rows: [] } }; } result[bank] = (parsed.data && parsed.data.rows) || []; totalCount += result[bank].length; }); return _v2Ok({ year: year, banks: result, total: totalCount }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SECTION 4 — Date-range queries (lazy loading) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // API: get bills filtered by date range — much faster than getBillsWithUuid + client filter function v2GetBillsByDateRange(req) { try { var year = String(req.year); for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) { if (String(headers[i]).trim() === 'date') { dateIdx = i; break; } } if (dateIdx < 0) return _v2Err('No `date` column in bills_' + year); var result = []; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { var dateVal = data[r][dateIdx]; if (dateVal instanceof Date) { try { dateVal = Utilities.formatDate(dateVal, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } if (!_v2DateInRange(dateVal, fromISO, toISO)) continue; var obj = {}; for (var h = 0; h < headers.length; h++) { var name = String(headers[h]).trim(); if (!name) continue; var v = data[r][h]; if (v instanceof Date) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } obj[name] = v; } if (!obj[V2_SCHEMA.bills.uuidColumn]) continue; result.push(obj); } return _v2Ok({ bills: result, count: result.length, total_in_year: Math.max(0, lastRow - 1), from: fromISO, to: toISO, }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message); } } // API: convenience — get bills for a single month function v2GetBillsByMonth(req) { var year = String(req.year); var month = String(req.month).padStart(2, '0'); // '01' to '12' if (!year || !month || month.length !== 2) { return _v2Err('Need year + month (e.g. month="04")'); } var lastDay = new Date(Number(year), Number(month), 0).getDate(); return v2GetBillsByDateRange({ year: year, from: year + '-' + month + '-01', to: year + '-' + month + '-' + String(lastDay).padStart(2, '0'), }); } // API: get statements (per bank) by date range function v2GetStatementsByBankAndDateRange(req) { try { var bank = v2NormalizeBank(req.bank); var year = String(req.year); var fromISO = req.from; var toISO = req.to; var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol === 0) return _v2Ok({ statements: [], count: 0, bank: bank }); var data = sheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var dateIdx = -1; for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) { if (String(headers[i]).trim() === 'date') { dateIdx = i; break; } } if (dateIdx < 0) return _v2Err('No date column'); var result = []; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { var dateVal = data[r][dateIdx]; if (dateVal instanceof Date) { try { dateVal = Utilities.formatDate(dateVal, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } if (!_v2DateInRange(dateVal, fromISO, toISO)) continue; var obj = { bank: bank }; for (var h = 0; h < headers.length; h++) { var name = String(headers[h]).trim(); if (!name) continue; var v = data[r][h]; if (v instanceof Date) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'dd/MM/yyyy'); } catch (e) {} } obj[name] = v; } if (!obj[V2_SCHEMA.statements.uuidColumn]) continue; result.push(obj); } return _v2Ok({ statements: result, count: result.length, bank: bank }); } catch (e) { return _v2Err(e.message); } } // API: get meta (locks, summary, cash_count) by date range function v2GetMetaByDateRange(req) { try { var year = String(req.year); var fromISO = req.from; var toISO = req.to; if (!year) return _v2Err('Missing year'); var sheet = _v2SS().getSheetByName('meta_' + year); if (!sheet) return _v2Ok({ rows: [], count: 0 }); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol === 0) return _v2Ok({ rows: [], count: 0 }); } catch (e) {} } if (!_v2DateInRange(dateVal, fromISO, toISO)) continue; } var obj = {}; for (var h2 = 0; h2 < headers.length; h2++) { var name = String(headers[h2]).trim(); if (!name) continue; var v = data[r][h2];
