} if (toAdd.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, toAdd.length).setValues([toAdd]); } } } // Force text format var actualHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); _forcePcTextFormat(sheet, actualHeaders); return { sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET, created: created, headers: PC_FRONT_HEADERS, row_count: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), }; } function _forcePcTextFormat(sheet, headers) { try { var textSet = {}; for (var i = 0; i < PC_FRONT_TEXT_COLS.length; i++) textSet[PC_FRONT_TEXT_COLS[i]] = true; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var h = String(headers[j]).trim(); if (textSet[h]) { sheet.getRange(1, j + 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } } catch (_e) { /* swallow */ } } function _forcePcTextFormatRange(sheet, headers, startRow, numRows) { try { if (numRows <= 0) return; var textSet = {}; for (var i = 0; i < PC_FRONT_TEXT_COLS.length; i++) textSet[PC_FRONT_TEXT_COLS[i]] = true; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var h = String(headers[j]).trim(); if (textSet[h]) { sheet.getRange(startRow, j + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); } } } catch (_e) { /* swallow */ } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // upsertPcBatch — bulk insert/replace by (year, month) of original // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'upsertPcBatch', // year: 2026, month: 1, // original year/month being uploaded // records: [...], // line items (parsed by frontend) // replace: true, // delete existing original_year=Y, original_month=M, status=pending // } // // ⚠ IMPORTANT: replace=true ลบเฉพาะ status='pending' ของ (original_year, original_month) // ที่ตรงกับ batch — เพื่อ preserve submitted/excluded ที่ user ทำไว้แล้ว function upsertPcBatch(p) { try { var year = Number(p.year); var month = Number(p.month); var source = String(p.source || 'front').trim().toLowerCase(); var records = p.records || []; var replace = (p.replace === true || p.replace === 'true'); if (!year || !month) return _uuidErr('Missing year or month'); if (month < 1 || month > 12) return _uuidErr('Invalid month: ' + month); if (source !== 'front' && source !== 'invoice') { return _uuidErr('Invalid source: ' + source + ' (must be "front" or "invoice")'); } if (!Array.isArray(records)) return _uuidErr('records must be an array'); var ss = _uuidSS(); // Auto-migrate schema (append missing columns เช่น submission_id, parent_pc_uuid) _ensurePcSheet(ss); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var actualHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var headerIdx = {}; for (var i = 0; i < actualHeaders.length; i++) { if (actualHeaders[i]) headerIdx[actualHeaders[i]] = i; } _forcePcTextFormat(sheet, actualHeaders); // ── Step 1: Delete existing (filter by source — กัน front upload ลบ invoice หรือกลับกัน) ── var deleted = 0; var preserved = 0; if (replace) { var result = _pcDeletePendingByOriginalPeriod(sheet, actualHeaders, year, month, source); deleted = result.deleted; preserved = result.preserved; } // ── Step 2: Build batch metadata ── var batch = Utilities.getUuid(); var importedAt = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Bangkok', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); // ── Step 3: Build rows ── var rowsToInsert = []; for (var r = 0; r < records.length; r++) { var rec = records[r] || {}; // Force fields from request (authoritative) rec.original_year = year; rec.original_month = month; rec.imported_at = importedAt; rec.import_batch = batch; // Default status = pending (unless explicitly set) if (!rec.status) rec.status = 'pending'; // Auto-generate UUID if missing if (!rec.pc_uuid) rec.pc_uuid = Utilities.getUuid(); rowsToInsert.push(_pcBuildRow(rec, actualHeaders)); } // ── Step 4: Append ── var inserted = 0; if (rowsToInsert.length > 0) { var startRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1; _forcePcTextFormatRange(sheet, actualHeaders, startRow, rowsToInsert.length); sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, rowsToInsert.length, actualHeaders.length).setValues(rowsToInsert); _forcePcTextFormatRange(sheet, actualHeaders, startRow, rowsToInsert.length); inserted = rowsToInsert.length; } return _uuidOk({ year: year, month: month, replace: replace, deleted: deleted, preserved: preserved, inserted: inserted, batch: batch, imported_at: importedAt, total_rows: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } function _pcBuildRow(rec, headers) { var row = new Array(headers.length); for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) { var col = headers[i]; var v = rec[col]; if (v === undefined || v === null) { row[i] = ''; } else if (v instanceof Date) { row[i] = _formatDateForSheet(v, col); } else if (typeof v === 'object') { row[i] = JSON.stringify(v); } else if (typeof v === 'string') { row[i] = _normalizeDateString(v, col); } else { row[i] = v; } } return row; } // Delete only pending rows for (original_year, original_month) — preserve submitted/excluded // Delete only pending rows for (original_year, original_month, source) // — preserve submitted/excluded // — preserve rows ของ source อื่น (e.g. upload invoice ไม่กระทบ front) function _pcDeletePendingByOriginalPeriod(sheet, headers, year, month, source) { var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return { deleted: 0, preserved: 0 }; var oyIdx = headers.indexOf('original_year'); var omIdx = headers.indexOf('original_month'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var srcIdx = headers.indexOf('source'); if (oyIdx < 0 || omIdx < 0 || stIdx < 0) { throw new Error('Sheet missing required columns: original_year/original_month/status'); } // Note: srcIdx might be -1 in legacy schema — treat as 'front' default source = String(source || 'front').toLowerCase(); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, headers.length).getValues(); // Partition: keep rows ที่ไม่ใช่ pending+matched_period+matched_source // Strategy: rewrite ทั้ง data block รอบเดียว (เร็วกว่า deleteRow ทีละ row 100x) var keepRows = []; var deletedCount = 0; var preservedCount = 0; // submitted/excluded ของ THIS source+period for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var y = Number(data[i][oyIdx]); var m = Number(data[i][omIdx]); var st = String(data[i][stIdx] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); var rowSource = (srcIdx >= 0) ? String(data[i][srcIdx] || 'front').trim().toLowerCase() : 'front'; // legacy rows default to front var isThisPeriod = (y === year && m === month); var isThisSource = (rowSource === source); if (isThisPeriod && isThisSource) { // Same period AND same source — check status if (st === 'pending' || st === '') { // Drop (replace target) deletedCount++; } else { // Preserve (submitted/excluded) — same period, same source keepRows.push(data[i]); preservedCount++; } } else { // Different period OR different source — always keep keepRows.push(data[i]); } } // Rewrite strategy: // 1) clearContent() ทั้งบล็อก data // 2) setValues() เขียน keepRows กลับด้านบน // 3) deleteRows() ลบส่วนเกิน — สำคัญมาก! ถ้าไม่ลบ insert ใหม่จะไปต่อท้าย // ทำให้ data กระจัดกระจาย (lastRow ยังเป็นเลขเดิม) if (deletedCount > 0) { var totalDataRows = lastRow - 1; var dataRange = sheet.getRange(2, 1, totalDataRows, headers.length); dataRange.clearContent(); if (keepRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, keepRows.length, headers.length).setValues(keepRows); } // ลบ rows ส่วนเกินที่ว่างเปล่า — เพื่อให้ getLastRow() กลับมาตรงกับจำนวนจริง // และ insert ใหม่จะไปที่ตำแหน่งถูกต้อง (ต่อจาก keepRows) var emptyRowsCount = totalDataRows - keepRows.length; if (emptyRowsCount > 0) { // deleteRows: starting row, how many to delete // ลบจาก row หลัง keepRows (ถ้า keepRows = 1000, ลบจาก row 1002 ลงไป) var deleteStartRow = 2 + keepRows.length; sheet.deleteRows(deleteStartRow, emptyRowsCount); } } return { deleted: deletedCount, preserved: preservedCount }; } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // getPcLineitems — fetch by view-month logic // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'getPcLineitems', // year: 2026, months: [1,2,3] // "เดือนที่ดู" — multi-month support // includeStatuses: ['pending','submitted','excluded'] // optional, default all // } // // View-month rule (per user spec): // - status='pending' → match original_year/month // - status='submitted' → match submit_year/month // - status='excluded' → match original_year/month function getPcLineitems(p) { try { var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!sheet) return _uuidOk({ rows: [], count: 0, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET }); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol === 0) return _uuidOk({ rows: [], count: 0, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); // Parse filter var year = p.year ? Number(p.year) : null; var months = _parseNumberArray(p.months); var monthSet = months.length ? _toSet(months) : null; var statuses = p.includeStatuses || p.statuses; var statusSet = null; if (Array.isArray(statuses) && statuses.length > 0) { statusSet = {}; for (var i = 0; i < statuses.length; i++) { statusSet[String(statuses[i]).toLowerCase()] = true; } } else if (typeof statuses === 'string') { try { var arr = JSON.parse(statuses); if (Array.isArray(arr)) { statusSet = {}; for (var k = 0; k < arr.length; k++) statusSet[String(arr[k]).toLowerCase()] = true; } } catch (_e) { statusSet = {}; statusSet[String(statuses).toLowerCase()] = true; } } var oyIdx = headers.indexOf('original_year'); var omIdx = headers.indexOf('original_month'); var syIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_year'); var smIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_month'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var rows = []; for (var r = 0; r < data.length; r++) { var st = String(data[r][stIdx] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!st) st = 'pending'; // Status filter if (statusSet && !statusSet[st]) continue; // Period filter — view-month rule var matchYear, matchMonth; if (st === 'submitted') { matchYear = Number(data[r][syIdx]); matchMonth = Number(data[r][smIdx]); } else { // pending or excluded → match original matchYear = Number(data[r][oyIdx]); matchMonth = Number(data[r][omIdx]); } if (year !== null && matchYear !== year) continue; if (monthSet && !monthSet[matchMonth]) continue; // Build object var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { var col = headers[j]; if (!col) continue; var v = data[r][j]; if (v instanceof Date) { try { v = _formatDateForSheet(v, col); } catch (e) { try { v = Utilities.formatDate(v, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } catch (_e) { v = String(v); } } } obj[col] = v; } if (!obj.pc_uuid) continue; // Normalize status for output obj.status = st; rows.push(obj); } return _uuidOk({ rows: rows, count: rows.length, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // updatePcStatus — bulk update status + submit info // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'updatePcStatus', // uuids: ['uuid1', 'uuid2', ...], // newStatus: 'submitted'|'pending'|'excluded', // submitYear: 2026, // required if newStatus='submitted' // submitMonth: 2, // required if newStatus='submitted' // submitDate: '2026-02-15' // optional, default = first day of submit month // } // // Behavior: // - newStatus='submitted' → set submit_year/month/date + submitted_at = now // - newStatus='pending' → clear submit_year/month/date/submitted_at // - newStatus='excluded' → clear submit fields (excluded follows original) function updatePcStatus(p) { try { var uuids = p.uuids || []; var newStatus = String(p.newStatus || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!Array.isArray(uuids)) { // Try to parse JSON string if (typeof p.uuids === 'string') { try { uuids = JSON.parse(p.uuids); } catch(e) { return _uuidErr('Invalid uuids format'); } } else { return _uuidErr('uuids must be an array'); } } if (uuids.length === 0) return _uuidErr('Empty uuids list'); if (['pending', 'submitted', 'excluded'].indexOf(newStatus) < 0) { return _uuidErr('Invalid newStatus: ' + newStatus); } // Validate submit info if needed var sy = null, sm = null, sd = ''; if (newStatus === 'submitted') { sy = Number(p.submitYear); sm = Number(p.submitMonth); if (!sy || !sm || sm < 1 || sm > 12) { return _uuidErr('submitted requires submitYear + submitMonth (1-12)'); } sd = String(p.submitDate || '').trim(); if (!sd) { // Default: first day of submit month sd = sy + '-' + String(sm).padStart(2, '0') + '-01'; } } var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!sheet) return _uuidErr('Sheet not found: ' + PC_FRONT_SHEET); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ updated: 0, notFound: uuids.length }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var uuidIdx = headers.indexOf('pc_uuid'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var syIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_year'); var smIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_month'); var sdIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_date'); var saIdx = headers.indexOf('submitted_at'); if (uuidIdx < 0 || stIdx < 0) { return _uuidErr('Sheet missing pc_uuid or status column'); } _forcePcTextFormat(sheet, headers); // Build uuid set var uuidSet = {}; for (var u = 0; u < uuids.length; u++) { uuidSet[String(uuids[u]).trim()] = true; } var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var updated = 0; var nowIso = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Bangkok', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); for (var r = 0; r < data.length; r++) { var uuid = String(data[r][uuidIdx] || '').trim(); if (!uuidSet[uuid]) continue; var sheetRow = r + 2; // Update status sheet.getRange(sheetRow, stIdx + 1).setValue(newStatus); if (newStatus === 'submitted') { if (syIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, syIdx + 1).setValue(sy); if (smIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, smIdx + 1).setValue(sm); if (sdIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, sdIdx + 1).setNumberFormat('@').setValue(sd); if (saIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, saIdx + 1).setNumberFormat('@').setValue(nowIso); } else { // pending or excluded — clear submit fields if (syIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, syIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (smIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, smIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (sdIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, sdIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (saIdx >= 0) sheet.getRange(sheetRow, saIdx + 1).setValue(''); } updated++; } return _uuidOk({ updated: updated, notFound: uuids.length - updated, newStatus: newStatus, submitYear: sy, submitMonth: sm, submitDate: sd, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // PC SUBMISSIONS — "ใบส่ง PC" (1 brand × 1 target month = 1 submission) // Mixes Front + Invoice in same submission, but tracked separately // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ var PC_SUBMISSIONS_SHEET = 'pc_submissions'; var PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS = [ 'submission_id', // PK — generated: 'sub_{brandSlug}_{YYYY-MM}' 'brand', // 'PHILIPS'/'Zeberg'/'For You'/'Mitsubishi'/'SIMON' 'target_year', // ปลายทาง (ปีที่ส่ง) 'target_month', // ปลายทาง (เดือนที่ส่ง) // Aggregated totals (denormalized — refreshed on every update) 'total_amount', // รวมทั้ง front + invoice 'total_lines', 'total_bills', 'front_amount', 'front_lines', 'invoice_amount', 'invoice_lines', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'notes', // optional ]; var PC_SUBMISSIONS_TEXT_COLS = [ 'submission_id', 'brand', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'notes', ]; // Internal: get or create pc_submissions sheet function _pcSubGetSheet() { var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_SUBMISSIONS_SHEET); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(PC_SUBMISSIONS_SHEET); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS.length).setValues([PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS]); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); // Force text format PC_SUBMISSIONS_TEXT_COLS.forEach(function(col) { var idx = PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS.indexOf(col); if (idx >= 0) sheet.getRange(2, idx + 1, 1000, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); }); } return sheet; } // Generate submission_id from (brand, year, month) function _pcSubGenId(brand, year, month) { var b = String(brand || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''); var ym = year + '-' + String(month).padStart(2, '0'); return 'sub_' + b + '_' + ym; } // Read all submissions as objects function _pcSubReadAll() { var sheet = _pcSubGetSheet(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return []; var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var data = sheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var result = []; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) { obj[headers[j]] = data[i][j]; } if (obj.submission_id) result.push(obj); } return result; } // Find submission by id — returns {row: number, data: object} or null function _pcSubFindById(submissionId) { var sheet = _pcSubGetSheet(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return null; var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); var data = sheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var idIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); if (idIdx < 0) return null; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][idIdx]).trim() === submissionId) { var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) obj[headers[j]] = data[i][j]; return { row: i + 1, data: obj, headers: headers }; } } return null; } // Recompute totals for a submission by scanning pc_lineitems function _pcSubRecomputeTotals(submissionId) { var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); var totals = { total_amount: 0, total_lines: 0, total_bills: 0, front_amount: 0, front_lines: 0, invoice_amount: 0, invoice_lines: 0, }; if (!pcSheet) return totals; var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return totals; var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var subIdIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); var srcIdx = headers.indexOf('source'); var totalIdx = headers.indexOf('total'); var billNoIdx = headers.indexOf('bill_no'); if (subIdIdx < 0) return totals; var billsSeen = {}; // de-dup bills per source for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][subIdIdx]).trim() !== submissionId) continue; var amt = Number(data[i][totalIdx]) || 0; var src = (srcIdx >= 0) ? String(data[i][srcIdx] || 'front').toLowerCase() : 'front'; var billNo = (billNoIdx >= 0) ? String(data[i][billNoIdx] || '') : ''; var billKey = src + '::' + billNo; totals.total_amount += amt; totals.total_lines++; if (!billsSeen[billKey]) { billsSeen[billKey] = true; totals.total_bills++; } if (src === 'invoice') { totals.invoice_amount += amt; totals.invoice_lines++; } else { totals.front_amount += amt; totals.front_lines++; } } return totals; } // Update submission row totals + updated_at function _pcSubUpdateRow(submissionId) { var found = _pcSubFindById(submissionId); if (!found) return null; var totals = _pcSubRecomputeTotals(submissionId); var sheet = _pcSubGetSheet(); var headers = found.headers; var row = found.row; var updates = { total_amount: totals.total_amount, total_lines: totals.total_lines, total_bills: totals.total_bills, front_amount: totals.front_amount, front_lines: totals.front_lines, invoice_amount: totals.invoice_amount, invoice_lines: totals.invoice_lines, updated_at: _isoNowBangkok(), }; Object.keys(updates).forEach(function(k) { var idx = headers.indexOf(k); if (idx >= 0) sheet.getRange(row, idx + 1).setValue(updates[k]); }); return updates; } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // createOrAddPcSubmission — สร้างใบใหม่ หรือเพิ่มเข้าใบเดิม (idempotent) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'createOrAddPcSubmission', // brand: 'Zeberg', // target_year: 2026, target_month: 2, // uuids: ['pc_uuid1', 'pc_uuid2', ...], // pc_lineitem uuids ที่จะเพิ่ม // notes: 'optional' // } // Returns: { submission_id, created (bool), totals: {...} } function createOrAddPcSubmission(p) { try { var brand = String(p.brand || '').trim(); var targetYear = Number(p.target_year); var targetMonth = Number(p.target_month); var uuids = Array.isArray(p.uuids) ? p.uuids : []; if (!brand) return _uuidErr('Missing brand'); if (!targetYear || !targetMonth) return _uuidErr('Missing target year/month'); if (uuids.length === 0) return _uuidErr('No UUIDs provided'); var subId = _pcSubGenId(brand, targetYear, targetMonth); var subSheet = _pcSubGetSheet(); var existing = _pcSubFindById(subId); var nowIso = _isoNowBangkok(); var created = false; // Auto-migrate pc_lineitems schema (เพื่อให้ submission_id column มีอยู่) var ss = _uuidSS(); _ensurePcSheet(ss); if (!existing) { // Create new submission row var newRow = new Array(PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS.length).fill(''); var setCol = function(name, val) { var idx = PC_SUBMISSIONS_HEADERS.indexOf(name); if (idx >= 0) newRow[idx] = val; }; setCol('submission_id', subId); setCol('brand', brand); setCol('target_year', targetYear); setCol('target_month', targetMonth); setCol('total_amount', 0); setCol('total_lines', 0); setCol('total_bills', 0); setCol('front_amount', 0); setCol('front_lines', 0); setCol('invoice_amount', 0); setCol('invoice_lines', 0); setCol('created_at', nowIso); setCol('updated_at', nowIso); setCol('notes', String(p.notes || '')); subSheet.appendRow(newRow); created = true; } // Update pc_lineitems: status='submitted', submit_year/month, submission_id var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!pcSheet) return _uuidErr('pc_lineitems sheet not found'); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidErr('No PC line items'); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var uuidIdx = headers.indexOf('pc_uuid'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var syIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_year'); var smIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_month'); var sdIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_date'); var subAtIdx = headers.indexOf('submitted_at'); var subIdIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); if (uuidIdx < 0 || stIdx < 0 || subIdIdx < 0) { return _uuidErr('Sheet missing required columns'); } var uuidSet = {}; uuids.forEach(function(u) { uuidSet[u] = true; }); var updated = 0; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { var u = String(data[i][uuidIdx]).trim(); if (!uuidSet[u]) continue; var rowNum = i + 1; pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, stIdx + 1).setValue('submitted'); if (syIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, syIdx + 1).setValue(targetYear); if (smIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, smIdx + 1).setValue(targetMonth); if (sdIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, sdIdx + 1).setValue(nowIso.split('T')[0]); if (subAtIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subAtIdx + 1).setValue(nowIso); pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subIdIdx + 1).setValue(subId); updated++; } var totals = _pcSubUpdateRow(subId); return _uuidOk({ submission_id: subId, brand: brand, target_year: targetYear, target_month: targetMonth, created: created, updated_lines: updated, totals: totals, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // removeFromPcSubmission — เอาบิลออกจากใบ → status คืน pending // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'removeFromPcSubmission', // submission_id: 'sub_zeberg_2026-02', // uuids: ['pc_uuid1', ...] // } function removeFromPcSubmission(p) { try { var subId = String(p.submission_id || '').trim(); var uuids = Array.isArray(p.uuids) ? p.uuids : []; if (!subId) return _uuidErr('Missing submission_id'); if (uuids.length === 0) return _uuidErr('No UUIDs'); var found = _pcSubFindById(subId); if (!found) return _uuidErr('Submission not found: ' + subId); var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidErr('No PC line items'); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var uuidIdx = headers.indexOf('pc_uuid'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var syIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_year'); var smIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_month'); var sdIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_date'); var subAtIdx = headers.indexOf('submitted_at'); var subIdIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); var uuidSet = {}; uuids.forEach(function(u) { uuidSet[u] = true; }); var updated = 0; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { var u = String(data[i][uuidIdx]).trim(); var rowSub = String(data[i][subIdIdx] || '').trim(); if (!uuidSet[u] || rowSub !== subId) continue; var rowNum = i + 1; pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, stIdx + 1).setValue('pending'); if (syIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, syIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (smIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, smIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (sdIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, sdIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (subAtIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subAtIdx + 1).setValue(''); pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subIdIdx + 1).setValue(''); updated++; } // Recompute totals; if 0 → delete submission row var totals = _pcSubRecomputeTotals(subId); if (totals.total_lines === 0) { // Auto-delete empty submission _pcSubGetSheet().deleteRow(found.row); return _uuidOk({ submission_id: subId, removed: updated, deleted_submission: true }); } else { _pcSubUpdateRow(subId); return _uuidOk({ submission_id: subId, removed: updated, totals: totals }); } } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // listPcSubmissions — list ใบส่งทั้งหมด พร้อม filter // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'listPcSubmissions', // year: 2026 (optional), month: 2 (optional), brand: 'Zeberg' (optional) // } function listPcSubmissions(p) { try { var fy = p.year ? Number(p.year) : null; var fm = p.month ? Number(p.month) : null; var fb = p.brand ? String(p.brand).trim() : null; var all = _pcSubReadAll(); var filtered = all.filter(function(s) { if (fy && Number(s.target_year) !== fy) return false; if (fm && Number(s.target_month) !== fm) return false; if (fb && fb !== 'all' && String(s.brand) !== fb) return false; return true; }); // Sort: target_year desc, target_month desc, brand asc filtered.sort(function(a, b) { if (Number(a.target_year) !== Number(b.target_year)) return Number(b.target_year) - Number(a.target_year); if (Number(a.target_month) !== Number(b.target_month)) return Number(b.target_month) - Number(a.target_month); return String(a.brand).localeCompare(String(b.brand)); }); return _uuidOk({ submissions: filtered, total: filtered.length }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // getPcSubmissionDetail — รายการบิลทั้งหมดของ 1 ใบ // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'getPcSubmissionDetail', submission_id: '...' } function getPcSubmissionDetail(p) { try { var subId = String(p.submission_id || '').trim(); if (!subId) return _uuidErr('Missing submission_id'); var found = _pcSubFindById(subId); if (!found) return _uuidErr('Submission not found'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!pcSheet) return _uuidErr('pc_lineitems not found'); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ submission: found.data, lineitems: [] }); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var subIdIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); var lineitems = []; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][subIdIdx]).trim() !== subId) continue; var obj = {}; for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) obj[headers[j]] = data[i][j]; lineitems.push(obj); } return _uuidOk({ submission: found.data, lineitems: lineitems }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // deletePcSubmission — ลบใบส่งทั้งใบ → คืน pending ทุก record // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'deletePcSubmission', submission_id: '...' } function deletePcSubmission(p) { try { var subId = String(p.submission_id || '').trim(); if (!subId) return _uuidErr('Missing submission_id'); var found = _pcSubFindById(subId); if (!found) return _uuidErr('Submission not found'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidErr('No data'); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var syIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_year'); var smIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_month'); var sdIdx = headers.indexOf('submit_date'); var subAtIdx = headers.indexOf('submitted_at'); var subIdIdx = headers.indexOf('submission_id'); var resetCount = 0; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][subIdIdx]).trim() !== subId) continue; var rowNum = i + 1; pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, stIdx + 1).setValue('pending'); if (syIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, syIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (smIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, smIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (sdIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, sdIdx + 1).setValue(''); if (subAtIdx >= 0) pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subAtIdx + 1).setValue(''); pcSheet.getRange(rowNum, subIdIdx + 1).setValue(''); resetCount++; } // Delete submission row _pcSubGetSheet().deleteRow(found.row); return _uuidOk({ deleted_submission: subId, reset_lineitems: resetCount }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // PC SPLIT — แบ่งบิลเป็นหลายส่วน (manual) // 1 record qty=60 → 3 records qty=20 each // parent_pc_uuid + split_index ใช้ track relationship // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // splitPcLineitem — แบ่ง 1 record เป็น N records // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'splitPcLineitem', // pc_uuid: 'xxx', // record ที่จะ split // splits: [{qty: 20}, {qty: 20}, {qty: 20}] // จำนวนแต่ละส่วน // } // Returns: { // parent_uuid: 'xxx', // new_uuids: ['uuid1', 'uuid2', 'uuid3'], // total_qty: 60, // total_amount: 120000 // } function splitPcLineitem(p) { try { var pcUuid = String(p.pc_uuid || '').trim(); var splits = Array.isArray(p.splits) ? p.splits : []; if (!pcUuid) return _uuidErr('Missing pc_uuid'); if (splits.length < 2) return _uuidErr('Must split into at least 2 parts'); var ss = _uuidSS(); _ensurePcSheet(ss); // Auto-migrate schema var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!pcSheet) return _uuidErr('pc_lineitems sheet not found'); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidErr('No data'); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var idx = {}; for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) idx[headers[i]] = i; // Required columns var requiredCols = ['pc_uuid','qty','price','total','status','parent_pc_uuid','split_index']; for (var k = 0; k < requiredCols.length; k++) { if (idx[requiredCols[k]] === undefined) { return _uuidErr('Sheet missing column: ' + requiredCols[k]); } } // Find target row var targetRow = -1; var targetData = null; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][idx.pc_uuid]).trim() === pcUuid) { targetRow = i + 1; targetData = data[i]; break; } } if (targetRow < 0) return _uuidErr('Record not found: ' + pcUuid); // Validate: must be pending + not already split var status = String(targetData[idx.status] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (status !== 'pending' && status !== '') { return _uuidErr('สามารถ split ได้เฉพาะ records ที่เป็น pending (ปัจจุบัน: ' + status + ')'); } var parentUuid = String(targetData[idx.parent_pc_uuid] || '').trim(); if (parentUuid) { return _uuidErr('Record นี้เป็น split อยู่แล้ว — ต้อง unsplit ก่อน'); } // Validate split totals = original qty (sign-aware: R2 มี qty ลบ) var origQty = Number(targetData[idx.qty]) || 0; var origPrice = Number(targetData[idx.price]) || 0; var origTotal = Number(targetData[idx.total]) || 0; var isRefund = origQty < 0; var sumSplitQty = 0; for (var j = 0; j < splits.length; j++) { var sq = Number(splits[j].qty) || 0; if (sq === 0) return _uuidErr('qty ห้ามเป็น 0'); if (isRefund && sq > 0) return _uuidErr('บิลคืน (R2): qty ทุกส่วนต้องเป็นลบ'); if (!isRefund && sq < 0) return _uuidErr('บิลขาย (S2): qty ทุกส่วนต้องเป็นบวก'); sumSplitQty += sq; } // Allow small floating point tolerance for qty if (Math.abs(sumSplitQty - origQty) > 0.001) { return _uuidErr('รวมจำนวน split (' + sumSplitQty + ') ไม่เท่ากับจำนวนเดิม (' + origQty + ')'); } // Compute price per unit (preserve original price) // Use original total / qty to handle returns (negative qty → negative total preserved) var pricePerUnit = origQty !== 0 ? (origTotal / origQty) : origPrice; // Build N new records (clone target row, override qty/total/uuid/parent/index) var newRows = []; var newUuids = []; var nowIso = _isoNowBangkok(); for (var s = 0; s < splits.length; s++) { var newRow = targetData.slice(); // clone var newUuid = Utilities.getUuid(); newUuids.push(newUuid); var splitQty = Number(splits[s].qty) || 0; var splitTotal = Math.round(splitQty * pricePerUnit * 100) / 100; newRow[idx.pc_uuid] = newUuid; newRow[idx.qty] = splitQty; newRow[idx.total] = splitTotal; newRow[idx.parent_pc_uuid] = pcUuid; // shed parent newRow[idx.split_index] = s + 1; // 1-indexed // submission tracking — clear (split records ต้องเป็น pending fresh) if (idx.status !== undefined) newRow[idx.status] = 'pending'; if (idx.submit_year !== undefined) newRow[idx.submit_year] = ''; if (idx.submit_month !== undefined) newRow[idx.submit_month] = ''; if (idx.submit_date !== undefined) newRow[idx.submit_date] = ''; if (idx.submitted_at !== undefined) newRow[idx.submitted_at] = ''; if (idx.submission_id !== undefined) newRow[idx.submission_id] = ''; // Update timestamp if (idx.imported_at !== undefined) newRow[idx.imported_at] = nowIso; newRows.push(newRow); } // Atomic operation: // 1. Append N new rows // 2. Delete original row if (newRows.length > 0) { var startRow = pcSheet.getLastRow() + 1; pcSheet.getRange(startRow, 1, newRows.length, headers.length).setValues(newRows); } pcSheet.deleteRow(targetRow); return _uuidOk({ parent_uuid: pcUuid, new_uuids: newUuids, original_qty: origQty, original_total: origTotal, splits: splits.length, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // unsplitPcLineitems — รวม split siblings กลับเป็น record เดียว // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { // action: 'unsplitPcLineitems', // pc_uuid: 'xxx' // any of the split records (จะใช้ parent_pc_uuid หา siblings) // } // Returns: { merged_uuid, parent_uuid, removed_count, total_qty, total_amount } function unsplitPcLineitems(p) { try { var pcUuid = String(p.pc_uuid || '').trim(); if (!pcUuid) return _uuidErr('Missing pc_uuid'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!pcSheet) return _uuidErr('pc_lineitems sheet not found'); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidErr('No data'); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var idx = {}; for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) idx[headers[i]] = i; if (idx.parent_pc_uuid === undefined) return _uuidErr('Sheet missing parent_pc_uuid column'); // Find the given record + its parent_pc_uuid var targetParent = ''; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][idx.pc_uuid]).trim() === pcUuid) { targetParent = String(data[i][idx.parent_pc_uuid] || '').trim(); break; } } if (!targetParent) return _uuidErr('Record นี้ไม่ใช่ split (ไม่มี parent_pc_uuid)'); // Find ALL siblings (records with same parent_pc_uuid) var siblings = []; var siblingRowNums = []; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][idx.parent_pc_uuid] || '').trim() === targetParent) { siblings.push(data[i]); siblingRowNums.push(i + 1); } } if (siblings.length === 0) return _uuidErr('ไม่พบ split siblings'); // Validate: ทุก sibling ต้องเป็น pending (ห้ามมีตัวที่ submitted/excluded) for (var k = 0; k < siblings.length; k++) { var st = String(siblings[k][idx.status] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (st && st !== 'pending') { return _uuidErr('ไม่สามารถ unsplit ได้ — ' + (k+1) + '/' + siblings.length + ' records อยู่ในสถานะ "' + st + '" (ต้องเป็น pending ทั้งหมด)'); } } // Sum all siblings var totalQty = 0, totalAmount = 0; siblings.forEach(function(r) { totalQty += Number(r[idx.qty]) || 0; totalAmount += Number(r[idx.total]) || 0; }); totalAmount = Math.round(totalAmount * 100) / 100; // Build merged row from first sibling (preserves all immutable fields) var mergedRow = siblings[0].slice(); mergedRow[idx.pc_uuid] = targetParent; // restore original uuid mergedRow[idx.qty] = totalQty; mergedRow[idx.total] = totalAmount; mergedRow[idx.parent_pc_uuid] = ''; // clear split markers mergedRow[idx.split_index] = ''; if (idx.status !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.status] = 'pending'; if (idx.submit_year !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.submit_year] = ''; if (idx.submit_month !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.submit_month] = ''; if (idx.submit_date !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.submit_date] = ''; if (idx.submitted_at !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.submitted_at] = ''; if (idx.submission_id !== undefined) mergedRow[idx.submission_id] = ''; // Delete all siblings (bottom-up to avoid index shift) siblingRowNums.sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; }); for (var d = 0; d < siblingRowNums.length; d++) { pcSheet.deleteRow(siblingRowNums[d]); } // Append merged row pcSheet.appendRow(mergedRow); return _uuidOk({ merged_uuid: targetParent, parent_uuid: targetParent, removed_count: siblings.length, total_qty: totalQty, total_amount: totalAmount, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // countPcSplitsByPeriod — count split records ใน period (สำหรับ re-upload warning) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'countPcSplitsByPeriod', year: 2026, month: 1, source: 'front' } function countPcSplitsByPeriod(p) { try { var year = Number(p.year); var month = Number(p.month); var source = String(p.source || 'front').toLowerCase(); if (!year || !month) return _uuidErr('Missing year or month'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var pcSheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!pcSheet) return _uuidOk({ count: 0 }); var lastRow = pcSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ count: 0 }); var lastCol = pcSheet.getLastColumn(); var data = pcSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, lastCol).getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var oyIdx = headers.indexOf('original_year'); var omIdx = headers.indexOf('original_month'); var srcIdx = headers.indexOf('source'); var parentIdx = headers.indexOf('parent_pc_uuid'); if (parentIdx < 0) return _uuidOk({ count: 0 }); if (oyIdx < 0 || omIdx < 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet missing columns'); var count = 0; var billsAffected = {}; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (Number(data[i][oyIdx]) !== year) continue; if (Number(data[i][omIdx]) !== month) continue; var rowSrc = (srcIdx >= 0) ? String(data[i][srcIdx] || 'front').toLowerCase() : 'front'; if (rowSrc !== source) continue; var parent = String(data[i][parentIdx] || '').trim(); if (parent) { count++; billsAffected[parent] = true; } } return _uuidOk({ count: count, bills_affected: Object.keys(billsAffected).length, year: year, month: month, source: source, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message); } } // Helper: Bangkok ISO timestamp function _isoNowBangkok() { var d = new Date(); var off = '+07:00'; // Adjust to Bangkok (UTC+7) regardless of script timezone var bkk = new Date(d.getTime() + (7 * 3600 * 1000)); var Y = bkk.getUTCFullYear(); var M = String(bkk.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); var D = String(bkk.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0'); var h = String(bkk.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0'); var m = String(bkk.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); var s = String(bkk.getUTCSeconds()).padStart(2, '0'); return Y + '-' + M + '-' + D + 'T' + h + ':' + m + ':' + s + off; } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // deletePcByBatch — delete by import_batch (rollback an upload) // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'deletePcByBatch', batch: 'uuid' } function deletePcByBatch(p) { try { var batch = String(p.batch || '').trim(); if (!batch) return _uuidErr('Missing batch'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!sheet) return _uuidOk({ deleted: 0, message: 'Sheet not found' }); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ deleted: 0 }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var batchIdx = headers.indexOf('import_batch'); if (batchIdx < 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet missing import_batch column'); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var keepRows = []; var deletedCount = 0; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][batchIdx] || '').trim() === batch) { deletedCount++; } else { keepRows.push(data[i]); } } // Rewrite: clear → setValues → deleteRows ส่วนเกิน if (deletedCount > 0) { var totalDataRows = lastRow - 1; var dataRange = sheet.getRange(2, 1, totalDataRows, lastCol); dataRange.clearContent(); if (keepRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(2, 1, keepRows.length, lastCol).setValues(keepRows); } // ลบ rows ส่วนเกิน var emptyRowsCount = totalDataRows - keepRows.length; if (emptyRowsCount > 0) { sheet.deleteRows(2 + keepRows.length, emptyRowsCount); } } return _uuidOk({ deleted: deletedCount, batch: batch, total_rows: Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // checkPcPeriod — check existing data for an upload preview // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Payload: { action: 'checkPcPeriod', year: 2026, month: 1 } // // Returns count breakdown by status for (original_year, original_month): // { pending, submitted, excluded, total } // // Frontend uses this to show overwrite warning: // "บิล ม.ค. มี pending 250 / submitted 50 / excluded 10 // → จะลบ pending 250 แทนที่ด้วยข้อมูลใหม่ // preserve submitted+excluded 60" function checkPcPeriod(p) { try { var year = Number(p.year); var month = Number(p.month); var source = p.source ? String(p.source).trim().toLowerCase() : null; // optional if (!year || !month) return _uuidErr('Missing year or month'); var ss = _uuidSS(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(PC_FRONT_SHEET); if (!sheet) return _uuidOk({ pending: 0, submitted: 0, excluded: 0, total: 0, year: year, month: month, source: source, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET, }); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow < 2) return _uuidOk({ pending: 0, submitted: 0, excluded: 0, total: 0, year: year, month: month, source: source, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET, }); var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function(h) { return String(h).trim(); }); var oyIdx = headers.indexOf('original_year'); var omIdx = headers.indexOf('original_month'); var stIdx = headers.indexOf('status'); var srcIdx = headers.indexOf('source'); if (oyIdx < 0 || omIdx < 0 || stIdx < 0) return _uuidErr('Sheet missing required columns'); var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); var counts = { pending: 0, submitted: 0, excluded: 0, total: 0 }; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var y = Number(data[i][oyIdx]); var m = Number(data[i][omIdx]); if (y !== year || m !== month) continue; // Filter by source if specified if (source) { var rowSource = (srcIdx >= 0) ? String(data[i][srcIdx] || 'front').trim().toLowerCase() : 'front'; if (rowSource !== source) continue; } var st = String(data[i][stIdx] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!st) st = 'pending'; if (counts[st] !== undefined) counts[st]++; counts.total++; } return _uuidOk({ pending: counts.pending, submitted: counts.submitted, excluded: counts.excluded, total: counts.total, year: year, month: month, source: source, sheet: PC_FRONT_SHEET, }); } catch (e) { return _uuidErr(e.message + ' | ' + (e.stack || '')); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // BILLING NOTES MODULE (ใบวางบิล + ติดตามหนี้) — 4/5/26 // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Sheets: // billing_notes — header แต่ละใบวางบิล (1 row = 1 ใบ) // billing_payments — log การรับชำระ (1 row = 1 รายการชำระ) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const BILLING_NOTES_SHEET = 'billing_notes'; const BILLING_PAYMENTS_SHEET = 'billing_payments'; const BILLING_NOTES_HEADERS = [ 'billing_uuid', // PK 'billing_no', // SB001YY-XXXXXX 'billing_date', // ISO yyyy-mm-dd 'due_date', // ISO yyyy-mm-dd 'cust_code', // รหัสลูกค้า 'cust_name', 'cust_address', 'cust_tax_id', 'cust_phone', 'bill_uuids', // JSON array 'bills_snapshot', // JSON array of {bill_uuid, bill_no, date, amount, type, station, wht_amount} 'total_amount', 'paid_amount', 'status', // pending | partial | paid | cancelled 'last_payment_date', 'remarks', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', ]; const BILLING_PAYMENTS_HEADERS = [ 'payment_uuid', 'billing_uuid', 'billing_no', 'cust_code', 'cust_name', 'payment_date', 'amount', 'wht_amount', 'fee_amount', 'payment_method', // Cash | Transfer | Cheque | Card 'reference', 'remarks', 'action_by', 'action_at', ]; // ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function _billingNowIso() { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); } function _billingTodayIso() { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } function _billingUuid() { return Utilities.getUuid(); } function _billingNormalizeIso(d) { if (!d) return ''; if (d instanceof Date) return Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var s = String(d).trim(); var m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (m) { var y = Number(m[1]); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return y + '-' + m[2] + '-' + m[3]; } var p = s.split('/'); if (p.length === 3) { var dd = String(p[0]).padStart(2,'0'); var mm = String(p[1]).padStart(2,'0'); var yy = Number(p[2]); if (yy > 2400) yy -= 543; return yy + '-' + mm + '-' + dd; } return s; } function _billingDefaultDueDate(billingDateIso) { var iso = _billingNormalizeIso(billingDateIso) || _billingTodayIso(); var parts = iso.split('-'); var y = Number(parts[0]); var m = Number(parts[1]); m += 1; if (m > 12) { m = 1; y += 1; } return y + '-' + String(m).padStart(2,'0') + '-05'; } function _billingFormatNoYY(yearFull) { var y = Number(yearFull); if (y > 2400) y -= 543; return String(y % 100).padStart(2,'0'); } function _getBillingNotesSheet() { return getOrCreateSheet(BILLING_NOTES_SHEET, BILLING_NOTES_HEADERS); } function _getBillingPaymentsSheet() { return getOrCreateSheet(BILLING_PAYMENTS_SHEET, BILLING_PAYMENTS_HEADERS); } // ── Endpoints ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── function getNextBillingNo(req) { var year = req && req.year ? Number(req.year) : Number(_billingTodayIso().split('-')[0]); var yy = _billingFormatNoYY(year); var prefix = 'SB001' + yy + '-'; var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var data = sheetToObjects(sheet); var maxNum = 0; data.forEach(function(r) { var bn = String(r.billing_no || ''); if (bn.indexOf(prefix) === 0) { var n = Number(bn.substring(prefix.length)); if (!isNaN(n) && n > maxNum) maxNum = n; } }); var next = maxNum + 1; return jsonOk({ billing_no: prefix + String(next).padStart(6, '0'), next: next, year: year }); } function getBillingNotes(req) { var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var data = sheetToObjects(sheet); if (req.year) { var yy = _billingFormatNoYY(req.year); data = data.filter(function(r) { return String(r.billing_no || '').indexOf('SB001' + yy + '-') === 0; }); } if (req.status) data = data.filter(function(r) { return r.status === req.status; }); if (req.cust_code) data = data.filter(function(r) { return r.cust_code === req.cust_code; }); if (req.billing_uuid) data = data.filter(function(r) { return r.billing_uuid === req.billing_uuid; }); data.forEach(function(r) { try { r.bill_uuids = r.bill_uuids ? JSON.parse(r.bill_uuids) : []; } catch (e) { r.bill_uuids = []; } try { r.bills_snapshot = r.bills_snapshot ? JSON.parse(r.bills_snapshot) : []; } catch (e) { r.bills_snapshot = []; } r.billing_date = _billingNormalizeIso(r.billing_date); r.due_date = _billingNormalizeIso(r.due_date); if (r.last_payment_date) r.last_payment_date = _billingNormalizeIso(r.last_payment_date); r.total_amount = Number(r.total_amount) || 0; r.paid_amount = Number(r.paid_amount) || 0; r.outstanding = Math.max(0, r.total_amount - r.paid_amount); }); return jsonOk(data); } function createBillingNote(req) { var billingNo = req.billing_no; if (!billingNo) { var nextRes = getNextBillingNo({ year: req.billing_date ? Number(_billingNormalizeIso(req.billing_date).split('-')[0]) : null }); var nextData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContent()); billingNo = nextData.data.billing_no; } if (!req.cust_name) return jsonError('กรุณาระบุชื่อลูกค้า'); if (!req.bill_uuids || !Array.isArray(req.bill_uuids) || req.bill_uuids.length === 0) { return jsonError('กรุณาเลือกบิลอย่างน้อย 1 ใบ'); } var billingDate = _billingNormalizeIso(req.billing_date) || _billingTodayIso(); var dueDate = _billingNormalizeIso(req.due_date) || _billingDefaultDueDate(billingDate); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var allBills = []; ss.getSheets().forEach(function(sh) { if (/^bills_\d{4}$/.test(sh.getName())) allBills = allBills.concat(sheetToObjects(sh)); }); var snap = []; var total = 0; req.bill_uuids.forEach(function(uuid) { var b = allBills.find(function(x) { return x.bill_uuid === uuid; }); if (b) { var amt = Number(b.amount) || 0; snap.push({ bill_uuid: b.bill_uuid, bill_no: b.bill_no || '', date: _billingNormalizeIso(b.date), amount: amt, type: b.type || '', station: b.station || '', wht_amount: Number(b.wht_amount) || 0, }); total += amt; } }); if (snap.length === 0) return jsonError('ไม่พบบิลที่ระบุ'); var now = _billingNowIso(); var record = { billing_uuid: _billingUuid(), billing_no: billingNo, billing_date: billingDate, due_date: dueDate, cust_code: req.cust_code || '', cust_name: req.cust_name, cust_address: req.cust_address || '', cust_tax_id: req.cust_tax_id || '', cust_phone: req.cust_phone || '', bill_uuids: JSON.stringify(req.bill_uuids), bills_snapshot: JSON.stringify(snap), total_amount: total, paid_amount: 0, status: 'pending', last_payment_date: '', remarks: req.remarks || '', created_at: now, created_by: req.created_by || 'system', updated_at: now, }; appendRow(BILLING_NOTES_SHEET, record, BILLING_NOTES_HEADERS); var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); ['billing_date','due_date','last_payment_date'].forEach(function(col) { var idx = BILLING_NOTES_HEADERS.indexOf(col); if (idx >= 0) sheet.getRange(lastRow, idx + 1).setNumberFormat('@'); }); return jsonOk(record); } function updateBillingNote(req) { if (!req.billing_uuid) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ billing_uuid'); var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var uuidCol = headers.indexOf('billing_uuid'); var rowIdx = -1; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; }); if (b) { var amt = Number(b.amount) || 0; snap.push({ bill_uuid: b.bill_uuid, bill_no: b.bill_no || '', date: _billingNormalizeIso(b.date), amount: amt, type: b.type || '', station: b.station || '', wht_amount: Number(b.wht_amount) || 0, }); total += amt; } }); var biUuidCol = headers.indexOf('bill_uuids'); var snapCol = headers.indexOf('bills_snapshot'); var totCol = headers.indexOf('total_amount'); if (biUuidCol >= 0) sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, biUuidCol + 1).setValue(JSON.stringify(req.bill_uuids)); if (paid > 0) return jsonError('ใบวางบิลนี้มีการรับชำระแล้ว ไม่สามารถลบได้ — ใช้ "ยกเลิก" แทน'); sheet.deleteRow(r + 1); return jsonOk({ billing_uuid: req.billing_uuid, deleted: true }); } } return jsonError('ไม่พบใบวางบิล'); } function cancelBillingNote(req) { if (!req.billing_uuid) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ billing_uuid'); var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var headers = data[0]; r++) { if (String(data[r][uuidCol]) === String(req.billing_uuid)) { sheet.getRange(r + 1, statusCol + 1).setValue('cancelled'); if (uaCol >= 0) sheet.getRange(r + 1, uaCol + 1).setValue(_billingNowIso()); } } return jsonError('ไม่พบใบวางบิล'); } function recordBillingPayment(req) { if (!req.billing_uuid) return jsonError('ไม่ได้ระบุ billing_uuid'); if (!req.amount || Number(req.amount) <= 0) return jsonError('กรุณาระบุยอดที่ชำระ'); var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var headers = data[0]; var uuidCol = headers.indexOf('billing_uuid'); var totCol = headers.indexOf('total_amount'); var paidCol = headers.indexOf('paid_amount'); var statusCol = headers.indexOf('status'); var lpdCol = headers.indexOf('last_payment_date'); var uaCol = headers.indexOf('updated_at'); var biUuidCol = headers.indexOf('bill_uuids'); var noCol = headers.indexOf('billing_no'); var ccCol = headers.indexOf('cust_code'); var cnCol = headers.indexOf('cust_name'); var rowIdx = -1; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (String(data[r][uuidCol]) === String(req.billing_uuid)) { rowIdx = r; break; } } if (rowIdx === -1) return jsonError('ไม่พบใบวางบิล'); var currentStatus = data[rowIdx][statusCol]; if (currentStatus === 'cancelled') return jsonError('ใบวางบิลถูกยกเลิกแล้ว'); if (currentStatus === 'paid') return jsonError('ใบวางบิลชำระครบแล้ว'); var total = Number(data[rowIdx][totCol]) || 0; var paid = Number(data[rowIdx][paidCol]) || 0; var amt = Number(req.amount); var wht = Number(req.wht_amount) || 0; var fee = Number(req.fee_amount) || 0; var newPaid = paid + amt + wht + fee; var newStatus; if (newPaid >= total - 0.01) newStatus = 'paid'; else if (newPaid > 0) newStatus = 'partial'; else newStatus = 'pending'; var payDate = _billingNormalizeIso(req.payment_date) || _billingTodayIso(); var now = _billingNowIso(); sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, paidCol + 1).setValue(newPaid); sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, statusCol + 1).setValue(newStatus); if (lpdCol >= 0) { sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, lpdCol + 1).setNumberFormat('@'); sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, lpdCol + 1).setValue(payDate); } if (uaCol >= 0) sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 1, uaCol + 1).setValue(now); if (newStatus === 'paid' && biUuidCol >= 0) { var billUuids = []; try { billUuids = JSON.parse(data[rowIdx][biUuidCol] || '[]'); } catch (e) { billUuids = []; } if (billUuids.length > 0) _billingMarkBillsPaid(billUuids, data[rowIdx][noCol], now); } var paymentRecord = { payment_uuid: _billingUuid(), billing_uuid: req.billing_uuid, billing_no: data[rowIdx][noCol] || '', cust_code: data[rowIdx][ccCol] || '', cust_name: data[rowIdx][cnCol] || '', payment_date: payDate, amount: amt, wht_amount: wht, fee_amount: fee, payment_method: req.payment_method || 'Cash', reference: req.reference || '', remarks: req.remarks || '', action_by: req.action_by || 'system', action_at: now, }; appendRow(BILLING_PAYMENTS_SHEET, paymentRecord, BILLING_PAYMENTS_HEADERS); var paySheet = _getBillingPaymentsSheet(); var payLastRow = paySheet.getLastRow(); var pdIdx = BILLING_PAYMENTS_HEADERS.indexOf('payment_date'); if (pdIdx >= 0) paySheet.getRange(payLastRow, pdIdx + 1).setNumberFormat('@'); return jsonOk({ billing_uuid: req.billing_uuid, new_paid: newPaid, new_status: newStatus, outstanding: Math.max(0, total - newPaid), payment_uuid: paymentRecord.payment_uuid, }); } function _billingMarkBillsPaid(billUuids, billingNo, nowIso) { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); ss.getSheets().forEach(function(sh) { if (!/^bills_\d{4}$/.test(sh.getName())) return; var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return; var headers = data[0]; var uuidCol = headers.indexOf('bill_uuid'); var msCol = headers.indexOf('match_status'); var refCol = headers.indexOf('matched_stmt_ref'); if (refCol < 0) refCol = headers.indexOf('match_ref'); var paidAtCol = headers.indexOf('matched_at'); if (paidAtCol < 0) paidAtCol = headers.indexOf('paid_at'); if (uuidCol < 0 || msCol < 0) return; for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (billUuids.indexOf(data[r][uuidCol]) >= 0) { sh.getRange(r + 1, msCol + 1).setValue('paid'); if (refCol >= 0) sh.getRange(r + 1, refCol + 1).setValue(billingNo || ''); if (paidAtCol >= 0) sh.getRange(r + 1, paidAtCol + 1).setValue(nowIso); } } }); } function getBillingPayments(req) { var sheet = _getBillingPaymentsSheet(); var data = sheetToObjects(sheet); if (req.billing_uuid) data = data.filter(function(r) { return r.billing_uuid === req.billing_uuid; }); if (req.cust_code) data = data.filter(function(r) { return r.cust_code === req.cust_code; }); if (req.from_date && req.to_date) { var f = _billingNormalizeIso(req.from_date); var t = _billingNormalizeIso(req.to_date); data = data.filter(function(r) { var d = _billingNormalizeIso(r.payment_date); return d >= f && d <= t; }); } data.forEach(function(r) { r.payment_date = _billingNormalizeIso(r.payment_date); r.amount = Number(r.amount) || 0; r.wht_amount = Number(r.wht_amount) || 0; r.fee_amount = Number(r.fee_amount) || 0; }); data.sort(function(a, b) { return (b.payment_date || '').localeCompare(a.payment_date || ''); }); return jsonOk(data); } function getBillingDebtAging(req) { var asOf = _billingNormalizeIso(req.as_of_date) || _billingTodayIso(); var asOfDate = new Date(asOf + 'T00:00:00'); var sheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var notes = sheetToObjects(sheet); notes = notes.filter(function(n) { return n.status === 'pending' || n.status === 'partial'; }); var byCust = {}; notes.forEach(function(n) { var key = n.cust_code || n.cust_name || 'UNKNOWN'; var due = _billingNormalizeIso(n.due_date); var dueDate = due ? new Date(due + 'T00:00:00') : asOfDate; var daysOverdue = Math.floor((asOfDate - dueDate) / 86400000); var outstanding = (Number(n.total_amount) || 0) - (Number(n.paid_amount) || 0); if (!byCust[key]) { byCust[key] = { cust_code: n.cust_code || '', cust_name: n.cust_name || '', cust_phone: n.cust_phone || '', notes: [], total: 0, b_current: 0, b_0_30: 0, b_31_60: 0, b_61_90: 0, b_90_plus: 0, oldest_days: 0, }; } var c = byCust[key]; c.total += outstanding; c.notes.push({ billing_uuid: n.billing_uuid, billing_no: n.billing_no, billing_date: _billingNormalizeIso(n.billing_date), due_date: due, total_amount: Number(n.total_amount) || 0, paid_amount: Number(n.paid_amount) || 0, outstanding: outstanding, status: n.status, days_overdue: daysOverdue, }); if (daysOverdue < 0) c.b_current += outstanding; else if (daysOverdue <= 30) c.b_0_30 += outstanding; else if (daysOverdue <= 60) c.b_31_60 += outstanding; else if (daysOverdue <= 90) c.b_61_90 += outstanding; else c.b_90_plus += outstanding; if (daysOverdue > c.oldest_days) c.oldest_days = daysOverdue; }); var result = Object.keys(byCust).map(function(k) { return byCust[k]; }); result.sort(function(a, b) { if (b.b_90_plus !== a.b_90_plus) return b.b_90_plus - a.b_90_plus; return b.total - a.total; }); var grand = { current: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.b_current; }, 0), b_0_30: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.b_0_30; }, 0), b_31_60: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.b_31_60; }, 0), b_61_90: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.b_61_90; }, 0), b_90_plus: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.b_90_plus; }, 0), total: result.reduce(function(s, c) { return s + c.total; }, 0), customers: result.length, }; return jsonOk({ as_of: asOf, customers: result, grand: grand }); } function getBillingCustomerStmt(req) { if (!req.cust_code && !req.cust_name) return jsonError('กรุณาระบุ cust_code หรือ cust_name'); var notesSheet = _getBillingNotesSheet(); var notes = sheetToObjects(notesSheet); if (req.cust_code) notes = notes.filter(function(n) { return n.cust_code === req.cust_code; }); else notes = notes.filter(function(n) { return n.cust_name === req.cust_name; }); var paySheet = _getBillingPaymentsSheet(); var payments = sheetToObjects(paySheet); if (req.cust_code) payments = payments.filter(function(p) { return p.cust_code === req.cust_code; }); else payments = payments.filter(function(p) { return p.cust_name === req.cust_name; }); notes.forEach(function(n) { try { n.bills_snapshot = n.bills_snapshot ? JSON.parse(n.bills_snapshot) : []; } catch (e) { n.bills_snapshot = []; } n.billing_date = _billingNormalizeIso(n.billing_date); n.due_date = _billingNormalizeIso(n.due_date); n.total_amount = Number(n.total_amount) || 0; n.paid_amount = Number(n.paid_amount) || 0; n.outstanding = Math.max(0, n.total_amount - n.paid_amount); }); payments.forEach(function(p) { p.payment_date = _billingNormalizeIso(p.payment_date); p.amount = Number(p.amount) || 0; }); var totalBilled = notes.filter(function(n) { return n.status !== 'cancelled'; }).reduce(function(s, n) { return s + n.total_amount; }, 0); var totalPaid = notes.filter(function(n) { return n.status !== 'cancelled'; }).reduce(function(s, n) { return s + n.paid_amount; }, 0); return jsonOk({ cust_code: req.cust_code || '', cust_name: req.cust_name || (notes[0] ? notes[0].cust_name : ''), notes: notes, payments: payments, summary: { total_billed: totalBilled, total_paid: totalPaid, outstanding: Math.max(0, totalBilled - totalPaid), note_count: notes.length, pending_count: notes.filter(function(n) { return n.status === 'pending' || n.status === 'partial'; }).length, }, }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SECTION 10 — Test functions (run in Apps Script editor) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
